--- SOURCE: mojo_samples/etl_benchmark.mojo ---
# etl_benchmark.mojo
#
# ETL benchmark harness for the START project's Mojo ETL samples.
#
# Original work, written for this project. Standard library only, plus
# chunked processing with per-chunk aggregates merged afterwards.
# (Mojo 1.2.0's plain `mojo` package no longer ships `parallelize`;
# it moved to the MAX package, so chunks run sequentially here.)
# Requires Mojo 1.2.0.
#
# What it measures, on real files on disk (never in-memory toys):
#   1. Worker scaling: the log parser and the tick parser, single-threaded
#      vs chunked with 1, 2 and 4 workers, on the same files. NOTE: in this
#      build the chunked parse runs sequentially (see the NOTE above), so
#      the worker count does not change wall time -- the runs still verify
#      that chunked results equal the single-threaded reference at every
#      worker count, and the scaling section becomes meaningful again once
#      MAX `parallelize` is available.
#   2. Chunk-size sensitivity: the same chunked parse with 500, 2000 and
#      8000 lines per chunk, showing the trade-off between per-chunk
#      overhead and load balance.
#   3. Read vs parse split: how much of the wall time is file I/O
#      (`read_text`) versus actual parsing, so the numbers are honest
#      about where the time goes.
#   4. Dataset scaling: small (5k logs / 10k ticks) and medium (100k logs
#      / 250k ticks) inputs.
#
# Method: one warmup run (to fill the compile cache and page cache),
# then three timed repetitions; the reported time is the median. Every
# timed run is checked for correctness first: the chunked aggregate
# must equal the single-threaded reference aggregate, or the run is
# reported as FAILED and the program exits non-zero. A benchmark that
# does not verify its answers is just a stopwatch.
#
# Results are printed as tables and written to two files in the data
# directory: benchmark_report.txt (human-readable) and
# benchmark_report.csv (one row per measurement, for later analysis).
#
# Usage:
#   mojo etl_benchmark.mojo [data-dir]
# Defaults: /tmp/etl_data. Missing input files are generated in place
# (small + medium scales) with the same deterministic generator family
# as etl_gen_data.mojo.

from std.pathlib import Path
from std.testing import assert_equal, assert_true, assert_almost_equal
from std.collections import Dict
from std.random import seed, random_float64
from std.sys import argv
from std.time import perf_counter
from std.os import mkdir


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Shared helpers.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def approx_eq(a: Float64, b: Float64, tol: Float64) -> Bool:
    var diff = a - b
    if diff < 0.0:
        diff = -diff
    var scale: Float64
    if a < 0.0:
        scale = -a
    else:
        scale = a
    if b < 0.0 and -b > scale:
        scale = -b
    elif b > scale:
        scale = b
    if scale < 1.0:
        scale = 1.0
    return diff <= tol * scale


def fmt2(x: Float64) -> String:
    var neg = False
    var v = x
    if v < 0.0:
        neg = True
        v = -v
    var scaled = Int(v * 100.0 + 0.5)
    var whole = scaled / 100
    var frac = scaled - whole * 100
    var frac_s = String(frac)
    if frac < 10:
        frac_s = "0" + frac_s
    var out = String(whole) + "." + frac_s
    if neg:
        return "-" + out
    return out


def fmt_int(n: Int) -> String:
    """Thousands-separated integer, e.g. 250000 -> '250,000'."""
    var s = String(n)
    var out = String("")
    var count = 0
    var i = s.byte_length() - 1
    while i >= 0:
        out = String(s[byte = i : i + 1]) + out
        count += 1
        if count == 3 and i > 0:
            out = "," + out
            count = 0
        i -= 1
    return out


def median3(a: Float64, b: Float64, c: Float64) -> Float64:
    if a <= b and b <= c:
        return b
    if c <= b and b <= a:
        return b
    if b <= a and a <= c:
        return a
    if c <= a and a <= b:
        return a
    return c


def to_owned_lines(text: String) raises -> List[String]:
    var slices = text.split("\n")
    var lines = List[String]()
    for i in range(len(slices)):
        lines.append(String(slices[i]))
    return lines^


def read_int_at(s: StringSlice, start: Int) -> Tuple[Int, Int, Bool]:
    var b = s.as_bytes()
    var i = start
    while i < len(b) and (b[i] == 32 or b[i] == 9):
        i += 1
    var neg = False
    if i < len(b) and b[i] == 45:
        neg = True
        i += 1
    var v = 0
    var any_digit = False
    while i < len(b) and b[i] >= 48 and b[i] <= 57:
        v = v * 10 + Int(b[i]) - 48
        any_digit = True
        i += 1
    if neg:
        v = -v
    return (v, i, any_digit)


def parse_int_slice(s: StringSlice) -> Tuple[Int, Bool]:
    var r = read_int_at(s, 0)
    return (r[0], r[2])


def parse_float_slice(s: StringSlice) -> Tuple[Float64, Bool]:
    var b = s.as_bytes()
    var i = 0
    while i < len(b) and (b[i] == 32 or b[i] == 9):
        i += 1
    var neg = False
    if i < len(b) and b[i] == 45:
        neg = True
        i += 1
    var v = 0.0
    var any_digit = False
    while i < len(b) and b[i] >= 48 and b[i] <= 57:
        v = v * 10.0 + Float64(Int(b[i]) - 48)
        any_digit = True
        i += 1
    if i < len(b) and b[i] == 46:
        i += 1
        var frac = 0.1
        while i < len(b) and b[i] >= 48 and b[i] <= 57:
            v += Float64(Int(b[i]) - 48) * frac
            frac *= 0.1
            any_digit = True
            i += 1
    if neg:
        v = -v
    return (v, any_digit)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Log parser (compact copy of the etl_log_parse.mojo core: same format,
# same aggregates, same map-reduce shape; the report printing is omitted
# here because the harness only needs timings and equality checks).
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


struct LogFields(Copyable, ImplicitlyCopyable, Movable):
    var ok: Bool
    var method: String
    var url: String
    var status: Int
    var size: Int
    var hour: Int

    def __init__(out self):
        self.ok = False
        self.method = ""
        self.url = ""
        self.status = 0
        self.size = 0
        self.hour = -1

    def __init__(out self, *, other: Self):
        self.ok = other.ok
        self.method = other.method
        self.url = other.url
        self.status = other.status
        self.size = other.size
        self.hour = other.hour


def parse_log_line(line: StringSlice) -> LogFields:
    var out = LogFields()
    if line.byte_length() < 20:
        return out
    var open_br = line.find("[")
    if open_br < 0:
        return out
    var ts = line[byte = open_br + 1 : open_br + 21]
    if ts.byte_length() < 20:
        return out
    var tb = ts.as_bytes()
    if tb[11] != 58 or tb[14] != 58 or tb[17] != 58:
        return out
    var hour = (Int(tb[12]) - 48) * 10 + (Int(tb[13]) - 48)
    if hour < 0 or hour > 23:
        return out
    var q1 = line.find('"')
    if q1 < 0:
        return out
    var rest_after_q1 = String(line[byte = q1 + 1 : line.byte_length()])
    var q2rel = rest_after_q1.find('"')
    if q2rel < 0:
        return out
    var request = line[byte = q1 + 1 : q1 + 1 + q2rel]
    var req_parts = request.split(" ")
    if len(req_parts) != 3:
        return out
    var method = String(req_parts[0])
    var url = String(req_parts[1])
    if method.byte_length() == 0 or url.byte_length() == 0:
        return out
    var tail = line[byte = q1 + 1 + q2rel + 1 : line.byte_length()]
    var status_read = read_int_at(tail, 0)
    if not status_read[2]:
        return out
    var status = status_read[0]
    var size_read = read_int_at(tail, status_read[1])
    if not size_read[2]:
        # A lone '-' means "no size recorded".
        var b = tail.as_bytes()
        var j = status_read[1]
        while j < len(b) and b[j] == 32:
            j += 1
        if j < len(b) and b[j] == 45:
            size_read = (0, j + 1, True)
        else:
            return out
    var size = size_read[0]
    if status < 100 or status > 599 or size < 0:
        return out
    out.ok = True
    out.method = method
    out.url = url
    out.status = status
    out.size = size
    out.hour = hour
    return out


struct LogAgg(Movable):
    var total: Int
    var parsed: Int
    var malformed: Int
    var bytes_total: Int
    var errors: Int
    var status_counts: Dict[Int, Int]
    var url_counts: Dict[String, Int]
    var method_counts: Dict[String, Int]

    def __init__(out self):
        self.total = 0
        self.parsed = 0
        self.malformed = 0
        self.bytes_total = 0
        self.errors = 0
        self.status_counts = Dict[Int, Int]()
        self.url_counts = Dict[String, Int]()
        self.method_counts = Dict[String, Int]()

    def add_line(mut self, f: LogFields):
        self.total += 1
        if not f.ok:
            self.malformed += 1
            return
        self.parsed += 1
        self.bytes_total += f.size
        if f.status >= 400:
            self.errors += 1
        self.status_counts[f.status] = self.status_counts.get(f.status, 0) + 1
        self.url_counts[f.url] = self.url_counts.get(f.url, 0) + 1
        self.method_counts[f.method] = self.method_counts.get(f.method, 0) + 1

    def merge(mut self, ref other: LogAgg):
        self.total += other.total
        self.parsed += other.parsed
        self.malformed += other.malformed
        self.bytes_total += other.bytes_total
        self.errors += other.errors
        for code in other.status_counts.keys():
            self.status_counts[code] = self.status_counts.get(code, 0) + other.status_counts.get(code, 0)
        for url in other.url_counts.keys():
            self.url_counts[url] = self.url_counts.get(url, 0) + other.url_counts.get(url, 0)
        for m in other.method_counts.keys():
            self.method_counts[m] = self.method_counts.get(m, 0) + other.method_counts.get(m, 0)


def log_agg_equal(ref a: LogAgg, ref b: LogAgg) -> Bool:
    if a.total != b.total or a.parsed != b.parsed or a.malformed != b.malformed:
        return False
    if a.bytes_total != b.bytes_total or a.errors != b.errors:
        return False
    if len(a.status_counts) != len(b.status_counts):
        return False
    for code in a.status_counts.keys():
        if code not in b.status_counts or b.status_counts.get(code, 0) != a.status_counts.get(code, 0):
            return False
    if len(a.url_counts) != len(b.url_counts):
        return False
    for url in a.url_counts.keys():
        if url not in b.url_counts or b.url_counts.get(url, 0) != a.url_counts.get(url, 0):
            return False
    if len(a.method_counts) != len(b.method_counts):
        return False
    for m in a.method_counts.keys():
        if m not in b.method_counts or b.method_counts.get(m, 0) != a.method_counts.get(m, 0):
            return False
    return True


def parse_log_range(lines: List[String], start: Int, end: Int) -> LogAgg:
    var agg = LogAgg()
    for i in range(start, end):
        if lines[i].byte_length() == 0:
            continue
        agg.add_line(parse_log_line(lines[i]))
    return agg^


def parse_log_reference(lines: List[String]) -> LogAgg:
    return parse_log_range(lines, 0, len(lines))


def parse_log_parallel(lines: List[String], workers: Int, chunk_size: Int) -> LogAgg:
    var n = len(lines)
    var n_chunks = (n + chunk_size - 1) / chunk_size
    if n_chunks < 1:
        n_chunks = 1
    var chunk_aggs = List[LogAgg]()
    for _ in range(n_chunks):
        chunk_aggs.append(LogAgg())

    def work(i: Int) {mut chunk_aggs, imm lines, imm n, imm chunk_size}:
        var start = i * chunk_size
        var end = start + chunk_size
        if end > n:
            end = n
        var agg = parse_log_range(lines, start, end)
        chunk_aggs[i] = agg^

    # NOTE (Mojo 1.2.0): the plain `mojo` package no longer ships
    # `parallelize` -- it moved to the MAX package, which is not a
    # stdlib dependency here. Chunks therefore run sequentially in
    # this build; the chunk/merge structure is unchanged, so restoring
    # multi-core dispatch is a one-line change (call parallelize
    # from MAX instead of this loop) once MAX is available.
    for i in range(n_chunks):
        work(i)
    var result = LogAgg()
    for i in range(n_chunks):
        result.merge(chunk_aggs[i])
    return result^

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Tick parser (compact copy of the etl_tick_parse.mojo core).
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


struct Bar(Copyable, ImplicitlyCopyable, Movable):
    var open: Float64
    var high: Float64
    var low: Float64
    var close: Float64
    var volume: Int
    var trades: Int
    var vwap_num: Float64
    var first_ts: Int
    var last_ts: Int

    def __init__(out self):
        self.open = 0.0
        self.high = 0.0
        self.low = 0.0
        self.close = 0.0
        self.volume = 0
        self.trades = 0
        self.vwap_num = 0.0
        self.first_ts = 0
        self.last_ts = 0

    def __init__(out self, *, other: Self):
        self.open = other.open
        self.high = other.high
        self.low = other.low
        self.close = other.close
        self.volume = other.volume
        self.trades = other.trades
        self.vwap_num = other.vwap_num
        self.first_ts = other.first_ts
        self.last_ts = other.last_ts

    def update(mut self, price: Float64, size: Int, ts: Int):
        if self.trades == 0:
            self.open = price
            self.high = price
            self.low = price
            self.close = price
            self.first_ts = ts
            self.last_ts = ts
        else:
            if price > self.high:
                self.high = price
            if price < self.low:
                self.low = price
            if ts < self.first_ts:
                self.first_ts = ts
                self.open = price
            if ts >= self.last_ts:
                self.last_ts = ts
                self.close = price
        self.volume += size
        self.trades += 1
        self.vwap_num += price * Float64(size)

    def merge_from(mut self, ref other: Bar):
        if other.trades == 0:
            return
        if self.trades == 0:
            self.open = other.open
            self.high = other.high
            self.low = other.low
            self.close = other.close
            self.volume = other.volume
            self.trades = other.trades
            self.vwap_num = other.vwap_num
            self.first_ts = other.first_ts
            self.last_ts = other.last_ts
            return
        if other.high > self.high:
            self.high = other.high
        if other.low < self.low:
            self.low = other.low
        if other.first_ts < self.first_ts:
            self.first_ts = other.first_ts
            self.open = other.open
        if other.last_ts >= self.last_ts:
            self.last_ts = other.last_ts
            self.close = other.close
        self.volume += other.volume
        self.trades += other.trades
        self.vwap_num += other.vwap_num


struct TickFields(Copyable, ImplicitlyCopyable, Movable):
    var ok: Bool
    var ts: Int
    var symbol: String
    var price: Float64
    var size: Int
    var exchange: String

    def __init__(out self):
        self.ok = False
        self.ts = 0
        self.symbol = ""
        self.price = 0.0
        self.size = 0
        self.exchange = ""

    def __init__(out self, *, other: Self):
        self.ok = other.ok
        self.ts = other.ts
        self.symbol = other.symbol
        self.price = other.price
        self.size = other.size
        self.exchange = other.exchange


def parse_tick_line(line: StringSlice) -> TickFields:
    var out = TickFields()
    if line.byte_length() < 8:
        return out
    var parts = line.split(",")
    if len(parts) != 5:
        return out
    var ts_read = parse_int_slice(parts[0])
    if not ts_read[1] or ts_read[0] <= 0:
        return out
    var symbol = String(parts[1])
    if symbol.byte_length() == 0:
        return out
    var price_read = parse_float_slice(parts[2])
    if not price_read[1] or price_read[0] <= 0.0:
        return out
    var size_read = parse_int_slice(parts[3])
    if not size_read[1] or size_read[0] <= 0:
        return out
    var exchange = String(parts[4])
    if exchange.byte_length() == 0:
        return out
    out.ok = True
    out.ts = ts_read[0]
    out.symbol = symbol
    out.price = price_read[0]
    out.size = size_read[0]
    out.exchange = exchange
    return out


struct TickAgg(Movable):
    var total: Int
    var parsed: Int
    var malformed: Int
    var total_volume: Int
    var symbols: Dict[String, Bar]
    var bars: Dict[String, Bar]
    var exchanges: Dict[String, Int]

    def __init__(out self):
        self.total = 0
        self.parsed = 0
        self.malformed = 0
        self.total_volume = 0
        self.symbols = Dict[String, Bar]()
        self.bars = Dict[String, Bar]()
        self.exchanges = Dict[String, Int]()

    def add_tick(mut self, f: TickFields):
        self.total += 1
        if not f.ok:
            self.malformed += 1
            return
        self.parsed += 1
        self.total_volume += f.size
        var sym_bar = self.symbols.get(f.symbol, Bar())
        sym_bar.update(f.price, f.size, f.ts)
        self.symbols[f.symbol] = sym_bar
        var key = f.symbol + "|" + String(f.ts / 300)
        var bar = self.bars.get(key, Bar())
        bar.update(f.price, f.size, f.ts)
        self.bars[key] = bar
        self.exchanges[f.exchange] = self.exchanges.get(f.exchange, 0) + 1

    def merge(mut self, ref other: TickAgg):
        self.total += other.total
        self.parsed += other.parsed
        self.malformed += other.malformed
        self.total_volume += other.total_volume
        for sym in other.symbols.keys():
            var merged = self.symbols.get(sym, Bar())
            var other_bar = other.symbols.get(sym, Bar())
            merged.merge_from(other_bar)
            self.symbols[sym] = merged
        for key in other.bars.keys():
            var merged_bar = self.bars.get(key, Bar())
            var other_bar2 = other.bars.get(key, Bar())
            merged_bar.merge_from(other_bar2)
            self.bars[key] = merged_bar
        for ex in other.exchanges.keys():
            self.exchanges[ex] = self.exchanges.get(ex, 0) + other.exchanges.get(ex, 0)


def bar_equal(a: Bar, b: Bar) -> Bool:
    if a.volume != b.volume or a.trades != b.trades:
        return False
    if a.first_ts != b.first_ts or a.last_ts != b.last_ts:
        return False
    if not approx_eq(a.open, b.open, 1e-9):
        return False
    if not approx_eq(a.high, b.high, 1e-9):
        return False
    if not approx_eq(a.low, b.low, 1e-9):
        return False
    if not approx_eq(a.close, b.close, 1e-9):
        return False
    if not approx_eq(a.vwap_num, b.vwap_num, 1e-9):
        return False
    return True


def tick_agg_equal(ref a: TickAgg, ref b: TickAgg) -> Bool:
    if a.total != b.total or a.parsed != b.parsed or a.malformed != b.malformed:
        return False
    if a.total_volume != b.total_volume:
        return False
    if len(a.symbols) != len(b.symbols):
        return False
    for sym in a.symbols.keys():
        if sym not in b.symbols:
            return False
        if not bar_equal(a.symbols.get(sym, Bar()), b.symbols.get(sym, Bar())):
            return False
    if len(a.bars) != len(b.bars):
        return False
    for key in a.bars.keys():
        if key not in b.bars:
            return False
        if not bar_equal(a.bars.get(key, Bar()), b.bars.get(key, Bar())):
            return False
    if len(a.exchanges) != len(b.exchanges):
        return False
    for ex in a.exchanges.keys():
        if ex not in b.exchanges or b.exchanges.get(ex, 0) != a.exchanges.get(ex, 0):
            return False
    return True


def parse_tick_range(lines: List[String], start: Int, end: Int) -> TickAgg:
    var agg = TickAgg()
    for i in range(start, end):
        if lines[i].byte_length() == 0:
            continue
        if lines[i].startswith("timestamp,"):
            continue
        agg.add_tick(parse_tick_line(lines[i]))
    return agg^


def parse_tick_reference(lines: List[String]) -> TickAgg:
    return parse_tick_range(lines, 0, len(lines))


def parse_tick_parallel(lines: List[String], workers: Int, chunk_size: Int) -> TickAgg:
    var n = len(lines)
    var n_chunks = (n + chunk_size - 1) / chunk_size
    if n_chunks < 1:
        n_chunks = 1
    var chunk_aggs = List[TickAgg]()
    for _ in range(n_chunks):
        chunk_aggs.append(TickAgg())

    def work(i: Int) {mut chunk_aggs, imm lines, imm n, imm chunk_size}:
        var start = i * chunk_size
        var end = start + chunk_size
        if end > n:
            end = n
        var agg = parse_tick_range(lines, start, end)
        chunk_aggs[i] = agg^

    # NOTE (Mojo 1.2.0): the plain `mojo` package no longer ships
    # `parallelize` -- it moved to the MAX package, which is not a
    # stdlib dependency here. Chunks therefore run sequentially in
    # this build; the chunk/merge structure is unchanged, so restoring
    # multi-core dispatch is a one-line change (call parallelize
    # from MAX instead of this loop) once MAX is available.
    for i in range(n_chunks):
        work(i)
    var result = TickAgg()
    for i in range(n_chunks):
        result.merge(chunk_aggs[i])
    return result^

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Benchmark harness.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


struct Measurement(Copyable, ImplicitlyCopyable, Movable):
    var dataset: String
    var parser: String
    var mode: String
    var workers: Int
    var chunk_size: Int
    var lines: Int
    var bytes: Int
    var seconds: Float64
    var lines_per_s: Float64
    var mb_per_s: Float64
    var speedup: Float64
    var efficiency: Float64
    var correct: Bool

    def __init__(out self):
        self.dataset = ""
        self.parser = ""
        self.mode = ""
        self.workers = 0
        self.chunk_size = 0
        self.lines = 0
        self.bytes = 0
        self.seconds = 0.0
        self.lines_per_s = 0.0
        self.mb_per_s = 0.0
        self.speedup = 0.0
        self.efficiency = 0.0
        self.correct = False

    def __init__(out self, *, other: Self):
        self.dataset = other.dataset
        self.parser = other.parser
        self.mode = other.mode
        self.workers = other.workers
        self.chunk_size = other.chunk_size
        self.lines = other.lines
        self.bytes = other.bytes
        self.seconds = other.seconds
        self.lines_per_s = other.lines_per_s
        self.mb_per_s = other.mb_per_s
        self.speedup = other.speedup
        self.efficiency = other.efficiency
        self.correct = other.correct


struct Harness(Movable):
    var results: List[Measurement]
    var failures: Int

    def __init__(out self):
        self.results = List[Measurement]()
        self.failures = 0

    def record(mut self, m: Measurement):
        self.results.append(m)
        if not m.correct:
            self.failures += 1

    def write_reports(self, data_dir: String) raises:
        var txt_lines = List[String]()
        txt_lines.append("ETL BENCHMARK REPORT")
        txt_lines.append("===================")
        txt_lines.append("Generated by etl_benchmark.mojo (Mojo 1.2.0, sequential chunks (parallelize moved to MAX)).")
        txt_lines.append("Each time is the median of 3 runs after 1 warmup run.")
        txt_lines.append("Speedup is relative to the single-threaded reference on the same file.")
        txt_lines.append("Efficiency is speedup divided by worker count.")
        txt_lines.append("")
        txt_lines.append(
            "dataset | parser | mode | workers | chunk | lines | seconds | lines/s | MB/s | speedup | eff | correct"
        )
        var csv_lines = List[String]()
        csv_lines.append("dataset,parser,mode,workers,chunk_size,lines,bytes,seconds,lines_per_s,mb_per_s,speedup,efficiency,correct")
        for m in self.results:
            var correct_s = "yes"
            if not m.correct:
                correct_s = "NO"
            txt_lines.append(
                m.dataset
                + " | "
                + m.parser
                + " | "
                + m.mode
                + " | "
                + String(m.workers)
                + " | "
                + String(m.chunk_size)
                + " | "
                + fmt_int(m.lines)
                + " | "
                + fmt2(m.seconds)
                + " | "
                + fmt_int(Int(m.lines_per_s))
                + " | "
                + fmt2(m.mb_per_s)
                + " | "
                + fmt2(m.speedup)
                + " | "
                + fmt2(m.efficiency)
                + " | "
                + correct_s
            )
            csv_lines.append(
                m.dataset
                + ","
                + m.parser
                + ","
                + m.mode
                + ","
                + String(m.workers)
                + ","
                + String(m.chunk_size)
                + ","
                + String(m.lines)
                + ","
                + String(m.bytes)
                + ","
                + fmt2(m.seconds)
                + ","
                + String(Int(m.lines_per_s))
                + ","
                + fmt2(m.mb_per_s)
                + ","
                + fmt2(m.speedup)
                + ","
                + fmt2(m.efficiency)
                + ","
                + correct_s
            )
        txt_lines.append("")
        txt_lines.append("failures (incorrect parallel results): " + String(self.failures))
        Path(data_dir + "/benchmark_report.txt").write_text(String("\n").join(txt_lines) + "\n")
        Path(data_dir + "/benchmark_report.csv").write_text(String("\n").join(csv_lines) + "\n")


def time_read(path: Path) raises -> Tuple[Float64, Int]:
    """Median read_text time over 3 runs (plus one warmup), and the size."""
    _ = path.read_text()
    var t1 = perf_counter()
    var text = path.read_text()
    var e1 = perf_counter() - t1
    var size = text.byte_length()
    var t2 = perf_counter()
    _ = path.read_text()
    var e2 = perf_counter() - t2
    var t3 = perf_counter()
    _ = path.read_text()
    var e3 = perf_counter() - t3
    return (median3(e1, e2, e3), size)


def bench_logs(mut h: Harness, dataset: String, lines: List[String], n_bytes: Int, ref_seconds: Float64):
    """Worker scaling for the log parser on one dataset. `ref_seconds`
    is the already-measured single-threaded time, used for speedups."""
    print("  [logs/" + dataset + "] workers=1..4, chunk=2000")
    var ref_agg = parse_log_reference(lines)
    for workers in [1, 2, 4]:
        var warm = parse_log_parallel(lines, workers, 2000)
        _ = log_agg_equal(ref_agg, warm)
        var t1 = perf_counter()
        var a1 = parse_log_parallel(lines, workers, 2000)
        var e1 = perf_counter() - t1
        var t2 = perf_counter()
        var a2 = parse_log_parallel(lines, workers, 2000)
        var e2 = perf_counter() - t2
        var t3 = perf_counter()
        var a3 = parse_log_parallel(lines, workers, 2000)
        var e3 = perf_counter() - t3
        var med = median3(e1, e2, e3)
        var ok = log_agg_equal(ref_agg, a1) and log_agg_equal(ref_agg, a2) and log_agg_equal(ref_agg, a3)
        var m = Measurement()
        m.dataset = dataset
        m.parser = "logs"
        m.mode = "parallel"
        m.workers = workers
        m.chunk_size = 2000
        m.lines = ref_agg.total
        m.bytes = n_bytes
        m.seconds = med
        m.lines_per_s = Float64(m.lines) / med
        m.mb_per_s = Float64(n_bytes) / med / 1000000.0
        if med > 0.0 and ref_seconds > 0.0:
            m.speedup = ref_seconds / med
        if workers > 0:
            m.efficiency = m.speedup / Float64(workers)
        m.correct = ok
        h.record(m)
        print(
            "    workers=",
            workers,
            " median=",
            fmt2(med),
            "s lines/s=",
            fmt_int(Int(m.lines_per_s)),
            " speedup=",
            fmt2(m.speedup),
            " correct=",
            ok,
        )


def bench_ticks(mut h: Harness, dataset: String, lines: List[String], n_bytes: Int, ref_seconds: Float64):
    print("  [ticks/" + dataset + "] workers=1..4, chunk=2000")
    var ref_agg = parse_tick_reference(lines)
    for workers in [1, 2, 4]:
        var warm = parse_tick_parallel(lines, workers, 2000)
        _ = tick_agg_equal(ref_agg, warm)
        var t1 = perf_counter()
        var a1 = parse_tick_parallel(lines, workers, 2000)
        var e1 = perf_counter() - t1
        var t2 = perf_counter()
        var a2 = parse_tick_parallel(lines, workers, 2000)
        var e2 = perf_counter() - t2
        var t3 = perf_counter()
        var a3 = parse_tick_parallel(lines, workers, 2000)
        var e3 = perf_counter() - t3
        var med = median3(e1, e2, e3)
        var ok = tick_agg_equal(ref_agg, a1) and tick_agg_equal(ref_agg, a2) and tick_agg_equal(ref_agg, a3)
        var m = Measurement()
        m.dataset = dataset
        m.parser = "ticks"
        m.mode = "parallel"
        m.workers = workers
        m.chunk_size = 2000
        m.lines = ref_agg.total
        m.bytes = n_bytes
        m.seconds = med
        m.lines_per_s = Float64(m.lines) / med
        m.mb_per_s = Float64(n_bytes) / med / 1000000.0
        if med > 0.0 and ref_seconds > 0.0:
            m.speedup = ref_seconds / med
        if workers > 0:
            m.efficiency = m.speedup / Float64(workers)
        m.correct = ok
        h.record(m)
        print(
            "    workers=",
            workers,
            " median=",
            fmt2(med),
            "s lines/s=",
            fmt_int(Int(m.lines_per_s)),
            " speedup=",
            fmt2(m.speedup),
            " correct=",
            ok,
        )


def bench_reference(mut h: Harness, dataset: String, parser: String, lines: List[String], n_bytes: Int) -> Float64:
    """Time the single-threaded reference (median of 3 after warmup) and
    record it as the baseline measurement."""
    print("  [" + parser + "/" + dataset + "] reference (single-threaded)")
    var e1: Float64
    var e2: Float64
    var e3: Float64
    var total: Int
    if parser == "logs":
        var warm = parse_log_reference(lines)
        total = warm.total
        var t1 = perf_counter()
        _ = parse_log_reference(lines)
        e1 = perf_counter() - t1
        var t2 = perf_counter()
        _ = parse_log_reference(lines)
        e2 = perf_counter() - t2
        var t3 = perf_counter()
        _ = parse_log_reference(lines)
        e3 = perf_counter() - t3
    else:
        var warm = parse_tick_reference(lines)
        total = warm.total
        var t1 = perf_counter()
        _ = parse_tick_reference(lines)
        e1 = perf_counter() - t1
        var t2 = perf_counter()
        _ = parse_tick_reference(lines)
        e2 = perf_counter() - t2
        var t3 = perf_counter()
        _ = parse_tick_reference(lines)
        e3 = perf_counter() - t3
    var med = median3(e1, e2, e3)
    var m = Measurement()
    m.dataset = dataset
    m.parser = parser
    m.mode = "reference"
    m.workers = 1
    m.chunk_size = 0
    m.lines = total
    m.bytes = n_bytes
    m.seconds = med
    m.lines_per_s = Float64(total) / med
    m.mb_per_s = Float64(n_bytes) / med / 1000000.0
    m.speedup = 1.0
    m.efficiency = 1.0
    m.correct = True
    h.record(m)
    print("    median=", fmt2(med), "s lines/s=", fmt_int(Int(m.lines_per_s)))
    return med


def bench_chunk_sizes(mut h: Harness, dataset: String, parser: String, lines: List[String], n_bytes: Int, ref_seconds: Float64):
    """Chunk-size sensitivity at 4 workers: 500 / 2000 / 8000 lines."""
    print("  [" + parser + "/" + dataset + "] chunk-size study, 4 workers")
    for chunk in [500, 2000, 8000]:
        var med: Float64
        var ok: Bool
        var total: Int
        if parser == "logs":
            var ref_agg = parse_log_reference(lines)
            total = ref_agg.total
            var w = parse_log_parallel(lines, 4, chunk)
            _ = log_agg_equal(ref_agg, w)
            var t1 = perf_counter()
            var a1 = parse_log_parallel(lines, 4, chunk)
            var e1 = perf_counter() - t1
            var t2 = perf_counter()
            var a2 = parse_log_parallel(lines, 4, chunk)
            var e2 = perf_counter() - t2
            var t3 = perf_counter()
            var a3 = parse_log_parallel(lines, 4, chunk)
            var e3 = perf_counter() - t3
            med = median3(e1, e2, e3)
            ok = log_agg_equal(ref_agg, a1) and log_agg_equal(ref_agg, a2) and log_agg_equal(ref_agg, a3)
        else:
            var ref_agg = parse_tick_reference(lines)
            total = ref_agg.total
            var w = parse_tick_parallel(lines, 4, chunk)
            _ = tick_agg_equal(ref_agg, w)
            var t1 = perf_counter()
            var a1 = parse_tick_parallel(lines, 4, chunk)
            var e1 = perf_counter() - t1
            var t2 = perf_counter()
            var a2 = parse_tick_parallel(lines, 4, chunk)
            var e2 = perf_counter() - t2
            var t3 = perf_counter()
            var a3 = parse_tick_parallel(lines, 4, chunk)
            var e3 = perf_counter() - t3
            med = median3(e1, e2, e3)
            ok = tick_agg_equal(ref_agg, a1) and tick_agg_equal(ref_agg, a2) and tick_agg_equal(ref_agg, a3)
        var m = Measurement()
        m.dataset = dataset
        m.parser = parser
        m.mode = "chunk-study"
        m.workers = 4
        m.chunk_size = chunk
        m.lines = total
        m.bytes = n_bytes
        m.seconds = med
        m.lines_per_s = Float64(total) / med
        m.mb_per_s = Float64(n_bytes) / med / 1000000.0
        if med > 0.0 and ref_seconds > 0.0:
            m.speedup = ref_seconds / med
        m.efficiency = m.speedup / 4.0
        m.correct = ok
        h.record(m)
        print(
            "    chunk=",
            chunk,
            " median=",
            fmt2(med),
            "s lines/s=",
            fmt_int(Int(m.lines_per_s)),
            " speedup=",
            fmt2(m.speedup),
            " correct=",
            ok,
        )

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Unit tests for the harness itself (timing helpers and formatters).
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def test_median3() raises:
    assert_almost_equal(median3(1.0, 2.0, 3.0), 2.0, atol=1e-9, rtol=1e-9)
    assert_almost_equal(median3(3.0, 1.0, 2.0), 2.0, atol=1e-9, rtol=1e-9)
    assert_almost_equal(median3(2.0, 2.0, 2.0), 2.0, atol=1e-9, rtol=1e-9)
    assert_almost_equal(median3(5.0, 1.0, 4.0), 4.0, atol=1e-9, rtol=1e-9)
    print("  test_median3 OK")


def test_fmt() raises:
    assert_equal(fmt2(0.0), "0.00")
    assert_equal(fmt2(123.456), "123.46")
    assert_equal(fmt2(-2.5), "-2.50")
    assert_equal(fmt_int(0), "0")
    assert_equal(fmt_int(999), "999")
    assert_equal(fmt_int(1000), "1,000")
    assert_equal(fmt_int(250000), "250,000")
    assert_equal(fmt_int(1234567), "1,234,567")
    print("  test_fmt OK")


def test_parsers_small() raises:
    var log_text = String(
        '1.1.1.1 - - [05/Nov/2026:09:00:01 +0000] "GET /a HTTP/1.1" 200 100 "-" "ua"\n'
        + '1.1.1.2 - - [05/Nov/2026:09:00:02 +0000] "GET /b HTTP/1.1" 404 50 "-" "ua"\n'
    )
    var log_lines = to_owned_lines(log_text)
    var ref_log = parse_log_reference(log_lines)
    assert_equal(ref_log.parsed, 2)
    assert_equal(ref_log.errors, 1)
    assert_equal(ref_log.bytes_total, 150)
    var par_log = parse_log_parallel(log_lines, 2, 1)
    assert_true(log_agg_equal(ref_log, par_log))
    var tick_text = String(
        "timestamp,symbol,price,size,exchange\n"
        + "1000,ACME,100.00,10,NYSE\n"
        + "1010,ACME,110.00,20,NYSE\n"
    )
    var tick_lines = to_owned_lines(tick_text)
    var ref_tick = parse_tick_reference(tick_lines)
    assert_equal(ref_tick.parsed, 2)
    assert_equal(ref_tick.total_volume, 30)
    var par_tick = parse_tick_parallel(tick_lines, 2, 1)
    assert_true(tick_agg_equal(ref_tick, par_tick))
    print("  test_parsers_small OK")


def run_tests() raises:
    print("Running unit tests:")
    test_median3()
    test_fmt()
    test_parsers_small()
    print("All benchmark unit tests passed.")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Embedded fallback generators (same format / seed family as etl_gen_data).
# Writes small + medium inputs when the data directory is missing them.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def gen_log_lines(n: Int, start_ts: Int) -> List[String]:
    var urls = List[String]()
    urls.append("/index.html")
    urls.append("/products")
    urls.append("/cart")
    urls.append("/checkout")
    urls.append("/api/v1/users")
    urls.append("/static/app.js")
    urls.append("/about")
    urls.append("/login")
    var lines = List[String]()
    var ts = start_ts
    for _ in range(n):
        ts += 1 + Int(random_float64() * 4.0)
        var url = urls[Int(random_float64() * Float64(len(urls)))]
        var r = random_float64()
        var status: Int
        if r < 0.68:
            status = 200
        elif r < 0.76:
            status = 304
        elif r < 0.82:
            status = 301
        elif r < 0.90:
            status = 404
        elif r < 0.94:
            status = 400
        elif r < 0.96:
            status = 403
        elif r < 0.98:
            status = 500
        else:
            status = 503
        var size = 120 + Int(random_float64() * 8000.0)
        if status != 200:
            size = 180 + Int(random_float64() * 2400.0)
        var hh = (ts / 3600) % 24
        var mm = (ts / 60) % 60
        var ss = ts % 60
        var hh_s = String(hh)
        if hh < 10:
            hh_s = "0" + hh_s
        var mm_s = String(mm)
        if mm < 10:
            mm_s = "0" + mm_s
        var ss_s = String(ss)
        if ss < 10:
            ss_s = "0" + ss_s
        var ip = String(1 + Int(random_float64() * 223.0)) + "." + String(Int(random_float64() * 256.0)) + "." + String(Int(random_float64() * 256.0)) + "." + String(1 + Int(random_float64() * 254.0))
        lines.append(
            ip
            + ' - - [05/Nov/2026:'
            + hh_s
            + ":"
            + mm_s
            + ":"
            + ss_s
            + ' +0000] "GET '
            + url
            + ' HTTP/1.1" '
            + String(status)
            + " "
            + String(size)
            + ' "-" "curl/8.5.0"'
        )
    return lines^


def gen_tick_lines(n: Int, start_ts: Int) -> List[String]:
    var symbols = List[String]()
    symbols.append("ACME")
    symbols.append("BOLT")
    symbols.append("CRUX")
    symbols.append("DUNE")
    symbols.append("ECHO")
    symbols.append("FLUX")
    symbols.append("GALE")
    symbols.append("HALO")
    var bases = List[Float64]()
    bases.append(142.35)
    bases.append(87.10)
    bases.append(231.80)
    bases.append(45.60)
    bases.append(312.25)
    bases.append(19.95)
    bases.append(178.40)
    bases.append(64.75)
    var prices = List[Float64]()
    for b in bases:
        prices.append(b)
    var lines = List[String]()
    lines.append("timestamp,symbol,price,size,exchange")
    var ts = start_ts
    for _ in range(n):
        ts += Int(random_float64() * 3.0)
        var si = Int(random_float64() * Float64(len(symbols)))
        var price = prices[si] + (random_float64() - 0.5) * 0.02 * prices[si]
        if price < 1.0:
            price = 1.0 + random_float64() * 2.0
        prices[si] = price
        var size = 1 + Int(random_float64() * 500.0)
        var ex = "NYSE"
        if random_float64() < 0.5:
            ex = "NASDAQ"
        lines.append(
            String(ts) + "," + symbols[si] + "," + fmt2(price) + "," + String(size) + "," + ex
        )
    return lines^


def ensure_inputs(data_dir: String) raises:
    if not Path(data_dir).exists():
        mkdir(data_dir)
    var log_path = Path(data_dir + "/access.log")
    if not log_path.exists():
        print("Generating medium access.log (100,000 lines) ...")
        seed(20261105)
        log_path.write_text(String("\n").join(gen_log_lines(100000, 36000)) + "\n")
    var tick_path = Path(data_dir + "/ticks.csv")
    if not tick_path.exists():
        print("Generating medium ticks.csv (250,000 trades) ...")
        seed(20261106)
        tick_path.write_text(String("\n").join(gen_tick_lines(250000, 1793836800)) + "\n")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Entry point.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def main() raises:
    run_tests()
    var args = argv()
    var data_dir = "/tmp/etl_data"
    if len(args) > 1:
        data_dir = String(args[1])
    ensure_inputs(data_dir)
    var h = Harness()

    # --- Read-time split: how much of the job is I/O? ---
    print("Measuring file read times (median of 3):")
    var log_path = Path(data_dir + "/access.log")
    var tick_path = Path(data_dir + "/ticks.csv")
    var log_read = time_read(log_path)
    var tick_read = time_read(tick_path)
    print("  access.log:", fmt_int(log_read[1]), "bytes in", fmt2(log_read[0]), "s")
    print("  ticks.csv: ", fmt_int(tick_read[1]), "bytes in", fmt2(tick_read[0]), "s")

    # --- Load once, benchmark many times (parse time only) ---
    print("Loading files into memory ...")
    var log_text = log_path.read_text()
    var tick_text = tick_path.read_text()
    var log_lines = to_owned_lines(log_text)
    var tick_lines = to_owned_lines(tick_text)
    print("  logs:", len(log_lines), "lines;", " ticks:", len(tick_lines), "lines")

    # --- Worker scaling ---
    print("Worker scaling study (chunk=2000):")
    var log_ref_s = bench_reference(h, "medium", "logs", log_lines, log_read[1])
    bench_logs(h, "medium", log_lines, log_read[1], log_ref_s)
    var tick_ref_s = bench_reference(h, "medium", "ticks", tick_lines, tick_read[1])
    bench_ticks(h, "medium", tick_lines, tick_read[1], tick_ref_s)

    # --- Chunk-size sensitivity at 4 workers ---
    print("Chunk-size study:")
    bench_chunk_sizes(h, "medium", "logs", log_lines, log_read[1], log_ref_s)
    bench_chunk_sizes(h, "medium", "ticks", tick_lines, tick_read[1], tick_ref_s)

    # --- Small dataset: where does parallelism stop paying? ---
    print("Small-dataset study (first 5,000 log lines / 10,000 tick lines):")
    var small_logs = List[String]()
    for i in range(5000):
        small_logs.append(log_lines[i])
    var small_ticks = List[String]()
    for i in range(10000):
        small_ticks.append(tick_lines[i])
    var small_log_ref = bench_reference(h, "small", "logs", small_logs, log_read[1] / 20)
    bench_logs(h, "small", small_logs, log_read[1] / 20, small_log_ref)
    var small_tick_ref = bench_reference(h, "small", "ticks", small_ticks, tick_read[1] / 25)
    bench_ticks(h, "small", small_ticks, tick_read[1] / 25, small_tick_ref)

    # --- Reports ---
    print("Writing reports to " + data_dir + "/benchmark_report.{txt,csv} ...")
    h.write_reports(data_dir)
    print("Measurements recorded:", len(h.results), " failures:", h.failures)
    assert_true(h.failures == 0, "all parallel results must equal their references")
    # The parallel path must not be pathologically slower than the
    # reference on the medium log file with 4 workers (tolerates noise
    # and small-file overhead; it is a smoke bound, not a perf claim).
    var found = False
    for m in h.results:
        if m.dataset == "medium" and m.parser == "logs" and m.mode == "parallel" and m.workers == 4:
            found = True
            assert_true(m.speedup > 0.9, "4-worker log parse must stay within 10% of reference speed")
    assert_true(found, "expected a medium/logs/parallel/4 measurement")
    print("DONE: benchmark complete, all results verified correct.")

--- SOURCE: mojo_samples/etl_gen_data.mojo ---
# etl_gen_data.mojo
#
# Synthetic data generator for the START project's Mojo ETL pipeline samples.
#
# Original work, written for this project. Standard library only, no Python
# interop, no network access. Requires Mojo 1.2.0 (stdlib under the `std`
# namespace; `def`, not `fn`). No MAX dependency in this file.
#
# It writes three realistic, deterministic datasets to disk so the other
# pipeline programs have real files to ingest:
#   access.log   - web access logs in combined-log format
#   ticks.csv    - market tick data: timestamp,symbol,price,size,exchange
#   events.jsonl - structured JSON-lines service logs
#
# Determinism: every dataset is produced from a fixed seed, so re-running
# the generator with the same scale reproduces byte-identical files. That
# makes the parallel-vs-reference assertions in the parser programs
# meaningful: the file under test never changes between runs.
#
# Usage:
#   mojo etl_gen_data.mojo [small|medium|large] [output-dir]
# Defaults: medium, /tmp/etl_data
#
# The generator self-checks its output: it re-reads every file it wrote,
# counts lines, spot-checks invariants (prices positive, sizes positive,
# status codes from the known set, JSON lines balanced), and verifies that
# generating the same small sample twice yields identical bytes.

from std.pathlib import Path
from std.testing import assert_equal, assert_true, assert_almost_equal
from std.random import seed, random_float64
from std.sys import argv
from std.os import mkdir

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Small formatting helpers (no stdlib float formatting exists, so we do our
# own fixed-point rendering).
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def two_digit(n: Int) -> String:
    if n < 10:
        return "0" + String(n)
    return String(n)


def fmt_float2(x: Float64) -> String:
    """Render a float with exactly two digits after the decimal point."""
    var neg = False
    var v = x
    if v < 0.0:
        neg = True
        v = -v
    var scaled = Int(v * 100.0 + 0.5)
    var whole = scaled / 100
    var frac = scaled - whole * 100
    var out = String(whole) + "." + two_digit(frac)
    if neg:
        return "-" + out
    return out


def fmt_float4(x: Float64) -> String:
    """Render a float with exactly four digits after the decimal point."""
    var neg = False
    var v = x
    if v < 0.0:
        neg = True
        v = -v
    var scaled = Int(v * 10000.0 + 0.5)
    var whole = scaled / 10000
    var frac = scaled - whole * 10000
    var frac_str = String(frac)
    while frac_str.byte_length() < 4:
        frac_str = "0" + frac_str
    var out = String(whole) + "." + frac_str
    if neg:
        return "-" + out
    return out


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Calendar math: epoch seconds -> ISO-8601 UTC timestamp.
# Uses the civil-from-days algorithm (days since 1970-01-01), which handles
# leap years exactly. `days_to_ymd` is the inverse of `ymd_to_days`, and the
# unit tests below check both directions plus a known epoch.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def days_to_ymd(z: Int) -> Tuple[Int, Int, Int]:
    var zz = z + 719468
    var era = zz / 146097
    if zz < 0:
        era -= 1
    var doe = zz - era * 146097
    var yoe = (doe - doe / 1460 + doe / 36524 - doe / 146096) / 365
    var y = yoe + era * 400
    var doy = doe - (365 * yoe + yoe / 4 - yoe / 100)
    var mp = (5 * doy + 2) / 153
    var d = doy - (153 * mp + 2) / 5 + 1
    var m = mp + 3
    if mp >= 10:
        m = mp - 9
    if m <= 2:
        y += 1
    return (y, m, d)


def ymd_to_days(y: Int, m: Int, d: Int) -> Int:
    var yy = y
    if m <= 2:
        yy -= 1
    var era = yy / 400
    if yy < 0:
        era -= 1
    var yoe = yy - era * 400
    var mp = m - 3
    if m <= 2:
        mp = m + 9
    var doy = (153 * mp + 2) / 5 + d - 1
    var doe = yoe * 365 + yoe / 4 - yoe / 100 + doy
    return era * 146097 + doe - 719468


def format_ts(epoch: Int) -> String:
    """Epoch seconds -> 'YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM:SSZ' (UTC)."""
    var days = epoch / 86400
    var secs = epoch - days * 86400
    var ymd = days_to_ymd(days)
    var hh = secs / 3600
    var mm = (secs - hh * 3600) / 60
    var ss = secs - hh * 3600 - mm * 60
    return (
        String(ymd[0])
        + "-"
        + two_digit(ymd[1])
        + "-"
        + two_digit(ymd[2])
        + "T"
        + two_digit(hh)
        + ":"
        + two_digit(mm)
        + ":"
        + two_digit(ss)
        + "Z"
    )


def month_name(m: Int) -> String:
    var names = List[String]()
    names.append("Jan")
    names.append("Feb")
    names.append("Mar")
    names.append("Apr")
    names.append("May")
    names.append("Jun")
    names.append("Jul")
    names.append("Aug")
    names.append("Sep")
    names.append("Oct")
    names.append("Nov")
    names.append("Dec")
    return names[m - 1]


def format_log_ts(epoch: Int) -> String:
    """Epoch seconds -> '05/Nov/2026:10:23:11 +0000' (combined log format)."""
    var days = epoch / 86400
    var secs = epoch - days * 86400
    var ymd = days_to_ymd(days)
    var hh = secs / 3600
    var mm = (secs - hh * 3600) / 60
    var ss = secs - hh * 3600 - mm * 60
    return (
        two_digit(ymd[2])
        + "/"
        + month_name(ymd[1])
        + "/"
        + String(ymd[0])
        + ":"
        + two_digit(hh)
        + ":"
        + two_digit(mm)
        + ":"
        + two_digit(ss)
        + " +0000"
    )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Random helpers built on the stdlib's seeded global RNG.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def rand_int(n: Int) -> Int:
    """Uniform integer in [0, n)."""
    return Int(random_float64() * Float64(n))


def pick_str(options: List[String]) -> String:
    return options[rand_int(len(options))]


def rand_ip() -> String:
    # Realistic mix: mostly public ranges, some RFC1918, a few loopback.
    var r = random_float64()
    if r < 0.08:
        return "10." + String(rand_int(256)) + "." + String(rand_int(256)) + "." + String(rand_int(256))
    if r < 0.14:
        return "192.168." + String(rand_int(256)) + "." + String(rand_int(256))
    if r < 0.17:
        return "172." + String(16 + rand_int(16)) + "." + String(rand_int(256)) + "." + String(rand_int(256))
    return (
        String(1 + rand_int(223))
        + "."
        + String(rand_int(256))
        + "."
        + String(rand_int(256))
        + "."
        + String(1 + rand_int(254))
    )


def lognormalish(mean: Float64, spread: Float64) -> Float64:
    """Cheap positive skew: average of uniforms, stretched. Good enough for
    synthetic sizes/latencies; this is a generator, not a statistics library."""
    var u = (random_float64() + random_float64() + random_float64()) / 3.0
    return mean * (1.0 + (u - 0.5) * 2.0 * spread)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Static vocabularies. The generator only ever emits values from these
# tables, which is what lets the parsers validate "status in known set"
# and lets the pipeline join on a closed dimension of URLs / symbols.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def log_urls() -> List[String]:
    var urls = List[String]()
    urls.append("/index.html")
    urls.append("/products")
    urls.append("/products/42")
    urls.append("/products/1087")
    urls.append("/cart")
    urls.append("/checkout")
    urls.append("/search?q=mojo")
    urls.append("/search?q=etl")
    urls.append("/api/v1/users")
    urls.append("/api/v1/orders")
    urls.append("/api/v1/ticks")
    urls.append("/static/app.js")
    urls.append("/static/style.css")
    urls.append("/images/logo.png")
    urls.append("/about")
    urls.append("/login")
    return urls^


def log_methods() -> List[String]:
    var m = List[String]()
    m.append("GET")
    m.append("GET")
    m.append("GET")
    m.append("GET")
    m.append("GET")
    m.append("POST")
    m.append("POST")
    m.append("PUT")
    m.append("DELETE")
    m.append("HEAD")
    return m^


def log_status() -> Int:
    # Weighted status distribution: mostly 200, a long tail of errors.
    var r = random_float64()
    if r < 0.68:
        return 200
    if r < 0.76:
        return 304
    if r < 0.82:
        return 301
    if r < 0.90:
        return 404
    if r < 0.94:
        return 400
    if r < 0.96:
        return 403
    if r < 0.98:
        return 500
    return 503


def log_user_agents() -> List[String]:
    var uas = List[String]()
    uas.append("Mozilla/5.0 (X11; Linux x86_64) AppleWebKit/537.36 Chrome/131.0 Safari/537.36")
    uas.append("Mozilla/5.0 (Macintosh; Intel Mac OS X 14_7) AppleWebKit/605.1.15 Version/18.2 Safari/605.1.15")
    uas.append("Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64; rv:133.0) Gecko/20100101 Firefox/133.0")
    uas.append("curl/8.5.0")
    uas.append("MojoETL-Monitor/1.2 (+https://example.invalid/health)")
    uas.append("Mozilla/5.0 (iPhone; CPU iPhone OS 18_2 like Mac OS X) AppleWebKit/605.1.15 Mobile/15E148")
    return uas^


def log_referrers() -> List[String]:
    var refs = List[String]()
    refs.append("-")
    refs.append("-")
    refs.append("https://search.example.com/")
    refs.append("https://news.example.org/article/8812")
    refs.append("/products")
    refs.append("/index.html")
    return refs^


def tick_symbols() -> List[String]:
    var s = List[String]()
    s.append("ACME")
    s.append("BOLT")
    s.append("CRUX")
    s.append("DUNE")
    s.append("ECHO")
    s.append("FLUX")
    s.append("GALE")
    s.append("HALO")
    return s^


def tick_base_price(symbol: String) -> Float64:
    if symbol == "ACME":
        return 142.35
    if symbol == "BOLT":
        return 87.10
    if symbol == "CRUX":
        return 231.80
    if symbol == "DUNE":
        return 45.60
    if symbol == "ECHO":
        return 312.25
    if symbol == "FLUX":
        return 19.95
    if symbol == "GALE":
        return 178.40
    return 64.75


def tick_exchanges() -> List[String]:
    var e = List[String]()
    e.append("NYSE")
    e.append("NASDAQ")
    e.append("NYSE")
    e.append("NASDAQ")
    e.append("BATS")
    return e^


def json_services() -> List[String]:
    var s = List[String]()
    s.append("gateway")
    s.append("auth")
    s.append("orders")
    s.append("pricing")
    s.append("inventory")
    s.append("notify")
    return s^


def json_level() -> String:
    var r = random_float64()
    if r < 0.62:
        return "INFO"
    if r < 0.82:
        return "DEBUG"
    if r < 0.93:
        return "WARN"
    return "ERROR"


def json_regions() -> List[String]:
    var r = List[String]()
    r.append("us-east-1")
    r.append("us-west-2")
    r.append("eu-west-1")
    r.append("ap-south-1")
    return r^


def json_message(level: String, service: String) -> String:
    if level == "ERROR":
        var msgs = List[String]()
        msgs.append("upstream timeout after 3000ms")
        msgs.append("connection reset by peer")
        msgs.append("order validation failed: negative quantity")
        msgs.append("payment gateway returned 502")
        msgs.append("cache stampede detected on hot key")
        return pick_str(msgs)
    if level == "WARN":
        var msgs = List[String]()
        msgs.append("retrying upstream request (attempt 2)")
        msgs.append("slow query: 812ms on orders table")
        msgs.append("rate limit approaching for client")
        msgs.append("deprecated endpoint called")
        return pick_str(msgs)
    if level == "DEBUG":
        var msgs = List[String]()
        msgs.append("cache hit for key")
        msgs.append("request headers parsed")
        msgs.append("connection pool checkout ok")
        return pick_str(msgs)
    var msgs = List[String]()
    msgs.append("request completed")
    msgs.append("order placed")
    msgs.append("price snapshot published")
    msgs.append("user session refreshed")
    msgs.append("inventory reserved")
    return pick_str(msgs)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Generators. Each one returns the full file content as one String built
# from a List of lines (join is linear; repeated `+` on a growing String
# would be quadratic). Lines are produced in timestamp order so downstream
# time-bucketing never has to sort.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def gen_access_log(n_lines: Int, start_epoch: Int) -> String:
    var urls = log_urls()
    var methods = log_methods()
    var uas = log_user_agents()
    var refs = log_referrers()
    var lines = List[String]()
    var ts = start_epoch
    for _ in range(n_lines):
        ts += 1 + rand_int(4)
        var method = pick_str(methods)
        var url = pick_str(urls)
        var status = log_status()
        var size: Int
        if status == 200:
            size = Int(lognormalish(4200.0, 0.9))
            if size < 120:
                size = 120
        elif status == 304 or status == 301:
            size = rand_int(400)
        else:
            size = 180 + rand_int(2400)
        var referrer = pick_str(refs)
        var ua = pick_str(uas)
        var line = (
            rand_ip()
            + " - - ["
            + format_log_ts(ts)
            + '] "'
            + method
            + " "
            + url
            + ' HTTP/1.1" '
            + String(status)
            + " "
            + String(size)
            + ' "'
            + referrer
            + '" "'
            + ua
            + '"'
        )
        lines.append(line)
    return String("\n").join(lines) + "\n"


def gen_ticks(n_lines: Int, start_epoch: Int) -> String:
    var symbols = tick_symbols()
    var exchanges = tick_exchanges()
    # Random-walk state per symbol, kept in parallel lists.
    var prices = List[Float64]()
    for s in symbols:
        prices.append(tick_base_price(s))
    var lines = List[String]()
    var ts = start_epoch
    for _ in range(n_lines):
        ts += rand_int(3)
        var si = rand_int(len(symbols))
        var sym = symbols[si]
        var price = prices[si]
        # Random walk: small drift plus a shock, reflected at a floor so
        # prices stay strictly positive (a parser invariant).
        var shock = (random_float64() - 0.5) * 0.02 * price
        var drift = 0.00002 * price
        price = price + drift + shock
        if price < 1.0:
            price = 1.0 + random_float64() * 2.0
        prices[si] = price
        var size = 1 + rand_int(500)
        if random_float64() < 0.05:
            size = 1000 + rand_int(9000)
        var line = (
            String(ts)
            + ","
            + sym
            + ","
            + fmt_float2(price)
            + ","
            + String(size)
            + ","
            + pick_str(exchanges)
        )
        lines.append(line)
    var header = "timestamp,symbol,price,size,exchange\n"
    return header + String("\n").join(lines) + "\n"


def gen_jsonl(n_lines: Int, start_epoch: Int) -> String:
    var services = json_services()
    var regions = json_regions()
    var lines = List[String]()
    var ts = start_epoch
    for _ in range(n_lines):
        ts += 1 + rand_int(3)
        var level = json_level()
        var service = pick_str(services)
        var latency = lognormalish(42.0, 1.4)
        if latency < 0.5:
            latency = 0.5
        if level == "ERROR":
            latency = latency + 900.0 + random_float64() * 1800.0
        elif level == "WARN":
            latency = latency + 120.0 + random_float64() * 500.0
        var req = "req-" + String(100000 + rand_int(900000))
        var msg = json_message(level, service)
        var statuses = List[Int]()
        statuses.append(200)
        statuses.append(200)
        statuses.append(200)
        statuses.append(201)
        statuses.append(204)
        statuses.append(301)
        statuses.append(400)
        statuses.append(401)
        statuses.append(403)
        statuses.append(404)
        statuses.append(500)
        statuses.append(502)
        statuses.append(503)
        var status = statuses[rand_int(len(statuses))]
        if level == "ERROR":
            status = 500 + rand_int(4)
        var line = (
            '{"ts":"'
            + format_ts(ts)
            + '","level":"'
            + level
            + '","service":"'
            + service
            + '","msg":"'
            + msg
            + '","latency_ms":'
            + fmt_float2(latency)
            + ',"status":'
            + String(status)
            + ',"request_id":"'
            + req
            + '","meta":{"region":"'
            + pick_str(regions)
            + '"}}'
        )
        lines.append(line)
    return String("\n").join(lines) + "\n"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Verification: re-read what was written and check invariants.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def count_lines(text: String) -> Int:
    var n = 0
    for line in text.split("\n"):
        if line.byte_length() > 0:
            n += 1
    return n


def verify_access_log(path: Path, expect_lines: Int) raises:
    var text = path.read_text()
    var lines = text.split("\n")
    var seen = 0
    var bad_status = 0
    for line in lines:
        if line.byte_length() == 0:
            continue
        seen += 1
        # Status is the token after the closing quote of the request.
        var q = line.find('"')
        # Second quote: search the remainder of the line.
        var after_first = String(line[byte = q + 1 : line.byte_length()])
        var q2rel = after_first.find('"')
        var q2 = q + 1 + q2rel
        assert_true(q >= 0 and q2 > q, "log line must contain a quoted request")
        var rest = String(line[byte = q2 + 2 : line.byte_length()])
        var parts = rest.split(" ")
        var status = Int(String(parts[0]))
        if status != 200 and status != 301 and status != 304 and status != 400 and status != 403 and status != 404 and status != 500 and status != 503:
            bad_status += 1
    assert_equal(seen, expect_lines)
    assert_equal(bad_status, 0)
    print("  access.log OK:", seen, "lines, all statuses in the known set")


def verify_ticks(path: Path, expect_lines: Int) raises:
    var text = path.read_text()
    var lines = text.split("\n")
    var seen = 0
    var bad = 0
    for line in lines:
        if line.byte_length() == 0:
            continue
        if String(line) == "timestamp,symbol,price,size,exchange":
            continue
        seen += 1
        var parts = line.split(",")
        if len(parts) != 5:
            bad += 1
            continue
        var price = Float64(String(parts[2]))
        var size = Int(String(parts[3]))
        if price <= 0.0 or size <= 0:
            bad += 1
    assert_equal(seen, expect_lines)
    assert_equal(bad, 0)
    print("  ticks.csv OK:", seen, "trades, all prices/sizes positive")


def verify_jsonl(path: Path, expect_lines: Int) raises:
    var text = path.read_text()
    var lines = text.split("\n")
    var seen = 0
    var bad = 0
    for line in lines:
        if line.byte_length() == 0:
            continue
        seen += 1
        var s = String(line)
        if not s.startswith("{") or not s.endswith("}"):
            bad += 1
        if s.find('"level":"') < 0 or s.find('"service":"') < 0 or s.find('"latency_ms":') < 0:
            bad += 1
    assert_equal(seen, expect_lines)
    assert_equal(bad, 0)
    print("  events.jsonl OK:", seen, "events, all objects well-formed")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Unit tests (run on every invocation; they use tiny inputs and no files
# except the determinism check, which compares two in-memory generations).
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def test_calendar() raises:
    assert_equal(format_ts(0), "1970-01-01T00:00:00Z")
    assert_equal(format_ts(1793836800), "2026-11-05T00:00:00Z")
    assert_equal(format_ts(1768465800), "2026-01-15T08:30:00Z")
    assert_equal(format_ts(86400), "1970-01-02T00:00:00Z")
    assert_equal(format_log_ts(1793836800), "05/Nov/2026:00:00:00 +0000")
    # Round-trip: ymd -> days -> ymd for a spread of dates incl. leap day.
    var dates = List[Tuple[Int, Int, Int]]()
    dates.append((1970, 1, 1))
    dates.append((2000, 2, 29))
    dates.append((2024, 2, 29))
    dates.append((2026, 11, 5))
    dates.append((1999, 12, 31))
    dates.append((2100, 3, 1))
    for d in dates:
        var back = days_to_ymd(ymd_to_days(d[0], d[1], d[2]))
        assert_equal(back[0], d[0])
        assert_equal(back[1], d[1])
        assert_equal(back[2], d[2])
    print("  test_calendar OK")


def test_formatting() raises:
    assert_equal(fmt_float2(0.0), "0.00")
    assert_equal(fmt_float2(123.456), "123.46")
    assert_equal(fmt_float2(3.1), "3.10")
    assert_equal(fmt_float2(-2.5), "-2.50")
    assert_equal(fmt_float4(1.23456), "1.2346")
    assert_equal(fmt_float4(0.5), "0.5000")
    assert_equal(two_digit(5), "05")
    assert_equal(two_digit(12), "12")
    print("  test_formatting OK")


def test_determinism() raises:
    seed(777)
    var a = gen_access_log(200, 1793836800)
    seed(777)
    var b = gen_access_log(200, 1793836800)
    assert_equal(a, b)
    seed(777)
    var c = gen_ticks(200, 1793836800)
    seed(777)
    var d = gen_ticks(200, 1793836800)
    assert_equal(c, d)
    seed(777)
    var e = gen_jsonl(200, 1793836800)
    seed(777)
    var f = gen_jsonl(200, 1793836800)
    assert_equal(e, f)
    # Different seeds must differ (guards against a silently constant RNG).
    seed(778)
    var g = gen_access_log(200, 1793836800)
    assert_true(a != g, "different seeds must produce different logs")
    print("  test_determinism OK")


def test_generated_line_shapes() raises:
    seed(99)
    var log = gen_access_log(1, 1793836800)
    var parts = log.split(" ")
    assert_true(len(parts) >= 10, "combined log line has many fields")
    assert_true(String(parts[3]).startswith("[05/Nov/2026:00:00:0"), "log ts is in range")
    seed(99)
    var ticks = gen_ticks(1, 1793836800)
    var tlines = ticks.split("\n")
    assert_equal(String(tlines[0]), "timestamp,symbol,price,size,exchange")
    assert_equal(len(tlines[1].split(",")), 5)
    seed(99)
    var ev = gen_jsonl(1, 1793836800)
    assert_true(ev.startswith('{"ts":"2026-11-05'), "jsonl starts with ts")
    assert_true(ev.find('"meta":{"region":"') >= 0, "jsonl has nested meta")
    print("  test_generated_line_shapes OK")


def run_tests() raises:
    print("Running unit tests:")
    test_calendar()
    test_formatting()
    test_determinism()
    test_generated_line_shapes()
    print("All generator unit tests passed.")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Entry point.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def main() raises:
    run_tests()
    var args = argv()
    var scale = "medium"
    if len(args) > 1:
        scale = String(args[1])
    var out_dir = "/tmp/etl_data"
    if len(args) > 2:
        out_dir = String(args[2])
    var n_log = 100000
    var n_tick = 250000
    var n_json = 50000
    if scale == "small":
        n_log = 5000
        n_tick = 10000
        n_json = 3000
    elif scale == "large":
        n_log = 500000
        n_tick = 1000000
        n_json = 250000
    elif scale != "medium":
        print("Unknown scale '" + scale + "', expected small|medium|large.")
        print("Usage: mojo etl_gen_data.mojo [small|medium|large] [output-dir]")
        return
    print("Generating " + scale + " datasets in " + out_dir + " ...")
    if not Path(out_dir).exists():
        mkdir(out_dir)
    var start_epoch = 1793836800  # 2026-11-05T00:00:00Z
    seed(20261105)
    var log_text = gen_access_log(n_log, start_epoch)
    var log_path = Path(out_dir + "/access.log")
    log_path.write_text(log_text)
    print("  wrote access.log:", log_text.byte_length(), "bytes")
    seed(20261106)
    var tick_text = gen_ticks(n_tick, start_epoch)
    var tick_path = Path(out_dir + "/ticks.csv")
    tick_path.write_text(tick_text)
    print("  wrote ticks.csv:", tick_text.byte_length(), "bytes")
    seed(20261107)
    var json_text = gen_jsonl(n_json, start_epoch)
    var json_path = Path(out_dir + "/events.jsonl")
    json_path.write_text(json_text)
    print("  wrote events.jsonl:", json_text.byte_length(), "bytes")
    print("Verifying written files:")
    verify_access_log(log_path, n_log)
    verify_ticks(tick_path, n_tick)
    verify_jsonl(json_path, n_json)
    print("DONE: generated", n_log, "log lines,", n_tick, "ticks,", n_json, "events in", out_dir)

# etl_incremental.mojo
#
# Incremental ETL with checkpoints for the START project's Mojo ETL
# samples.
#
# Original work, written for this project. Standard library only, plus
# chunked processing with per-chunk aggregates merged afterwards.
# (Mojo 1.2.0's plain `mojo` package no longer ships `parallelize`;
# it moved to the MAX package, so chunks run sequentially here.)
# Requires Mojo 1.2.0.
#
# Batch ETL re-reads the whole file every run. Incremental ETL does not:
# it remembers where the previous run stopped (a checkpoint) and only
# processes what arrived since, then merges the new partial state into
# the persisted state. This program demonstrates the pattern on the tick
# feed, which is append-only and time-ordered:
#
#   phase A  -> process the first half of ticks.csv, write a checkpoint
#               (last processed line offset + last processed timestamp)
#               and a state snapshot (per-symbol OHLCV + totals as CSV)
#   phase B  -> reload the checkpoint and the state snapshot, process
#               only the second half, merge it into the loaded state
#   verify   -> reparse the whole file from scratch and assert the
#               incrementally built state is exactly equal (integers)
#               or within 1e-9 relative tolerance (VWAP numerators)
#
# The checkpoint carries two guards, because either one alone can lie:
#   - line offset: how many lines were consumed (cheap, exact)
#   - watermark: the largest timestamp seen (catches a truncated or
#     replaced file: if the file's first new timestamp is at or below
#     the watermark, the run refuses to proceed incrementally and falls
#     back to a full reparse instead of silently double-counting)
#
# State snapshots are plain CSV so a human (or another tool) can read
# them; the checkpoint is a small key=value text file for the same
# reason. Both live under <data-dir>/incremental_state/ (scratch, not
# part of the corpus).
#
# Usage:
#   mojo etl_incremental.mojo [data-dir] [workers]
# Defaults: /tmp/etl_data, 4 workers. If ticks.csv is missing, a
# deterministic 100,000-trade file is generated in place first.

from std.pathlib import Path
from std.testing import assert_equal, assert_true, assert_almost_equal
from std.collections import Dict
from std.random import seed, random_float64
from std.sys import argv
from std.time import perf_counter
from std.os import mkdir


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Shared helpers.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def approx_eq(a: Float64, b: Float64, tol: Float64) -> Bool:
    var diff = a - b
    if diff < 0.0:
        diff = -diff
    var scale: Float64
    if a < 0.0:
        scale = -a
    else:
        scale = a
    if b < 0.0 and -b > scale:
        scale = -b
    elif b > scale:
        scale = b
    if scale < 1.0:
        scale = 1.0
    return diff <= tol * scale


def two_digit(n: Int) -> String:
    if n < 10:
        return "0" + String(n)
    return String(n)


def fmt2(x: Float64) -> String:
    var neg = False
    var v = x
    if v < 0.0:
        neg = True
        v = -v
    var scaled = Int(v * 100.0 + 0.5)
    var whole = scaled / 100
    var frac = scaled - whole * 100
    var out = String(whole) + "." + two_digit(frac)
    if neg:
        return "-" + out
    return out


def parse_int_slice(s: StringSlice) -> Tuple[Int, Bool]:
    var b = s.as_bytes()
    var i = 0
    while i < len(b) and (b[i] == 32 or b[i] == 9):
        i += 1
    var neg = False
    if i < len(b) and b[i] == 45:
        neg = True
        i += 1
    var v = 0
    var any_digit = False
    while i < len(b) and b[i] >= 48 and b[i] <= 57:
        v = v * 10 + Int(b[i]) - 48
        any_digit = True
        i += 1
    if neg:
        v = -v
    return (v, any_digit)


def parse_float_slice(s: StringSlice) -> Tuple[Float64, Bool]:
    var b = s.as_bytes()
    var i = 0
    while i < len(b) and (b[i] == 32 or b[i] == 9):
        i += 1
    var neg = False
    if i < len(b) and b[i] == 45:
        neg = True
        i += 1
    var v = 0.0
    var any_digit = False
    while i < len(b) and b[i] >= 48 and b[i] <= 57:
        v = v * 10.0 + Float64(Int(b[i]) - 48)
        any_digit = True
        i += 1
    if i < len(b) and b[i] == 46:
        i += 1
        var frac = 0.1
        while i < len(b) and b[i] >= 48 and b[i] <= 57:
            v += Float64(Int(b[i]) - 48) * frac
            frac *= 0.1
            any_digit = True
            i += 1
    if neg:
        v = -v
    return (v, any_digit)


def to_owned_lines(text: String) raises -> List[String]:
    var slices = text.split("\n")
    var lines = List[String]()
    for i in range(len(slices)):
        lines.append(String(slices[i]))
    return lines^


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Trade parsing and per-symbol state.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


struct Bar(Copyable, ImplicitlyCopyable, Movable):
    var open: Float64
    var high: Float64
    var low: Float64
    var close: Float64
    var volume: Int
    var trades: Int
    var vwap_num: Float64
    var first_ts: Int
    var last_ts: Int

    def __init__(out self):
        self.open = 0.0
        self.high = 0.0
        self.low = 0.0
        self.close = 0.0
        self.volume = 0
        self.trades = 0
        self.vwap_num = 0.0
        self.first_ts = 0
        self.last_ts = 0

    def __init__(out self, *, other: Self):
        self.open = other.open
        self.high = other.high
        self.low = other.low
        self.close = other.close
        self.volume = other.volume
        self.trades = other.trades
        self.vwap_num = other.vwap_num
        self.first_ts = other.first_ts
        self.last_ts = other.last_ts

    def update(mut self, price: Float64, size: Int, ts: Int):
        if self.trades == 0:
            self.open = price
            self.high = price
            self.low = price
            self.close = price
            self.first_ts = ts
            self.last_ts = ts
        else:
            if price > self.high:
                self.high = price
            if price < self.low:
                self.low = price
            if ts < self.first_ts:
                self.first_ts = ts
                self.open = price
            if ts >= self.last_ts:
                self.last_ts = ts
                self.close = price
        self.volume += size
        self.trades += 1
        self.vwap_num += price * Float64(size)

    def merge_from(mut self, ref other: Bar):
        if other.trades == 0:
            return
        if self.trades == 0:
            self.open = other.open
            self.high = other.high
            self.low = other.low
            self.close = other.close
            self.volume = other.volume
            self.trades = other.trades
            self.vwap_num = other.vwap_num
            self.first_ts = other.first_ts
            self.last_ts = other.last_ts
            return
        if other.high > self.high:
            self.high = other.high
        if other.low < self.low:
            self.low = other.low
        if other.first_ts < self.first_ts:
            self.first_ts = other.first_ts
            self.open = other.open
        if other.last_ts >= self.last_ts:
            self.last_ts = other.last_ts
            self.close = other.close
        self.volume += other.volume
        self.trades += other.trades
        self.vwap_num += other.vwap_num

    def vwap(self) -> Float64:
        if self.volume == 0:
            return 0.0
        return self.vwap_num / Float64(self.volume)


struct Trade(Copyable, ImplicitlyCopyable, Movable):
    var ok: Bool
    var ts: Int
    var symbol: String
    var price: Float64
    var size: Int

    def __init__(out self):
        self.ok = False
        self.ts = 0
        self.symbol = ""
        self.price = 0.0
        self.size = 0

    def __init__(out self, *, other: Self):
        self.ok = other.ok
        self.ts = other.ts
        self.symbol = other.symbol
        self.price = other.price
        self.size = other.size


def parse_trade(line: StringSlice) -> Trade:
    var out = Trade()
    if line.byte_length() < 8:
        return out
    var parts = line.split(",")
    if len(parts) != 5:
        return out
    var ts_read = parse_int_slice(parts[0])
    if not ts_read[1] or ts_read[0] <= 0:
        return out
    var symbol = String(parts[1])
    if symbol.byte_length() == 0:
        return out
    var price_read = parse_float_slice(parts[2])
    if not price_read[1] or price_read[0] <= 0.0:
        return out
    var size_read = parse_int_slice(parts[3])
    if not size_read[1] or size_read[0] <= 0:
        return out
    out.ok = True
    out.ts = ts_read[0]
    out.symbol = symbol
    out.price = price_read[0]
    out.size = size_read[0]
    return out


struct TickState(Movable):
    var total: Int
    var parsed: Int
    var malformed: Int
    var total_volume: Int
    var last_ts: Int
    var symbols: Dict[String, Bar]

    def __init__(out self):
        self.total = 0
        self.parsed = 0
        self.malformed = 0
        self.total_volume = 0
        self.last_ts = 0
        self.symbols = Dict[String, Bar]()

    def add_trade(mut self, t: Trade):
        self.total += 1
        if not t.ok:
            self.malformed += 1
            return
        self.parsed += 1
        self.total_volume += t.size
        if t.ts > self.last_ts:
            self.last_ts = t.ts
        var bar = self.symbols.get(t.symbol, Bar())
        bar.update(t.price, t.size, t.ts)
        self.symbols[t.symbol] = bar

    def merge(mut self, ref other: TickState):
        self.total += other.total
        self.parsed += other.parsed
        self.malformed += other.malformed
        self.total_volume += other.total_volume
        if other.last_ts > self.last_ts:
            self.last_ts = other.last_ts
        for sym in other.symbols.keys():
            var merged = self.symbols.get(sym, Bar())
            var other_bar = other.symbols.get(sym, Bar())
            merged.merge_from(other_bar)
            self.symbols[sym] = merged


def state_equal(ref a: TickState, ref b: TickState) -> Bool:
