                resting.state = STATE_CANCELLED
                self.done.append(resting^)
                self.n_cancelled += 1
                continue
            var fill = order.remaining
            if self.asks[0].remaining < fill:
                fill = self.asks[0].remaining
            var px = self.asks[0].price
            var resting_oid = self.asks[0].oid
            var resting_client = self.asks[0].client_id
            self.asks[0].remaining -= fill
            if self.asks[0].remaining == 0:
                self.asks[0].state = STATE_FILLED
            else:
                self.asks[0].state = STATE_PARTIAL
            order.remaining -= fill
            if order.remaining == 0:
                order.state = STATE_FILLED
            else:
                order.state = STATE_PARTIAL
            self._record_trade(
                order.oid, resting_oid, order.client_id, resting_client, px, fill, False
            )
            n_trades += 1
            if self.asks[0].remaining == 0:
                var filled_order = self.asks.pop(0)
                self.done.append(filled_order^)
        return n_trades

    def _match_sell(mut self, mut order: Order, limit_price: Int) -> Int:
        var n_trades = 0
        while (
            order.remaining > 0
            and len(self.bids) > 0
            and self.bids[0].price >= limit_price
        ):
            if self.bids[0].client_id == order.client_id and self.stp_mode != STP_NONE:
                if self.stp_mode == STP_REJECT_INCOMING:
                    self.last_stp_reject = True
                    return n_trades
                var resting = self.bids.pop(0)
                resting.state = STATE_CANCELLED
                self.done.append(resting^)
                self.n_cancelled += 1
                continue
            var fill = order.remaining
            if self.bids[0].remaining < fill:
                fill = self.bids[0].remaining
            var px = self.bids[0].price
            var resting_oid = self.bids[0].oid
            var resting_client = self.bids[0].client_id
            self.bids[0].remaining -= fill
            if self.bids[0].remaining == 0:
                self.bids[0].state = STATE_FILLED
            else:
                self.bids[0].state = STATE_PARTIAL
            order.remaining -= fill
            if order.remaining == 0:
                order.state = STATE_FILLED
            else:
                order.state = STATE_PARTIAL
            self._record_trade(
                resting_oid, order.oid, resting_client, order.client_id, px, fill, False
            )
            n_trades += 1
            if self.bids[0].remaining == 0:
                var filled_order = self.bids.pop(0)
                self.done.append(filled_order^)
        return n_trades

    def _available(self, side: Int, limit_price: Int) -> Int:
        var total = 0
        if side == SIDE_BUY:
            var i = 0
            while i < len(self.asks):
                if self.asks[i].price > limit_price:
                    break
                total += self.asks[i].remaining
                i += 1
        else:
            var i = 0
            while i < len(self.bids):
                if self.bids[i].price < limit_price:
                    break
                total += self.bids[i].remaining
                i += 1
        return total

    # Rest a partially filled (or unfilled) order in the continuous book.
    def _rest(mut self, var order: Order):
        order.state = STATE_ACKED
        if order.remaining < order.qty:
            order.state = STATE_PARTIAL
        if order.side == SIDE_BUY:
            self._insert_bid(order^)
        else:
            self._insert_ask(order^)

    # Move a fully filled incoming order to the done list.
    def _finish_incoming(mut self, var order: Order):
        order.state = STATE_FILLED
        self.done.append(order^)

    # -- public order entry --------------------------------------------------
    # Returns the number of trades the order printed. A rejected order
    # prints zero trades and is recorded in the reject log.
    def submit(mut self, var order: Order) -> Int:
        self.n_submitted += 1
        # Validation first: never let a nonsense order near the book.
        if order.qty <= 0:
            self._reject(order.oid, String("quantity must be positive"))
            return 0
        if order.otype == TYPE_LIMIT or order.otype == TYPE_IOC or order.otype == TYPE_FOK:
            if order.price <= 0:
                self._reject(order.oid, String("limit price must be positive"))
                return 0
        if (
            order.otype != TYPE_LIMIT
            and order.otype != TYPE_MARKET
            and order.otype != TYPE_IOC
            and order.otype != TYPE_FOK
        ):
            self._reject(order.oid, String("unknown order type"))
            return 0
        if self.phase == PHASE_HALTED:
            self._reject(order.oid, String("session halted"))
            return 0
        if self.phase == PHASE_CLOSED:
            self._reject(order.oid, String("session closed"))
            return 0
        order.seq = self._take_seq()
        self.last_stp_reject = False
        if self.phase == PHASE_PREOPEN:
            # Auction collection: no matching yet. Market orders are not
            # meaningful in an auction and are rejected.
            if order.otype == TYPE_MARKET:
                self._reject(order.oid, String("market order not allowed in auction"))
                return 0
            order.state = STATE_ACKED
            if order.side == SIDE_BUY:
                self._insert_auction_bid(order^)
            else:
                self._insert_auction_ask(order^)
            return 0
        # Continuous session.
        var n: Int
        if order.otype == TYPE_MARKET:
            if order.side == SIDE_BUY:
                n = self._match_buy(order, 1_000_000_000)
            else:
                n = self._match_sell(order, 0)
            if self.last_stp_reject and order.remaining > 0:
                var stp_oid = order.oid
                order.state = STATE_REJECTED
                self.done.append(order^)
                self._reject(stp_oid, String("self-trade prevented"))
                return n
            if order.remaining > 0:
                # Unfilled market quantity is discarded, never rested.
                order.state = STATE_CANCELLED
                self.done.append(order^)
                self.n_cancelled += 1
            else:
                self._finish_incoming(order^)
            return n
        if order.otype == TYPE_IOC:
            if order.side == SIDE_BUY:
                n = self._match_buy(order, order.price)
            else:
                n = self._match_sell(order, order.price)
            if self.last_stp_reject and order.remaining > 0:
                var stp_oid = order.oid
                order.state = STATE_REJECTED
                self.done.append(order^)
                self._reject(stp_oid, String("self-trade prevented"))
                return n
            if order.remaining > 0:
                order.state = STATE_CANCELLED
                self.done.append(order^)
                self.n_cancelled += 1
            else:
                self._finish_incoming(order^)
            return n
        if order.otype == TYPE_FOK:
            var avail = self._available(order.side, order.price)
            if avail < order.qty:
                self._reject(order.oid, String("FOK quantity not available"))
                return 0
            if order.side == SIDE_BUY:
                n = self._match_buy(order, order.price)
            else:
                n = self._match_sell(order, order.price)
            if self.last_stp_reject and order.remaining > 0:
                var stp_oid = order.oid
                order.state = STATE_REJECTED
                self.done.append(order^)
                self._reject(stp_oid, String("self-trade prevented"))
                return n
            if order.remaining > 0:
                # Should not happen (availability was checked), but if STP
                # cancelled a resting order mid-match, rest the remainder.
                self._rest(order^)
            else:
                self._finish_incoming(order^)
            return n
        # Plain limit order.
        if order.side == SIDE_BUY:
            n = self._match_buy(order, order.price)
        else:
            n = self._match_sell(order, order.price)
        if self.last_stp_reject and order.remaining > 0:
            var stp_oid = order.oid
            order.state = STATE_REJECTED
            self.done.append(order^)
            self._reject(stp_oid, String("self-trade prevented"))
            return n
        if order.remaining > 0:
            self._rest(order^)
        else:
            self._finish_incoming(order^)
        return n

    # Cancel by order id, from the continuous book or the auction book.
    # A cancelled order moves to the done list with state CANCELLED.
    def cancel(mut self, oid: Int) -> Bool:
        var i = 0
        while i < len(self.bids):
            if self.bids[i].oid == oid:
                var gone = self.bids.pop(i)
                gone.state = STATE_CANCELLED
                self.done.append(gone^)
                self.n_cancelled += 1
                return True
            i += 1
        var j = 0
        while j < len(self.asks):
            if self.asks[j].oid == oid:
                var gone = self.asks.pop(j)
                gone.state = STATE_CANCELLED
                self.done.append(gone^)
                self.n_cancelled += 1
                return True
            j += 1
        var k = 0
        while k < len(self.auction_bids):
            if self.auction_bids[k].oid == oid:
                var gone = self.auction_bids.pop(k)
                gone.state = STATE_CANCELLED
                self.done.append(gone^)
                self.n_cancelled += 1
                return True
            k += 1
        var m = 0
        while m < len(self.auction_asks):
            if self.auction_asks[m].oid == oid:
                var gone = self.auction_asks.pop(m)
                gone.state = STATE_CANCELLED
                self.done.append(gone^)
                self.n_cancelled += 1
                return True
            m += 1
        return False

    # Amend price/quantity of a resting order. The replacement gets a fresh
    # sequence number and goes to the back of its price level.
    def amend(mut self, oid: Int, new_price: Int, new_qty: Int) -> Bool:
        var side = NO_PRICE
        var client_id = 0
        var otype = TYPE_LIMIT
        var ts_ns = 0
        var in_auction = False
        var i = 0
        while i < len(self.bids):
            if self.bids[i].oid == oid:
                side = SIDE_BUY
                client_id = self.bids[i].client_id
                otype = self.bids[i].otype
                ts_ns = self.bids[i].ts_ns
                var _gone = self.bids.pop(i)
                break
            i += 1
        if side == NO_PRICE:
            var j = 0
            while j < len(self.asks):
                if self.asks[j].oid == oid:
                    side = SIDE_SELL
                    client_id = self.asks[j].client_id
                    otype = self.asks[j].otype
                    ts_ns = self.asks[j].ts_ns
                    var _gone = self.asks.pop(j)
                    break
                j += 1
        if side == NO_PRICE:
            var k = 0
            while k < len(self.auction_bids):
                if self.auction_bids[k].oid == oid:
                    side = SIDE_BUY
                    client_id = self.auction_bids[k].client_id
                    otype = self.auction_bids[k].otype
                    ts_ns = self.auction_bids[k].ts_ns
                    in_auction = True
                    var _gone = self.auction_bids.pop(k)
                    break
                k += 1
        if side == NO_PRICE:
            var m = 0
            while m < len(self.auction_asks):
                if self.auction_asks[m].oid == oid:
                    side = SIDE_SELL
                    client_id = self.auction_asks[m].client_id
                    otype = self.auction_asks[m].otype
                    ts_ns = self.auction_asks[m].ts_ns
                    in_auction = True
                    var _gone = self.auction_asks.pop(m)
                    break
                m += 1
        if side == NO_PRICE:
            return False
        self.n_cancelled += 1
        var replacement = Order(oid, client_id, side, new_price, new_qty, otype, ts_ns)
        if in_auction:
            replacement.seq = self._take_seq()
            replacement.state = STATE_ACKED
            if side == SIDE_BUY:
                self._insert_auction_bid(replacement^)
            else:
                self._insert_auction_ask(replacement^)
            return True
        var _n = self.submit(replacement^)
        return True

    # Cancel every resting order belonging to one client (a risk kill).
    def cancel_all_for_client(mut self, client_id: Int) -> Int:
        var count = 0
        var i = 0
        while i < len(self.bids):
            if self.bids[i].client_id == client_id:
                var gone = self.bids.pop(i)
                gone.state = STATE_CANCELLED
                self.done.append(gone^)
                self.n_cancelled += 1
                count += 1
            else:
                i += 1
        var j = 0
        while j < len(self.asks):
            if self.asks[j].client_id == client_id:
                var gone = self.asks.pop(j)
                gone.state = STATE_CANCELLED
                self.done.append(gone^)
                self.n_cancelled += 1
                count += 1
            else:
                j += 1
        return count

    # -- auction ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # Traded volume at a candidate clearing price: the smaller of the bid
    # quantity priced at or above it and the ask quantity priced at or
    # below it.
    def _volume_at(self, price: Int) -> Int:
        var bid_vol = 0
        var i = 0
        while i < len(self.auction_bids):
            if self.auction_bids[i].price >= price:
                bid_vol += self.auction_bids[i].remaining
            i += 1
        var ask_vol = 0
        var j = 0
        while j < len(self.auction_asks):
            if self.auction_asks[j].price <= price:
                ask_vol += self.auction_asks[j].remaining
            j += 1
        if bid_vol < ask_vol:
            return bid_vol
        return ask_vol

    # Best clearing price: maximum volume; ties break towards the
    # reference price, then towards the higher price. Returns 0 when the
    # auction book is empty.
    def clearing_price(self) -> Int:
        if len(self.auction_bids) == 0 or len(self.auction_asks) == 0:
            return 0
        var ref_px = self.reference_price
        if ref_px == 0:
            ref_px = (self.auction_bids[0].price + self.auction_asks[0].price) // 2
        var best_price = 0
        var best_vol = -1
        var best_dist = 0
        var i = 0
        while i < len(self.auction_bids):
            var p = self.auction_bids[i].price
            var v = self._volume_at(p)
            var dist = p - ref_px
            if dist < 0:
                dist = -dist
            if (
                v > best_vol
                or (v == best_vol and dist < best_dist)
                or (v == best_vol and dist == best_dist and p > best_price)
            ):
                best_vol = v
                best_price = p
                best_dist = dist
            i += 1
        var j = 0
        while j < len(self.auction_asks):
            var p = self.auction_asks[j].price
            var v = self._volume_at(p)
            var dist = p - ref_px
            if dist < 0:
                dist = -dist
            if (
                v > best_vol
                or (v == best_vol and dist < best_dist)
                or (v == best_vol and dist == best_dist and p > best_price)
            ):
                best_vol = v
                best_price = p
                best_dist = dist
            j += 1
        return best_price

    # Run the opening auction: cross at the clearing price, then carry the
    # unfilled remainder into the continuous book. Returns trade count.
    def run_auction(mut self) -> Int:
        var px = self.clearing_price()
        if px == 0:
            return 0
        var n_trades = 0
        while (
            len(self.auction_bids) > 0
            and len(self.auction_asks) > 0
            and self.auction_bids[0].price >= px
            and self.auction_asks[0].price <= px
        ):
            var fill = self.auction_bids[0].remaining
            if self.auction_asks[0].remaining < fill:
                fill = self.auction_asks[0].remaining
            var buy_oid = self.auction_bids[0].oid
            var sell_oid = self.auction_asks[0].oid
            var buy_client = self.auction_bids[0].client_id
            var sell_client = self.auction_asks[0].client_id
            self.auction_bids[0].remaining -= fill
            self.auction_asks[0].remaining -= fill
            if self.auction_bids[0].remaining == 0:
                self.auction_bids[0].state = STATE_FILLED
            else:
                self.auction_bids[0].state = STATE_PARTIAL
            if self.auction_asks[0].remaining == 0:
                self.auction_asks[0].state = STATE_FILLED
            else:
                self.auction_asks[0].state = STATE_PARTIAL
            self._record_trade(
                buy_oid, sell_oid, buy_client, sell_client, px, fill, True
            )
            n_trades += 1
            if self.auction_bids[0].remaining == 0:
                var filled_b = self.auction_bids.pop(0)
                self.done.append(filled_b^)
            if self.auction_asks[0].remaining == 0:
                var filled_a = self.auction_asks.pop(0)
                self.done.append(filled_a^)
        # Carry the remainder into the continuous book, in priority order.
        while len(self.auction_bids) > 0:
            var o = self.auction_bids.pop(0)
            if o.remaining < o.qty:
                o.state = STATE_PARTIAL
            else:
                o.state = STATE_ACKED
            self._insert_bid(o^)
        while len(self.auction_asks) > 0:
            var o = self.auction_asks.pop(0)
            if o.remaining < o.qty:
                o.state = STATE_PARTIAL
            else:
                o.state = STATE_ACKED
            self._insert_ask(o^)
        return n_trades

    # -- queries ---------------------------------------------------------------
    def best_bid(self) -> Int:
        if len(self.bids) == 0:
            return NO_PRICE
        return self.bids[0].price

    def best_ask(self) -> Int:
        if len(self.asks) == 0:
            return NO_PRICE
        return self.asks[0].price

    def total_qty(self, side: Int) -> Int:
        var total = 0
        if side == SIDE_BUY:
            var i = 0
            while i < len(self.bids):
                total += self.bids[i].remaining
                i += 1
        else:
            var i = 0
            while i < len(self.asks):
                total += self.asks[i].remaining
                i += 1
        return total

    # Lifecycle state of any order the engine has seen; STATE_UNKNOWN when
    # the id was never submitted.
    def order_state(self, oid: Int) -> Int:
        var i = 0
        while i < len(self.bids):
            if self.bids[i].oid == oid:
                return self.bids[i].state
            i += 1
        var j = 0
        while j < len(self.asks):
            if self.asks[j].oid == oid:
                return self.asks[j].state
            j += 1
        var k = 0
        while k < len(self.auction_bids):
            if self.auction_bids[k].oid == oid:
                return self.auction_bids[k].state
            k += 1
        var m = 0
        while m < len(self.auction_asks):
            if self.auction_asks[m].oid == oid:
                return self.auction_asks[m].state
            m += 1
        var n = 0
        while n < len(self.done):
            if self.done[n].oid == oid:
                return self.done[n].state
            n += 1
        var r = 0
        while r < len(self.rejected_oids):
            if self.rejected_oids[r] == oid:
                return STATE_REJECTED
            r += 1
        return STATE_UNKNOWN

    def filled_qty(self, oid: Int) -> Int:
        var i = 0
        while i < len(self.bids):
            if self.bids[i].oid == oid:
                return self.bids[i].filled()
            i += 1
        var j = 0
        while j < len(self.asks):
            if self.asks[j].oid == oid:
                return self.asks[j].filled()
            j += 1
        var k = 0
        while k < len(self.done):
            if self.done[k].oid == oid:
                return self.done[k].filled()
            k += 1
        return 0

    def check_invariants(self) -> Bool:
        var i = 0
        while i < len(self.bids):
            if self.bids[i].remaining <= 0 or self.bids[i].price <= 0:
                return False
            if i + 1 < len(self.bids):
                if self.bids[i].price < self.bids[i + 1].price:
                    return False
                if self.bids[i].price == self.bids[i + 1].price:
                    if self.bids[i].seq >= self.bids[i + 1].seq:
                        return False
            i += 1
        var j = 0
        while j < len(self.asks):
            if self.asks[j].remaining <= 0 or self.asks[j].price <= 0:
                return False
            if j + 1 < len(self.asks):
                if self.asks[j].price > self.asks[j + 1].price:
                    return False
                if self.asks[j].price == self.asks[j + 1].price:
                    if self.asks[j].seq >= self.asks[j + 1].seq:
                        return False
            j += 1
        if len(self.bids) > 0 and len(self.asks) > 0:
            if self.bids[0].price >= self.asks[0].price:
                return False
        return True

    def snapshot(self) -> String:
        var s = String("--- engine book ---\n")
        var i = len(self.asks) - 1
        while i >= 0:
            s = (
                s
                + String("  ASK ")
                + String(self.asks[i].price)
                + String(" x ")
                + String(self.asks[i].remaining)
                + String(" oid=")
                + String(self.asks[i].oid)
                + String(" ")
                + self.asks[i].state_name()
                + String("\n")
            )
            i -= 1
        var j = 0
        while j < len(self.bids):
            s = (
                s
                + String("  BID ")
                + String(self.bids[j].price)
                + String(" x ")
                + String(self.bids[j].remaining)
                + String(" oid=")
                + String(self.bids[j].oid)
                + String(" ")
                + self.bids[j].state_name()
                + String("\n")
            )
            j += 1
        s = (
            s
            + String("trades: ")
            + String(len(self.trades))
            + String(" volume: ")
            + String(self.total_volume)
            + String(" notional: ")
            + String(self.total_notional)
            + String("\n")
        )
        return s

    def tape(self) -> String:
        var s = String("--- trade tape ---\n")
        var i = 0
        while i < len(self.trades):
            s = s + self.trades[i].describe() + String("\n")
            i += 1
        return s

    def stats(self) -> String:
        var s = String("engine stats: submitted=") + String(self.n_submitted)
        s = s + String(" rejected=") + String(self.n_rejected)
        s = s + String(" cancelled=") + String(self.n_cancelled)
        s = s + String(" trades=") + String(len(self.trades))
        s = s + String(" volume=") + String(self.total_volume)
        s = s + String(" last_px=") + String(self.last_trade_price)
        return s

    # -- drop-copy queries ---------------------------------------------------
    # Reports involving one client (as buyer or seller), in order.
    # Reports are rebuilt from their fields here because a report is
    # move-only: the engine keeps the originals, the caller gets copies
    # with identical contents.
    def reports_for_client(self, client_id: Int) -> List[TradeReport]:
        var out = List[TradeReport]()
        var i = 0
        while i < len(self.reports):
            if self.reports[i].buy_client == client_id or self.reports[i].sell_client == client_id:
                var r = TradeReport(
                    self.reports[i].report_seq,
                    self.reports[i].trade_seq,
                    self.reports[i].symbol,
                    self.reports[i].buy_oid,
                    self.reports[i].sell_oid,
                    self.reports[i].buy_client,
                    self.reports[i].sell_client,
                    self.reports[i].price,
                    self.reports[i].qty,
                    self.reports[i].auction,
                )
                out.append(r^)
            i += 1
        return out^

    def client_fill_qty(self, client_id: Int) -> Int:
        var total = 0
        var i = 0
        while i < len(self.reports):
            if self.reports[i].buy_client == client_id or self.reports[i].sell_client == client_id:
                total += self.reports[i].qty
            i += 1
        return total

    def client_fill_notional(self, client_id: Int) -> Int:
        var total = 0
        var i = 0
        while i < len(self.reports):
            if self.reports[i].buy_client == client_id or self.reports[i].sell_client == client_id:
                total += self.reports[i].price * self.reports[i].qty
            i += 1
        return total

    def client_vwap(self, client_id: Int) -> Float64:
        var qty = self.client_fill_qty(client_id)
        if qty == 0:
            return 0.0
        return Float64(self.client_fill_notional(client_id)) / Float64(qty)

    def reports_summary(self) -> String:
        var s = String("drop-copy: reports=") + String(len(self.reports))
        s = s + String(" next_seq=") + String(self.next_report_seq)
        return s

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Tests. Hand-computed cases are documented in the header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def test_continuous_lifecycle_and_positions() raises:
    var eng = MatchingEngine()
    eng.set_phase(PHASE_OPEN)
    var n1 = eng.submit(Order(1, 2, SIDE_SELL, 10100, 5, TYPE_LIMIT, 1000))
    assert_equal(n1, 0)
    assert_equal(eng.order_state(1), STATE_ACKED)
    var n2 = eng.submit(Order(2, 1, SIDE_BUY, 10100, 7, TYPE_LIMIT, 2000))
    assert_equal(n2, 1)
    assert_equal(eng.trades[0].price, 10100)
    assert_equal(eng.trades[0].qty, 5)
    assert_equal(eng.order_state(1), STATE_FILLED)
    assert_equal(eng.order_state(2), STATE_PARTIAL)
    assert_equal(eng.filled_qty(1), 5)
    assert_equal(eng.filled_qty(2), 5)
    assert_equal(eng.position_qty(1), 5)
    assert_equal(eng.position_cash(1), -50500)
    assert_equal(eng.position_qty(2), -5)
    assert_equal(eng.position_cash(2), 50500)
    # Sell market 2 sweeps the remaining 2 of order 2.
    var n3 = eng.submit(Order(3, 2, SIDE_SELL, 0, 2, TYPE_MARKET, 3000))
    assert_equal(n3, 1)
    assert_equal(eng.order_state(2), STATE_FILLED)
    assert_equal(eng.order_state(3), STATE_FILLED)
    assert_equal(eng.position_qty(1), 7)
    assert_equal(eng.position_cash(1), -70700)
    assert_equal(eng.position_qty(2), -7)
    assert_equal(eng.position_cash(2), 70700)
    # Cross-check positions by recomputing from the tape.
    var q1 = 0
    var c1 = 0
    var q2 = 0
    var c2 = 0
    var i = 0
    while i < len(eng.trades):
        if eng.trades[i].buy_client == 1:
            q1 += eng.trades[i].qty
            c1 -= eng.trades[i].notional()
        if eng.trades[i].sell_client == 1:
            q1 -= eng.trades[i].qty
            c1 += eng.trades[i].notional()
        if eng.trades[i].buy_client == 2:
            q2 += eng.trades[i].qty
            c2 -= eng.trades[i].notional()
        if eng.trades[i].sell_client == 2:
            q2 -= eng.trades[i].qty
            c2 += eng.trades[i].notional()
        i += 1
    assert_equal(q1, eng.position_qty(1))
    assert_equal(c1, eng.position_cash(1))
    assert_equal(q2, eng.position_qty(2))
    assert_equal(c2, eng.position_cash(2))
    assert_true(eng.check_invariants())
    print("lifecycle ok: ACKED->PARTIAL->FILLED, positions exact, tape agrees")


def test_ioc_fok_engine() raises:
    var eng = MatchingEngine()
    eng.set_phase(PHASE_OPEN)
    var _a = eng.submit(Order(1, 2, SIDE_SELL, 10100, 2, TYPE_LIMIT, 1000))
    var n1 = eng.submit(Order(2, 1, SIDE_BUY, 10100, 5, TYPE_IOC, 2000))
    assert_equal(n1, 1)
    assert_equal(eng.order_state(2), STATE_CANCELLED)
    assert_equal(eng.best_bid(), NO_PRICE)
    var n2 = eng.submit(Order(3, 1, SIDE_BUY, 10100, 5, TYPE_FOK, 3000))
    assert_equal(n2, 0)
    assert_equal(eng.order_state(3), STATE_REJECTED)
    assert_equal(eng.reject_reason(3), String("FOK quantity not available"))
    var _b = eng.submit(Order(4, 2, SIDE_SELL, 10100, 5, TYPE_LIMIT, 4000))
    var n3 = eng.submit(Order(5, 1, SIDE_BUY, 10100, 5, TYPE_FOK, 5000))
    assert_equal(n3, 1)
    assert_equal(eng.order_state(5), STATE_FILLED)
    assert_true(eng.check_invariants())
    print("ioc/fok ok: IOC cancels remainder, FOK rejects then fills")


def test_stp_cancel_resting() raises:
    var eng = MatchingEngine()
    eng.set_phase(PHASE_OPEN)
    eng.set_stp_mode(STP_CANCEL_RESTING)
    var _a = eng.submit(Order(1, 7, SIDE_SELL, 10100, 5, TYPE_LIMIT, 1000))
    var n = eng.submit(Order(2, 7, SIDE_BUY, 10100, 5, TYPE_LIMIT, 2000))
    assert_equal(n, 0)
    assert_equal(len(eng.trades), 0)
    assert_equal(eng.order_state(1), STATE_CANCELLED)
    assert_equal(eng.best_bid(), 10100)
    assert_equal(eng.best_ask(), NO_PRICE)
    assert_true(eng.check_invariants())
    print("stp cancel-resting ok: own order cancelled, no self-trade")


def test_stp_reject_incoming() raises:
    var eng = MatchingEngine()
    eng.set_phase(PHASE_OPEN)
    eng.set_stp_mode(STP_REJECT_INCOMING)
    var _a = eng.submit(Order(1, 7, SIDE_SELL, 10100, 5, TYPE_LIMIT, 1000))
    var n = eng.submit(Order(2, 7, SIDE_BUY, 10100, 5, TYPE_LIMIT, 2000))
    assert_equal(n, 0)
    assert_equal(len(eng.trades), 0)
    assert_equal(eng.order_state(1), STATE_ACKED)
    assert_equal(eng.order_state(2), STATE_REJECTED)
    assert_equal(eng.reject_reason(2), String("self-trade prevented"))
    assert_equal(eng.best_ask(), 10100)
    assert_equal(eng.best_bid(), NO_PRICE)
    assert_true(eng.check_invariants())
    print("stp reject-incoming ok: incoming rejected, resting order intact")


def test_halt() raises:
    var eng = MatchingEngine()
    eng.set_phase(PHASE_OPEN)
    var _a = eng.submit(Order(1, 1, SIDE_BUY, 10000, 5, TYPE_LIMIT, 1000))
    eng.set_phase(PHASE_HALTED)
    var n = eng.submit(Order(2, 1, SIDE_SELL, 10000, 5, TYPE_LIMIT, 2000))
    assert_equal(n, 0)
    assert_equal(eng.order_state(2), STATE_REJECTED)
    assert_equal(eng.reject_reason(2), String("session halted"))
    # Cancels still work during a halt: a client can always reduce risk.
    assert_true(eng.cancel(1))
    assert_equal(eng.order_state(1), STATE_CANCELLED)
    eng.set_phase(PHASE_OPEN)
    var n2 = eng.submit(Order(3, 1, SIDE_SELL, 10000, 5, TYPE_LIMIT, 3000))
    assert_equal(n2, 0)
    assert_equal(eng.order_state(3), STATE_ACKED)
    print("halt ok: new orders rejected, cancels still work, resume works")


def test_auction() raises:
    # Hand-computed in the header: clearing price 10000, volume 14.
    var eng = MatchingEngine()
    assert_equal(eng.phase, PHASE_PREOPEN)
    var _a = eng.submit(Order(1, 1, SIDE_BUY, 10100, 5, TYPE_LIMIT, 1000))
    var _b = eng.submit(Order(2, 1, SIDE_BUY, 10000, 10, TYPE_LIMIT, 2000))
    var _c = eng.submit(Order(3, 1, SIDE_BUY, 9900, 5, TYPE_LIMIT, 3000))
    var _d = eng.submit(Order(4, 2, SIDE_SELL, 9900, 8, TYPE_LIMIT, 4000))
    var _e = eng.submit(Order(5, 2, SIDE_SELL, 10000, 6, TYPE_LIMIT, 5000))
    var _f = eng.submit(Order(6, 2, SIDE_SELL, 10100, 4, TYPE_LIMIT, 6000))
    assert_equal(len(eng.trades), 0)
    assert_equal(eng.clearing_price(), 10000)
    var n = eng.run_auction()
    assert_equal(n, 3)
    assert_equal(eng.total_volume, 14)
    assert_equal(eng.trades[0].price, 10000)
    assert_equal(eng.trades[1].price, 10000)
    assert_equal(eng.trades[2].price, 10000)
    assert_true(eng.trades[0].auction)
    assert_equal(eng.order_state(1), STATE_FILLED)
    assert_equal(eng.order_state(4), STATE_FILLED)
    assert_equal(eng.order_state(5), STATE_FILLED)
    assert_equal(eng.order_state(2), STATE_PARTIAL)
    assert_equal(eng.order_state(3), STATE_ACKED)
    assert_equal(eng.order_state(6), STATE_ACKED)
    assert_equal(eng.best_bid(), 10000)
    assert_equal(eng.total_qty(SIDE_BUY), 6)
    assert_equal(eng.best_ask(), 10100)
    assert_equal(eng.total_qty(SIDE_SELL), 4)
    assert_equal(eng.position_qty(1), 14)
    assert_equal(eng.position_cash(1), -140000)
    assert_equal(eng.position_qty(2), -14)
    assert_equal(eng.position_cash(2), 140000)
    assert_true(eng.check_invariants())
    # Continuous trading resumes after the auction.
    eng.set_phase(PHASE_OPEN)
    var n2 = eng.submit(Order(7, 3, SIDE_BUY, 0, 1, TYPE_MARKET, 7000))
    assert_equal(n2, 1)
    assert_equal(eng.trades[3].price, 10100)
    assert_true(not eng.trades[3].auction)
    assert_equal(eng.position_qty(3), 1)
    assert_equal(eng.position_cash(3), -10100)
    assert_equal(eng.position_qty(2), -15)
    assert_equal(eng.position_cash(2), 150100)
    print("auction ok: clearing 10000, volume 14, remainder carried over")


def test_auction_empty_and_no_cross() raises:
    var eng = MatchingEngine()
    assert_equal(eng.clearing_price(), 0)
    assert_equal(eng.run_auction(), 0)
    var _a = eng.submit(Order(1, 1, SIDE_BUY, 9900, 5, TYPE_LIMIT, 1000))
    var _b = eng.submit(Order(2, 2, SIDE_SELL, 10100, 5, TYPE_LIMIT, 2000))
    # No overlap: bids top at 9900, asks bottom at 10100. Volume at any
    # candidate price is zero, so clearing is the bid price 9900 with
    # volume 0 and no trades; both orders carry into the book.
    var px = eng.clearing_price()
    assert_true(px == 9900 or px == 10100)
    assert_equal(eng._volume_at(px), 0)
    assert_equal(eng.run_auction(), 0)
    assert_equal(eng.best_bid(), 9900)
    assert_equal(eng.best_ask(), 10100)
    print("auction no-cross ok: no trades, orders carried into the book")


def test_validation() raises:
    var eng = MatchingEngine()
    eng.set_phase(PHASE_OPEN)
    var n1 = eng.submit(Order(1, 1, SIDE_BUY, 10000, 0, TYPE_LIMIT, 1000))
    assert_equal(n1, 0)
    assert_equal(eng.reject_reason(1), String("quantity must be positive"))
    var n2 = eng.submit(Order(2, 1, SIDE_BUY, 0, 5, TYPE_LIMIT, 2000))
    assert_equal(n2, 0)
    assert_equal(eng.reject_reason(2), String("limit price must be positive"))
    var n3 = eng.submit(Order(3, 1, SIDE_BUY, 10000, 5, 99, 3000))
    assert_equal(n3, 0)
    assert_equal(eng.reject_reason(3), String("unknown order type"))
    assert_equal(eng.order_state(999), STATE_UNKNOWN)
    assert_equal(eng.reject_reason(999), String(""))
    # Market orders are not allowed during the auction collection phase.
    var eng2 = MatchingEngine()
    var n4 = eng2.submit(Order(4, 1, SIDE_BUY, 0, 5, TYPE_MARKET, 1000))
    assert_equal(n4, 0)
    assert_equal(eng2.reject_reason(4), String("market order not allowed in auction"))
    print("validation ok: bad qty, bad price, bad type, market-in-auction")


def test_cancel_all_and_amend() raises:
    var eng = MatchingEngine()
    eng.set_phase(PHASE_OPEN)
    var _a = eng.submit(Order(1, 1, SIDE_BUY, 10000, 5, TYPE_LIMIT, 1000))
    var _b = eng.submit(Order(2, 1, SIDE_BUY, 9900, 5, TYPE_LIMIT, 2000))
    var _c = eng.submit(Order(3, 2, SIDE_SELL, 10100, 5, TYPE_LIMIT, 3000))
    assert_equal(eng.cancel_all_for_client(1), 2)
    assert_equal(eng.order_state(1), STATE_CANCELLED)
    assert_equal(eng.order_state(2), STATE_CANCELLED)
    assert_equal(eng.order_state(3), STATE_ACKED)
    assert_equal(eng.best_bid(), NO_PRICE)
    assert_equal(eng.cancel_all_for_client(1), 0)
    assert_true(not eng.cancel(999))
    assert_true(eng.amend(3, 10200, 7))
    assert_equal(eng.best_ask(), 10200)
    assert_equal(eng.total_qty(SIDE_SELL), 7)
    assert_true(not eng.amend(999, 10200, 7))
    print("cancel-all/amend ok: client sweep, amend reprices and resizes")


def test_drop_copy() raises:
    var eng = MatchingEngine()
    eng.set_symbol(String("AAPL"))
    eng.set_phase(PHASE_OPEN)
    var _a = eng.submit(Order(1, 2, SIDE_SELL, 10100, 5, TYPE_LIMIT, 1000))
    var _b = eng.submit(Order(2, 1, SIDE_BUY, 10100, 7, TYPE_LIMIT, 2000))
    var _c = eng.submit(Order(3, 2, SIDE_SELL, 0, 2, TYPE_MARKET, 3000))
    assert_equal(len(eng.reports), 2)
    assert_equal(len(eng.trades), 2)
    assert_equal(eng.reports[0].report_seq, 0)
    assert_equal(eng.reports[0].trade_seq, eng.trades[0].seq)
    assert_equal(eng.reports[0].symbol, String("AAPL"))
    assert_equal(eng.reports[0].buy_oid, 2)
    assert_equal(eng.reports[0].sell_oid, 1)
    assert_equal(eng.reports[0].price, 10100)
    assert_equal(eng.reports[0].qty, 5)
    assert_true(not eng.reports[0].auction)
    assert_equal(eng.reports[1].report_seq, 1)
    assert_equal(eng.reports[1].qty, 2)
    assert_equal(eng.reports[1].trade_seq, eng.trades[1].seq)
    var mine = eng.reports_for_client(1)
    assert_equal(len(mine), 2)
    assert_equal(eng.client_fill_qty(1), 7)
    assert_equal(eng.client_fill_notional(1), 70700)
    assert_almost_equal(eng.client_vwap(1), 10100.0, atol=1e-9)
    assert_equal(eng.client_fill_qty(2), 7)
    assert_equal(eng.client_fill_notional(2), 70700)
    assert_equal(eng.client_fill_qty(99), 0)
    assert_almost_equal(eng.client_vwap(99), 0.0, atol=1e-9)
    # Auction trades are flagged in the drop copy. Clearing price: both
    # 9900 and 10100 trade the same volume 5 and sit 100 ticks from the
    # reference 10000, so the documented tie-break (higher price) picks
    # 10100.
    var eng2 = MatchingEngine()
    eng2.set_symbol(String("MSFT"))
    eng2.set_reference_price(10000)
    var _d = eng2.submit(Order(1, 1, SIDE_BUY, 10100, 5, TYPE_LIMIT, 1000))
    var _e = eng2.submit(Order(2, 2, SIDE_SELL, 9900, 5, TYPE_LIMIT, 2000))
    var _n = eng2.run_auction()
    assert_equal(len(eng2.reports), 1)
    assert_true(eng2.reports[0].auction)
    assert_equal(eng2.reports[0].symbol, String("MSFT"))
    assert_equal(eng2.reports[0].price, 10100)
    print("drop-copy ok: reports mirror the tape, per-client VWAP exact")
    print("  " + eng.reports_summary())


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Demo: a full session -- preopen, auction, open, continuous trading.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def demo() raises:
    print("=== HFT matching engine demo (simulated data, no real market) ===")
    var eng = MatchingEngine()
    print("phase: PREOPEN -- collecting auction orders")
    var _a = eng.submit(Order(1, 1, SIDE_BUY, 10100, 5, TYPE_LIMIT, 1000))
    var _b = eng.submit(Order(2, 1, SIDE_BUY, 10000, 10, TYPE_LIMIT, 2000))
    var _c = eng.submit(Order(3, 2, SIDE_SELL, 9900, 8, TYPE_LIMIT, 3000))
    var _d = eng.submit(Order(4, 2, SIDE_SELL, 10000, 6, TYPE_LIMIT, 4000))
    print("clearing price:", eng.clearing_price())
    var n = eng.run_auction()
    print("auction printed", n, "trades")
    print(eng.tape())
    eng.set_phase(PHASE_OPEN)
    print("phase: OPEN -- continuous trading")
    var n2 = eng.submit(Order(5, 3, SIDE_BUY, 10100, 3, TYPE_LIMIT, 5000))
    print("incoming buy printed", n2, "trade(s)")
    print(eng.snapshot())
    print(eng.stats())
    print("client 1 position:", eng.position_qty(1), "cash:", eng.position_cash(1))
    print("client 2 position:", eng.position_qty(2), "cash:", eng.position_cash(2))


def main() raises:
    demo()
    print("=== matching engine tests ===")
    test_continuous_lifecycle_and_positions()
    test_ioc_fok_engine()
    test_stp_cancel_resting()
    test_stp_reject_incoming()
    test_halt()
    test_auction()
    test_auction_empty_and_no_cross()
    test_validation()
    test_cancel_all_and_amend()
    test_drop_copy()
    print("ALL MATCHING ENGINE TESTS PASSED")

--- SOURCE: mojo_samples/hft_order_book.mojo ---
# hft_order_book.mojo
#
# Original code written for the START project's Mojo code samples.
# Component 3 (HFT FRAMEWORKS): a limit order book with price-time priority.
#
# SIMULATOR ONLY. This file does not connect to any exchange, broker, or
# market-data vendor, and it never moves real money. Every order, trade, and
# price below is invented inside this program for teaching and benchmarking.
# Prices are integer ticks (1 tick = 1 cent) so matching arithmetic is exact
# and never suffers floating-point rounding. Quantities are whole units.
#
# Standard library only. No external packages, no Python interop, no network.
#
# Design notes (the ideas a real book uses, rebuilt from scratch here):
#   * The book keeps two flat lists, bids and asks. Bids are sorted by price
#     descending, asks by price ascending; within one price, orders sit in
#     arrival (sequence) order. That ordering IS price-time priority: the best
#     price is always element 0 of its side, and the oldest order at that
#     price is always first in line.
#   * A new limit order first trades against the opposite side ("takes
#     liquidity") and only the unfilled remainder rests in the book ("makes
#     liquidity"). A market order only takes; whatever it cannot fill is
#     discarded, it never rests.
#   * A trade always prints at the price of the resting order, never at the
#     incoming order's limit. That is how a real continuous book works: the
#     aggressor pays the price the maker asked for.
#   * IOC (immediate-or-cancel) is a limit order whose remainder is cancelled
#     instead of resting. FOK (fill-or-kill) is all-or-nothing: if the book
#     cannot fill the whole quantity at acceptable prices, nothing trades.
#   * Every order carries a monotonically increasing sequence number assigned
#     by the book. Sequence numbers are the book's clock: they define time
#     priority, they make the book deterministic, and they let a replay
#     reproduce an identical book from an identical event log.
#   * The book exposes its own invariant checker. After any operation the
#     book must be sorted, must contain no empty or negative quantities, and
#     must not be crossed (best bid strictly below best ask). The tests below
#     call the checker after every scenario.
#
# What this file demonstrates (run it: it prints a worked book and then runs
# its own checks; exit code 0 means every check passed):
#   1. Limit, market, IOC and FOK orders, with hand-computed fills.
#   2. Price-time priority: two orders at the same price fill oldest first.
#   3. A market order sweeping several price levels, with exact VWAP.
#   4. Cancel and amend (amend loses time priority, as on a real venue).
#   5. Depth, spread, mid-price, imbalance and VWAP queries.
#   6. Volume conservation: traded + resting + cancelled == added.
#
# Hand-computed cases used in the tests (ticks; 10000 = $100.00):
#   Case A: asks 10100 x5, 10200 x5. Buy limit 10100 x7 -> 1 trade, 5 @10100,
#           remainder 2 rests as the new best bid at 10100.
#   Case B: bids 10000 x5 (old), 10000 x5 (young). Sell market x7 -> fills
#           5 against the old order, 2 against the young one; young keeps 3.
#   Case C: asks 10100 x3, 10200 x4, 10300 x5. Buy market x10 -> fills
#           3 @10100, 4 @10200, 3 @10300; cost 102000, VWAP 10200.
#   Case D: ask 10100 x2. Buy IOC 10100 x5 -> fills 2, remainder 3 cancelled,
#           book has no resting bid afterwards.
#   Case E: ask 10100 x2. Buy FOK 10100 x5 -> rejected whole, book untouched;
#           then buy FOK 10100 x2 -> fills 2, ask gone.
#   Case F: bid 10000 x10, cancel it, re-add, amend price to 9900 -> best
#           bid becomes 9900 and the order carries a fresh sequence number.
#   Case G: market order into an empty book -> zero trades, nothing rests.
#   Case H: a built-up book whose depth, totals, spread, mid-price and
#           imbalance are all asserted against hand-computed values.
#
# Icebergs (TYPE_ICEBERG, add_iceberg): only `peak` units show at the
# price level. A fill consumes the display; when the display is
# exhausted and hidden quantity remains, a fresh peak is displayed and
# the order goes to the BACK of its price level with a new sequence
# number -- the display refresh costs time priority, which is the
# price of hiding size. depth_at() counts total resting quantity
# (hidden included); visible_depth_at() counts only what is displayed.
#   Case I: ask iceberg 10100 total 100, peak 10. Buy limit 10100 x25
#           -> 3 trades (10, 10, 5); iceberg rests with 75 hidden and
#           5 displayed.
#   Case J: iceberg 10100 total 30 peak 10, then a plain ask 10100 x5
#           behind it. Buy 12 -> 10 from the iceberg (which refreshes
#           to the back), then 2 from the plain ask. The iceberg's
#           refresh cost it its place in line.
#   Case K: an iceberg that never trades shows peak in visible depth
#           and its full size in total depth; a peak >= qty behaves as
#           a plain limit order.

from std.testing import assert_equal, assert_true, assert_almost_equal
from std.builtin.sort import sort

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Constants. Sides, order types and reject reasons are plain integers so the
# book stays allocation-free on its hot path; the names below are the only
# place the numbers are written down.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

comptime SIDE_BUY = 0
comptime SIDE_SELL = 1

comptime TYPE_LIMIT = 0
comptime TYPE_MARKET = 1
comptime TYPE_IOC = 2
comptime TYPE_FOK = 3
comptime TYPE_ICEBERG = 4

comptime NO_PRICE = -1


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Order: one instruction sitting in (or arriving at) the book.
#
#   oid        unique order id, assigned by the caller / matching engine.
#   client_id  who sent it; used by the matching engine for self-trade
#              prevention and by the risk engine for per-client limits.
#   side       SIDE_BUY or SIDE_SELL.
#   price      limit price in ticks. Ignored for market orders (0 = no limit).
#   qty        original quantity.
#   remaining  quantity still unfilled; 0 means the order is done.
#   seq        book-assigned arrival sequence; defines time priority.
#   otype      TYPE_LIMIT / TYPE_MARKET / TYPE_IOC / TYPE_FOK / TYPE_ICEBERG.
#   peak       iceberg display size (0 = not an iceberg: the whole order
#              is visible). See the iceberg section below.
#   visible    quantity currently displayed at the price level. For a
#              plain order visible == remaining; for an iceberg only the
#              peak shows, and the rest is hidden until the display is
#              exhausted and refreshed.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

struct Order:
    var oid: Int
    var client_id: Int
    var side: Int
    var price: Int
    var qty: Int
    var remaining: Int
    var seq: Int
    var otype: Int
    var peak: Int
    var visible: Int

    def __init__(
        out self,
        oid: Int,
        client_id: Int,
        side: Int,
        price: Int,
        qty: Int,
        otype: Int,
        peak: Int = 0,
    ):
        self.oid = oid
        self.client_id = client_id
        self.side = side
        self.price = price
        self.qty = qty
        self.remaining = qty
        self.seq = 0
        self.otype = otype
        self.peak = peak
        if peak > 0 and peak < qty:
            self.visible = peak
        else:
            self.visible = qty

    def is_buy(self) -> Bool:
        return self.side == SIDE_BUY

    def is_done(self) -> Bool:
        return self.remaining <= 0

    def describe(self) -> String:
        var side_s = String("SELL")
        if self.side == SIDE_BUY:
            side_s = String("BUY ")
        var type_s = String("LIMIT")
        if self.otype == TYPE_MARKET:
            type_s = String("MARKET")
        elif self.otype == TYPE_IOC:
            type_s = String("IOC")
        elif self.otype == TYPE_FOK:
            type_s = String("FOK")
        return (
            String("oid=")
            + String(self.oid)
            + String(" ")
            + side_s
            + String(" ")
            + type_s
            + String(" px=")
            + String(self.price)
            + String(" qty=")
            + String(self.qty)
            + String(" rem=")
            + String(self.remaining)
            + String(" seq=")
            + String(self.seq)
        )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Trade: one fill. buy_oid / sell_oid name the two orders; price is the
# resting order's price; qty is the filled quantity; seq orders the tape.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

struct Trade:
    var buy_oid: Int
    var sell_oid: Int
    var price: Int
    var qty: Int
    var seq: Int

    def __init__(out self, buy_oid: Int, sell_oid: Int, price: Int, qty: Int, seq: Int):
        self.buy_oid = buy_oid
        self.sell_oid = sell_oid
        self.price = price
        self.qty = qty
        self.seq = seq

    def notional(self) -> Int:
        return self.price * self.qty

    def describe(self) -> String:
        return (
            String("trade seq=")
            + String(self.seq)
            + String(" buy_oid=")
            + String(self.buy_oid)
            + String(" sell_oid=")
            + String(self.sell_oid)
            + String(" px=")
            + String(self.price)
            + String(" qty=")
            + String(self.qty)
        )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# OrderBook.
#
# Invariants (checked by check_invariants):
#   * bids sorted by price descending, then seq ascending.
#   * asks sorted by price ascending, then seq ascending.
#   * every resting order has remaining > 0 and price > 0.
#   * best bid < best ask whenever both sides are non-empty (never crossed).
#   * sequence numbers strictly increase along each side's list.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

struct OrderBook:
    var bids: List[Order]
    var asks: List[Order]
    var trades: List[Trade]
    var next_seq: Int
    var total_volume: Int
    var total_notional: Int
    var last_trade_price: Int
    var orders_added: Int
    var orders_cancelled: Int
    var last_fok_ok: Bool

    def __init__(out self):
        self.bids = List[Order]()
        self.asks = List[Order]()
        self.trades = List[Trade]()
        self.next_seq = 1
        self.total_volume = 0
        self.total_notional = 0
        self.last_trade_price = 0
        self.orders_added = 0
        self.orders_cancelled = 0
        self.last_fok_ok = True

    # -- sequence ----------------------------------------------------------
    def _take_seq(mut self) -> Int:
        var s = self.next_seq
        self.next_seq += 1
        return s

    # -- sorted insertion --------------------------------------------------
    # Bids: insert before the first bid priced below the new order. Same
    # price orders stay ahead (they arrived earlier), which is time priority.
    def _insert_bid(mut self, var order: Order):
        var pos = len(self.bids)
        var i = 0
        while i < len(self.bids):
            if self.bids[i].price < order.price:
                pos = i
                break
            i += 1
        self.bids.insert(pos, order^)

    # Asks: insert before the first ask priced above the new order.
    def _insert_ask(mut self, var order: Order):
        var pos = len(self.asks)
        var i = 0
        while i < len(self.asks):
            if self.asks[i].price > order.price:
                pos = i
                break
            i += 1
        self.asks.insert(pos, order^)

    # -- tape --------------------------------------------------------------
    def _record_trade(mut self, buy_oid: Int, sell_oid: Int, price: Int, qty: Int):
        var t = Trade(buy_oid, sell_oid, price, qty, self._take_seq())
        self.trades.append(t^)
        self.total_volume += qty
        self.total_notional += price * qty
        self.last_trade_price = price

    # -- matching ----------------------------------------------------------
    # Match an incoming buy against the asks. Trades print at the resting
    # ask's price. Returns the number of trades printed.
    def _match_buy(mut self, mut order: Order, limit_price: Int) -> Int:
        var n_trades = 0
        while (
            order.remaining > 0
            and len(self.asks) > 0
            and self.asks[0].price <= limit_price
        ):
            var fill = order.remaining
            if self.asks[0].visible < fill:
                fill = self.asks[0].visible
            var px = self.asks[0].price
            var resting_oid = self.asks[0].oid
            self.asks[0].remaining -= fill
            self.asks[0].visible -= fill
            order.remaining -= fill
            self._record_trade(order.oid, resting_oid, px, fill)
            n_trades += 1
            if self.asks[0].remaining == 0:
                var _gone = self.asks.pop(0)
            elif self.asks[0].visible == 0:
                # Iceberg refresh: display a new peak and go to the back
                # of the price level (fresh sequence = lost priority).
                var refreshed = self.asks.pop(0)
                refreshed.visible = refreshed.peak
                if refreshed.visible > refreshed.remaining:
                    refreshed.visible = refreshed.remaining
                refreshed.seq = self._take_seq()
                self._insert_ask(refreshed^)
        return n_trades

    # Match an incoming sell against the bids. Trades print at the resting
    # bid's price.
    def _match_sell(mut self, mut order: Order, limit_price: Int) -> Int:
        var n_trades = 0
        while (
            order.remaining > 0
            and len(self.bids) > 0
            and self.bids[0].price >= limit_price
        ):
            var fill = order.remaining
            if self.bids[0].visible < fill:
                fill = self.bids[0].visible
            var px = self.bids[0].price
            var resting_oid = self.bids[0].oid
            self.bids[0].remaining -= fill
            self.bids[0].visible -= fill
            order.remaining -= fill
            self._record_trade(resting_oid, order.oid, px, fill)
            n_trades += 1
            if self.bids[0].remaining == 0:
                var _gone = self.bids.pop(0)
            elif self.bids[0].visible == 0:
                var refreshed = self.bids.pop(0)
                refreshed.visible = refreshed.peak
                if refreshed.visible > refreshed.remaining:
                    refreshed.visible = refreshed.remaining
                refreshed.seq = self._take_seq()
                self._insert_bid(refreshed^)
        return n_trades

    # How much is available on the opposite side at prices the incoming
    # order would accept? Used by FOK: the whole quantity must be there.
    def _available(self, side: Int, limit_price: Int) -> Int:
        var total = 0
        if side == SIDE_BUY:
            var i = 0
            while i < len(self.asks):
                if self.asks[i].price > limit_price:
                    break
                total += self.asks[i].remaining
                i += 1
        else:
            var i = 0
            while i < len(self.bids):
                if self.bids[i].price < limit_price:
                    break
                total += self.bids[i].remaining
                i += 1
        return total

    # -- public order entry ------------------------------------------------
    # Limit order: match what can be matched, rest the remainder. The
    # displayed quantity is re-derived before resting: an incoming
    # order's visible field is stale after matching (it was the
    # aggressor, so its own display was never drawn down), and a
    # resting order must always satisfy visible == min(peak, remaining).
    def add_limit(mut self, var order: Order) -> Int:
        order.seq = self._take_seq()
        self.orders_added += 1
        var n = 0
        if order.side == SIDE_BUY:
            n = self._match_buy(order, order.price)
            if order.remaining > 0:
                if order.peak > 0 and order.peak < order.remaining:
                    order.visible = order.peak
                else:
                    order.visible = order.remaining
                self._insert_bid(order^)
        else:
            n = self._match_sell(order, order.price)
            if order.remaining > 0:
                if order.peak > 0 and order.peak < order.remaining:
                    order.visible = order.peak
                else:
                    order.visible = order.remaining
                self._insert_ask(order^)
        return n

    # Market order: match at any price; the unfilled remainder is discarded.
    # A limit price of 0 is used internally to mean "no price limit".
    def add_market(mut self, var order: Order) -> Int:
        order.seq = self._take_seq()
        self.orders_added += 1
        var n = 0
        if order.side == SIDE_BUY:
            # No ask can be priced above "infinity"; use a huge limit.
            n = self._match_buy(order, 1_000_000_000)
        else:
            n = self._match_sell(order, 0)
        return n

    # IOC: like a limit order, except the remainder is cancelled.
    def add_ioc(mut self, var order: Order) -> Int:
        order.seq = self._take_seq()
        self.orders_added += 1
        var n = 0
        if order.side == SIDE_BUY:
            n = self._match_buy(order, order.price)
        else:
            n = self._match_sell(order, order.price)
        if order.remaining > 0:
            self.orders_cancelled += 1
        return n

    # FOK: all or nothing. Sets last_fok_ok so the caller can tell a
    # rejection (False, 0 trades) from a genuine zero fill.
    def add_fok(mut self, var order: Order) -> Int:
        order.seq = self._take_seq()
        self.orders_added += 1
        var avail = self._available(order.side, order.price)
        if avail < order.qty:
            self.last_fok_ok = False
            self.orders_cancelled += 1
            return 0
        self.last_fok_ok = True
        var n = 0
        if order.side == SIDE_BUY:
            n = self._match_buy(order, order.price)
        else:
            n = self._match_sell(order, order.price)
        return n

    # Iceberg order: only `peak` units show at the price level; the rest
    # is hidden and refreshes (at the back of the queue) as the display
    # is consumed. A peak of 0, or a peak >= qty, is just a limit order.
    def add_iceberg(mut self, var order: Order, peak: Int) -> Int:
        if peak <= 0 or peak >= order.qty:
            return self.add_limit(order^)
        order.peak = peak
        order.visible = peak
        order.otype = TYPE_ICEBERG
        return self.add_limit(order^)

    # Cancel by order id. Returns True if an order was actually removed.
    def cancel(mut self, oid: Int) -> Bool:
        var i = 0
        while i < len(self.bids):
            if self.bids[i].oid == oid:
                var _gone = self.bids.pop(i)
                self.orders_cancelled += 1
                return True
            i += 1
        var j = 0
        while j < len(self.asks):
            if self.asks[j].oid == oid:
                var _gone = self.asks.pop(j)
                self.orders_cancelled += 1
                return True
            j += 1
        return False

    # Amend: cancel the old order and re-enter it at a new price / quantity.
    # The amended order gets a fresh sequence number, so it goes to the back
    # of its price level -- exactly what a real venue does, and exactly why
    # amending to jump the queue does not work.
    def amend(mut self, oid: Int, new_price: Int, new_qty: Int) -> Bool:
        var side = NO_PRICE
        var client_id = 0
        var otype = TYPE_LIMIT
        var i = 0
        while i < len(self.bids):
            if self.bids[i].oid == oid:
                side = SIDE_BUY
                client_id = self.bids[i].client_id
                otype = self.bids[i].otype
                var _gone = self.bids.pop(i)
                break
            i += 1
        if side == NO_PRICE:
            var j = 0
            while j < len(self.asks):
                if self.asks[j].oid == oid:
                    side = SIDE_SELL
                    client_id = self.asks[j].client_id
                    otype = self.asks[j].otype
                    var _gone = self.asks.pop(j)
                    break
                j += 1
        if side == NO_PRICE:
            return False
        self.orders_cancelled += 1
        var replacement = Order(oid, client_id, side, new_price, new_qty, otype)
        var _n = self.add_limit(replacement^)
        return True

    # -- queries -----------------------------------------------------------
    def best_bid(self) -> Int:
        if len(self.bids) == 0:
            return NO_PRICE
        return self.bids[0].price

    def best_ask(self) -> Int:
        if len(self.asks) == 0:
            return NO_PRICE
        return self.asks[0].price

    def best_bid_qty(self) -> Int:
        if len(self.bids) == 0:
            return 0
        return self.bids[0].remaining

    def best_ask_qty(self) -> Int:
        if len(self.asks) == 0:
            return 0
        return self.asks[0].remaining

    def spread(self) -> Int:
        if len(self.bids) == 0 or len(self.asks) == 0:
            return NO_PRICE
        return self.asks[0].price - self.bids[0].price

    def mid_price(self) -> Float64:
        if len(self.bids) == 0 or len(self.asks) == 0:
            return 0.0
        return (Float64(self.bids[0].price) + Float64(self.asks[0].price)) / 2.0

    def total_qty(self, side: Int) -> Int:
        var total = 0
        if side == SIDE_BUY:
            var i = 0
            while i < len(self.bids):
                total += self.bids[i].remaining
                i += 1
        else:
            var i = 0
            while i < len(self.asks):
                total += self.asks[i].remaining
                i += 1
        return total

    # Quantity resting exactly at one price on one side.
    def depth_at(self, side: Int, price: Int) -> Int:
        var total = 0
        if side == SIDE_BUY:
            var i = 0
            while i < len(self.bids):
                if self.bids[i].price == price:
                    total += self.bids[i].remaining
                i += 1
        else:
            var i = 0
            while i < len(self.asks):
                if self.asks[i].price == price:
                    total += self.asks[i].remaining
                i += 1
        return total

    # Displayed quantity at one price: what the market can see. For a
    # plain order this equals depth_at; for an iceberg it is only the
    # peak (or the remainder, once the hidden part is nearly gone).
    def visible_depth_at(self, side: Int, price: Int) -> Int:
        var total = 0
        if side == SIDE_BUY:
            var i = 0
            while i < len(self.bids):
                if self.bids[i].price == price:
                    total += self.bids[i].visible
                i += 1
        else:
            var i = 0
            while i < len(self.asks):
                if self.asks[i].price == price:
                    total += self.asks[i].visible
                i += 1
        return total

    def visible_total_qty(self, side: Int) -> Int:
        var total = 0
        if side == SIDE_BUY:
            var i = 0
            while i < len(self.bids):
                total += self.bids[i].visible
                i += 1
        else:
            var i = 0
            while i < len(self.asks):
                total += self.asks[i].visible
                i += 1
        return total

    # Number of distinct price levels on a side.
    def n_levels(self, side: Int) -> Int:
        var count = 0
        var last_seen = NO_PRICE
        if side == SIDE_BUY:
            var i = 0
            while i < len(self.bids):
                if self.bids[i].price != last_seen:
                    count += 1
                    last_seen = self.bids[i].price
                i += 1
        else:
            var i = 0
            while i < len(self.asks):
                if self.asks[i].price != last_seen:
                    count += 1
                    last_seen = self.asks[i].price
                i += 1
        return count

    # Book imbalance in [-1, 1]: +1 means all resting quantity is bids,
    # -1 means all resting quantity is asks, 0 means balanced.
    def imbalance(self) -> Float64:
        var b = self.total_qty(SIDE_BUY)
        var a = self.total_qty(SIDE_SELL)
        if b + a == 0:
            return 0.0
        return Float64(b - a) / Float64(b + a)

    # Volume-weighted average price of the resting orders on one side,
    # optionally limited to the top `levels` price levels (0 = all levels).
    def vwap(self, side: Int, levels: Int) -> Float64:
        var notional: Int = 0
        var qty: Int = 0
        var seen_levels = 0
        var last_price = NO_PRICE
        if side == SIDE_BUY:
            var i = 0
            while i < len(self.bids):
                if self.bids[i].price != last_price:
                    seen_levels += 1
                    last_price = self.bids[i].price
                    if levels > 0 and seen_levels > levels:
                        break
                notional += self.bids[i].price * self.bids[i].remaining
                qty += self.bids[i].remaining
                i += 1
        else:
            var i = 0
            while i < len(self.asks):
                if self.asks[i].price != last_price:
                    seen_levels += 1
                    last_price = self.asks[i].price
                    if levels > 0 and seen_levels > levels:
                        break
                notional += self.asks[i].price * self.asks[i].remaining
                qty += self.asks[i].remaining
                i += 1
        if qty == 0:
            return 0.0
        return Float64(notional) / Float64(qty)

    # VWAP of the trades printed so far (the tape's average fill price).
    def tape_vwap(self) -> Float64:
        if self.total_volume == 0:
            return 0.0
        return Float64(self.total_notional) / Float64(self.total_volume)

    # Find a resting order's remaining quantity by id; -1 if not resting.
    def resting_qty(self, oid: Int) -> Int:
        var i = 0
        while i < len(self.bids):
            if self.bids[i].oid == oid:
                return self.bids[i].remaining
            i += 1
        var j = 0
        while j < len(self.asks):
            if self.asks[j].oid == oid:
                return self.asks[j].remaining
            j += 1
        return -1

    # -- invariants --------------------------------------------------------
    # Returns True only if every structural promise of the book holds.
    def check_invariants(self) -> Bool:
        var i = 0
        while i < len(self.bids):
            if self.bids[i].remaining <= 0 or self.bids[i].price <= 0:
                return False
            if self.bids[i].visible <= 0 or self.bids[i].visible > self.bids[i].remaining:
                return False
            if i + 1 < len(self.bids):
                if self.bids[i].price < self.bids[i + 1].price:
                    return False
                if self.bids[i].price == self.bids[i + 1].price:
                    if self.bids[i].seq >= self.bids[i + 1].seq:
                        return False
            i += 1
        var j = 0
        while j < len(self.asks):
            if self.asks[j].remaining <= 0 or self.asks[j].price <= 0:
                return False
            if self.asks[j].visible <= 0 or self.asks[j].visible > self.asks[j].remaining:
                return False
            if j + 1 < len(self.asks):
                if self.asks[j].price > self.asks[j + 1].price:
                    return False
                if self.asks[j].price == self.asks[j + 1].price:
                    if self.asks[j].seq >= self.asks[j + 1].seq:
                        return False
            j += 1
        if len(self.bids) > 0 and len(self.asks) > 0:
            if self.bids[0].price >= self.asks[0].price:
                return False
        return True

    # -- display -----------------------------------------------------------
    def snapshot(self) -> String:
        var s = String("--- order book ---\n")
        s = s + String("asks (best last):\n")
        var i = len(self.asks) - 1
        while i >= 0:
            s = (
                s
                + String("  ")
                + String(self.asks[i].price)
                + String(" x ")
                + String(self.asks[i].remaining)
                + String("  oid=")
                + String(self.asks[i].oid)
                + String("\n")
            )
            i -= 1
        s = s + String("  ---- spread ")
        if self.spread() == NO_PRICE:
            s = s + String("n/a")
        else:
            s = s + String(self.spread())
        s = s + String(" ----\n")
        var j = 0
        while j < len(self.bids):
            s = (
                s
                + String("  ")
                + String(self.bids[j].price)
                + String(" x ")
                + String(self.bids[j].remaining)
                + String("  oid=")
                + String(self.bids[j].oid)
                + String("\n")
            )
            j += 1
        s = s + String("bids (best first)\n")
        s = (
            s
            + String("trades: ")
            + String(len(self.trades))
            + String(" volume: ")
            + String(self.total_volume)
            + String(" tape vwap: ")
            + String(self.tape_vwap())
            + String("\n")
        )
        return s

    def tape(self) -> String:
        var s = String("--- trade tape ---\n")
        var i = 0
        while i < len(self.trades):
            s = s + self.trades[i].describe() + String("\n")
            i += 1
        return s

    def serialise(self) -> String:
        var s = String("BOOK|v1|")
        s = s + String(self.next_seq)
        s = s + String("|") + String(self.total_volume)
        s = s + String("|") + String(self.total_notional)
        s = s + String("|") + String(self.last_trade_price)
        s = s + String("|") + String(self.orders_added)
        s = s + String("|") + String(self.orders_cancelled)
        s = s + String("\n")
        var i = 0
        while i < len(self.bids):
            s = (
                s + String("O|B|")
                + String(self.bids[i].oid)
                + String("|") + String(self.bids[i].client_id)
                + String("|") + String(self.bids[i].price)
                + String("|") + String(self.bids[i].qty)
                + String("|") + String(self.bids[i].remaining)
                + String("|") + String(self.bids[i].seq)
                + String("|") + String(self.bids[i].otype)
                + String("|") + String(self.bids[i].peak)
                + String("|") + String(self.bids[i].visible)
                + String("\n")
            )
            i += 1
        var j = 0
        while j < len(self.asks):
            s = (
                s + String("O|A|")
                + String(self.asks[j].oid)
                + String("|") + String(self.asks[j].client_id)
                + String("|") + String(self.asks[j].price)
                + String("|") + String(self.asks[j].qty)
                + String("|") + String(self.asks[j].remaining)
                + String("|") + String(self.asks[j].seq)
                + String("|") + String(self.asks[j].otype)
                + String("|") + String(self.asks[j].peak)
                + String("|") + String(self.asks[j].visible)
                + String("\n")
            )
            j += 1
        var k = 0
        while k < len(self.trades):
            s = (
                s + String("T|")
                + String(self.trades[k].buy_oid)
                + String("|") + String(self.trades[k].sell_oid)
                + String("|") + String(self.trades[k].price)
                + String("|") + String(self.trades[k].qty)
                + String("|") + String(self.trades[k].seq)
                + String("\n")
            )
            k += 1
        return s

    # Rebuild the book from serialise() output. Returns False and leaves
    # the book untouched when the text fails validation.
    def restore(mut self, text: String) raises -> Bool:
        var lines = text.split("\n")
        if len(lines) == 0:
            return False
        var head = lines[0].split("|")
        if len(head) != 8:
            return False
        if String(head[0]) != "BOOK" or String(head[1]) != "v1":
            return False
        var h = 2
        while h < 8:
            if not _all_digits(String(head[h])):
                return False
            h += 1
        var new_bids = List[Order]()
        var new_asks = List[Order]()
        var new_trades = List[Trade]()
        var seen = Dict[Int, Bool]()
        var max_seq = 0
        var li = 1
        while li < len(lines):
            var line = String(lines[li])
            li += 1
            if line.byte_length() == 0:
                continue
            var parts = line.split("|")
            var tag = String(parts[0])
            if tag == "O":
                if len(parts) != 11:
                    return False
                var f = 2
                while f < 11:
                    if not _all_digits(String(parts[f])):
                        return False
                    f += 1
                var side = String(parts[1])
                if side != "B" and side != "A":
                    return False
                var oid = Int(String(parts[2]))
                var client = Int(String(parts[3]))
                var price = Int(String(parts[4]))
                var qty = Int(String(parts[5]))
                var remaining = Int(String(parts[6]))
                var seq = Int(String(parts[7]))
                var otype = Int(String(parts[8]))
                var peak = Int(String(parts[9]))
                var visible = Int(String(parts[10]))
                if price < 1 or qty < 1:
                    return False
                if remaining < 1 or remaining > qty:
                    return False
                if seq < 1 or otype < 0 or otype > 4:
                    return False
                if peak < 0 or visible < 1 or visible > remaining:
                    return False
                if peak > 0 and visible > peak:
                    return False
                if seen.get(oid, False):
                    return False
                seen[oid] = True
                if seq > max_seq:
                    max_seq = seq
                var o = Order(oid, client, SIDE_BUY, price, qty, otype, peak)
                if side == "A":
                    o.side = SIDE_SELL
                o.remaining = remaining
                o.seq = seq
                o.visible = visible
                if side == "B":
                    new_bids.append(o^)
                else:
                    new_asks.append(o^)
            elif tag == "T":
                if len(parts) != 6:
                    return False
                var g = 1
                while g < 6:
                    if not _all_digits(String(parts[g])):
                        return False
                    g += 1
                var t = Trade(
                    Int(String(parts[1])),
                    Int(String(parts[2])),
                    Int(String(parts[3])),
                    Int(String(parts[4])),
                    Int(String(parts[5])),
                )
                if t.seq > max_seq:
                    max_seq = t.seq
                new_trades.append(t^)
            else:
                return False
        var hdr_next_seq = Int(String(head[2]))
        if hdr_next_seq < 1:
            return False
        var new_next_seq = hdr_next_seq
        if max_seq + 1 > new_next_seq:
            new_next_seq = max_seq + 1
        self.bids = new_bids^
        self.asks = new_asks^
        self.trades = new_trades^
        self.next_seq = new_next_seq
        self.total_volume = Int(String(head[3]))
        self.total_notional = Int(String(head[4]))
        self.last_trade_price = Int(String(head[5]))
        self.orders_added = Int(String(head[6]))
        self.orders_cancelled = Int(String(head[7]))
        return True

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Serialisation: the whole book as text, and back again.
#
# A book that only lives in memory dies with the process. `serialise()`
# writes the book -- header counters, every resting order with its
# sequence number and iceberg state, and the trade tape -- as lines of
# pipe-separated fields:
#
#   BOOK|v1|<next_seq>|<total_volume>|<total_notional>|<last_trade_price>
#        |<orders_added>|<orders_cancelled>
#   O|B|oid|client|price|qty|remaining|seq|otype|peak|visible
#   T|buy_oid|sell_oid|price|qty|seq
#
# `restore()` reads that text back into a book. It parses into temporary
# lists first and only swaps them in once every line has validated, so a
# corrupt snapshot is rejected whole and the book it was aimed at is
# left exactly as it was. Rejection reasons: bad version, wrong field
# count, a non-numeric field, an out-of-range value (price 0, remaining
# above qty, a visible quantity above what remains, an unknown order
# type), or the same order id twice. Restoring replaces the book's
# contents; it is how a crashed book is rebuilt from its last snapshot,
# and how a book is moved between processes.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _all_digits(s: String) -> Bool:
    var n = s.byte_length()
    if n == 0:
        return False
    var seen_digit = False
    var i = 0
    for b in s.bytes():
        if i == 0 and b == 45 and n > 1:
            i += 1
            continue
        if b < 48 or b > 57:
            return False
        seen_digit = True
        i += 1
    return seen_digit


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Tests. Every case below is hand-computed in the header comment; the
# assertions are the proof. Each test builds a fresh book so cases cannot
# leak state into each other.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def test_case_a_basic_cross() raises:
    # Asks 10100 x5, 10200 x5. Buy limit 10100 x7 -> 1 trade of 5 @10100,
    # remainder 2 rests as best bid at 10100.
    var book = OrderBook()
