    var _a = book.add_limit(Order(1, 11, SIDE_SELL, 10100, 5, TYPE_LIMIT))
    var _b = book.add_limit(Order(2, 11, SIDE_SELL, 10200, 5, TYPE_LIMIT))
    var n = book.add_limit(Order(3, 22, SIDE_BUY, 10100, 7, TYPE_LIMIT))
    assert_equal(n, 1)
    assert_equal(len(book.trades), 1)
    assert_equal(book.trades[0].price, 10100)
    assert_equal(book.trades[0].qty, 5)
    assert_equal(book.trades[0].buy_oid, 3)
    assert_equal(book.trades[0].sell_oid, 1)
    assert_equal(book.best_bid(), 10100)
    assert_equal(book.best_bid_qty(), 2)
    assert_equal(book.best_ask(), 10200)
    assert_equal(book.best_ask_qty(), 5)
    assert_equal(book.resting_qty(1), -1)
    assert_equal(book.resting_qty(3), 2)
    assert_true(book.check_invariants())
    print("case A ok: basic cross, remainder rests")


def test_case_b_price_time_priority() raises:
    # Two bids at the same price: the older one fills first.
    var book = OrderBook()
    var _a = book.add_limit(Order(10, 11, SIDE_BUY, 10000, 5, TYPE_LIMIT))
    var _b = book.add_limit(Order(11, 11, SIDE_BUY, 10000, 5, TYPE_LIMIT))
    var n = book.add_market(Order(12, 22, SIDE_SELL, 0, 7, TYPE_MARKET))
    assert_equal(n, 2)
    assert_equal(book.trades[0].buy_oid, 10)
    assert_equal(book.trades[0].qty, 5)
    assert_equal(book.trades[1].buy_oid, 11)
    assert_equal(book.trades[1].qty, 2)
    assert_equal(book.trades[0].price, 10000)
    assert_equal(book.trades[1].price, 10000)
    assert_equal(book.resting_qty(10), -1)
    assert_equal(book.resting_qty(11), 3)
    assert_equal(book.best_bid_qty(), 3)
    assert_true(book.check_invariants())
    print("case B ok: price-time priority, oldest first")


def test_case_c_market_sweep_vwap() raises:
    # Asks 10100 x3, 10200 x4, 10300 x5. Buy market x10 fills
    # 3 @10100, 4 @10200, 3 @10300. Cost 102000, VWAP 10200.
    var book = OrderBook()
    var _a = book.add_limit(Order(1, 11, SIDE_SELL, 10100, 3, TYPE_LIMIT))
    var _b = book.add_limit(Order(2, 11, SIDE_SELL, 10200, 4, TYPE_LIMIT))
    var _c = book.add_limit(Order(3, 11, SIDE_SELL, 10300, 5, TYPE_LIMIT))
    var n = book.add_market(Order(4, 22, SIDE_BUY, 0, 10, TYPE_MARKET))
    assert_equal(n, 3)
    assert_equal(book.trades[0].price, 10100)
    assert_equal(book.trades[0].qty, 3)
    assert_equal(book.trades[1].price, 10200)
    assert_equal(book.trades[1].qty, 4)
    assert_equal(book.trades[2].price, 10300)
    assert_equal(book.trades[2].qty, 3)
    assert_equal(book.total_volume, 10)
    assert_equal(book.total_notional, 102000)
    assert_almost_equal(book.tape_vwap(), 10200.0, atol=1e-9)
    assert_equal(book.resting_qty(3), 2)
    assert_equal(book.best_ask(), 10300)
    assert_true(book.check_invariants())
    print("case C ok: market sweep across levels, VWAP 10200")


def test_case_d_ioc_partial() raises:
    # Ask 10100 x2. Buy IOC 10100 x5 fills 2, remainder 3 is cancelled.
    var book = OrderBook()
    var _a = book.add_limit(Order(1, 11, SIDE_SELL, 10100, 2, TYPE_LIMIT))
    var n = book.add_ioc(Order(2, 22, SIDE_BUY, 10100, 5, TYPE_IOC))
    assert_equal(n, 1)
    assert_equal(book.trades[0].qty, 2)
    assert_equal(book.best_bid(), NO_PRICE)
    assert_equal(book.best_ask(), NO_PRICE)
    assert_equal(book.total_qty(SIDE_BUY), 0)
    assert_equal(book.orders_cancelled, 1)
    assert_true(book.check_invariants())
    print("case D ok: IOC partial fill, remainder cancelled")


def test_case_e_fok() raises:
    # Ask 10100 x2. Buy FOK 10100 x5 is rejected whole (book untouched),
    # then buy FOK 10100 x2 fills completely.
    var book = OrderBook()
    var _a = book.add_limit(Order(1, 11, SIDE_SELL, 10100, 2, TYPE_LIMIT))
    var n1 = book.add_fok(Order(2, 22, SIDE_BUY, 10100, 5, TYPE_FOK))
    assert_equal(n1, 0)
    assert_true(not book.last_fok_ok)
    assert_equal(len(book.trades), 0)
    assert_equal(book.best_ask(), 10100)
    assert_equal(book.best_ask_qty(), 2)
    var n2 = book.add_fok(Order(3, 22, SIDE_BUY, 10100, 2, TYPE_FOK))
    assert_equal(n2, 1)
    assert_true(book.last_fok_ok)
    assert_equal(book.trades[0].qty, 2)
    assert_equal(book.best_ask(), NO_PRICE)
    assert_true(book.check_invariants())
    print("case E ok: FOK rejected whole, then filled whole")


def test_case_f_cancel_amend() raises:
    # Bid 10000 x10: cancel it, re-add, amend price to 9900. The amended
    # order is best bid at 9900 with a fresh (larger) sequence number.
    var book = OrderBook()
    var _a = book.add_limit(Order(20, 11, SIDE_BUY, 10000, 10, TYPE_LIMIT))
    assert_true(book.cancel(20))
    assert_equal(book.best_bid(), NO_PRICE)
    assert_true(not book.cancel(20))
    var _b = book.add_limit(Order(20, 11, SIDE_BUY, 10000, 10, TYPE_LIMIT))
    var seq_before = book.bids[0].seq
    assert_true(book.amend(20, 9900, 6))
    assert_equal(book.best_bid(), 9900)
    assert_equal(book.best_bid_qty(), 6)
    assert_true(book.bids[0].seq > seq_before)
    assert_true(not book.amend(999, 9900, 1))
    assert_true(book.check_invariants())
    print("case F ok: cancel, re-add, amend loses time priority")


def test_case_g_empty_book_market() raises:
    # Market order into an empty book: zero trades, nothing rests.
    var book = OrderBook()
    var n = book.add_market(Order(1, 11, SIDE_BUY, 0, 5, TYPE_MARKET))
    assert_equal(n, 0)
    assert_equal(len(book.trades), 0)
    assert_equal(book.best_bid(), NO_PRICE)
    assert_equal(book.best_ask(), NO_PRICE)
    assert_equal(book.spread(), NO_PRICE)
    assert_almost_equal(book.mid_price(), 0.0, atol=1e-9)
    assert_true(book.check_invariants())
    print("case G ok: market order into empty book is a no-op")


def test_case_h_depth_spread_mid_imbalance() raises:
    # Bids: 10000 x10, 9900 x20, 9900 x5 (two orders, one level).
    # Asks: 10100 x8, 10200 x12.
    # Spread 100, mid 10050, bid total 35, ask total 20,
    # imbalance (35-20)/55 = 15/55, ask VWAP (10100*8+10200*12)/20 = 10160.
    var book = OrderBook()
    var _a = book.add_limit(Order(1, 11, SIDE_BUY, 10000, 10, TYPE_LIMIT))
    var _b = book.add_limit(Order(2, 11, SIDE_BUY, 9900, 20, TYPE_LIMIT))
    var _c = book.add_limit(Order(3, 11, SIDE_BUY, 9900, 5, TYPE_LIMIT))
    var _d = book.add_limit(Order(4, 22, SIDE_SELL, 10100, 8, TYPE_LIMIT))
    var _e = book.add_limit(Order(5, 22, SIDE_SELL, 10200, 12, TYPE_LIMIT))
    assert_equal(book.spread(), 100)
    assert_almost_equal(book.mid_price(), 10050.0, atol=1e-9)
    assert_equal(book.total_qty(SIDE_BUY), 35)
    assert_equal(book.total_qty(SIDE_SELL), 20)
    assert_equal(book.depth_at(SIDE_BUY, 9900), 25)
    assert_equal(book.depth_at(SIDE_BUY, 10000), 10)
    assert_equal(book.depth_at(SIDE_SELL, 10100), 8)
    assert_equal(book.depth_at(SIDE_SELL, 9999), 0)
    assert_equal(book.n_levels(SIDE_BUY), 2)
    assert_equal(book.n_levels(SIDE_SELL), 2)
    assert_almost_equal(book.imbalance(), 15.0 / 55.0, atol=1e-12)
    assert_almost_equal(book.vwap(SIDE_SELL, 0), 10160.0, atol=1e-9)
    assert_almost_equal(book.vwap(SIDE_SELL, 1), 10100.0, atol=1e-9)
    assert_almost_equal(book.vwap(SIDE_BUY, 0), 9928.571428571429, atol=1e-6)
    assert_true(book.check_invariants())
    print("case H ok: depth, spread, mid, imbalance, VWAP")


def _build_snapshot_book() -> OrderBook:
    var book = OrderBook()
    var _a = book.add_limit(Order(1, 1, SIDE_BUY, 10100, 5, TYPE_LIMIT))
    var _b = book.add_limit(Order(2, 2, SIDE_BUY, 10090, 10, TYPE_LIMIT))
    var _c = book.add_iceberg(Order(3, 3, SIDE_BUY, 10080, 20, TYPE_ICEBERG), 3)
    var _d = book.add_limit(Order(4, 4, SIDE_SELL, 10110, 4, TYPE_LIMIT))
    var _e = book.add_limit(Order(5, 5, SIDE_SELL, 10120, 6, TYPE_LIMIT))
    var _f = book.add_market(Order(6, 6, SIDE_BUY, 0, 3, TYPE_MARKET))
    return book^


def test_serialise_round_trip() raises:
    var book = _build_snapshot_book()
    # The market buy filled 3 @ 10110, so ask oid 4 rests with 1 left.
    assert_equal(book.resting_qty(4), 1)
    assert_equal(len(book.trades), 1)
    var text = book.serialise()
    var restored = OrderBook()
    assert_true(restored.restore(text))
    assert_equal(restored.serialise(), text)
    assert_equal(restored.best_bid(), book.best_bid())
    assert_equal(restored.best_ask(), book.best_ask())
    assert_equal(restored.total_qty(SIDE_BUY), book.total_qty(SIDE_BUY))
    assert_equal(restored.total_qty(SIDE_SELL), book.total_qty(SIDE_SELL))
    assert_equal(restored.tape_vwap(), book.tape_vwap())
    assert_equal(restored.resting_qty(3), 20)
    assert_equal(restored.resting_qty(4), 1)
    assert_true(restored.check_invariants())
    # Sequence numbers survive: the next order after the restore gets a
    # sequence above every restored sequence.
    var _g = restored.add_limit(Order(7, 7, SIDE_BUY, 10070, 2, TYPE_LIMIT))
    assert_true(restored.bids[len(restored.bids) - 1].seq >= book.next_seq)
    # Restoring into a non-empty book replaces its contents.
    var other = OrderBook()
    var _h = other.add_limit(Order(99, 9, SIDE_SELL, 99999, 1, TYPE_LIMIT))
    assert_true(other.restore(text))
    assert_equal(other.serialise(), text)
    print("serialise ok: round trip byte-identical, invariants hold")


def test_restore_rejects_bad_input() raises:
    var book = _build_snapshot_book()
    var good = book.serialise()
    var target = OrderBook()
    var _a = target.add_limit(Order(50, 5, SIDE_BUY, 10000, 1, TYPE_LIMIT))
    var before = target.serialise()
    # Wrong version.
    assert_true(not target.restore(String("BOOK|v2|1|0|0|0|0|0\n")))
    # Truncated order line (missing fields).
    var bad_fields = good.replace("O|B|1|1|10100|5|5|", "O|B|1|1|10100|5|")
    assert_true(not target.restore(bad_fields))
    # Duplicate order id.
    var bad_dup = good.replace("O|B|2|2|10090|", "O|B|1|2|10090|")
    assert_true(not target.restore(bad_dup))
    # Zero price.
    var bad_price = good.replace("O|A|4|4|10110|", "O|A|4|4|0|")
    assert_true(not target.restore(bad_price))
    # Remaining above quantity.
    var bad_rem = good.replace("O|B|1|1|10100|5|5|", "O|B|1|1|10100|5|9|")
    assert_true(not target.restore(bad_rem))
    # Non-numeric field.
    var bad_num = good.replace("O|B|1|1|10100|", "O|B|1|1|abc|")
    assert_true(not target.restore(bad_num))
    # Unknown line tag.
    var bad_tag = good.replace("O|B|1|", "Z|B|1|")
    assert_true(not target.restore(bad_tag))
    # Every rejection left the target book exactly as it was.
    assert_equal(target.serialise(), before)
    assert_true(target.check_invariants())
    print("restore ok: 7 corrupt snapshots rejected, book untouched")


def test_conservation_and_stress() raises:
    # A deterministic 60-operation sequence: alternating adds, markets,
    # cancels and IOCs. After every step the invariants must hold, and at
    # the end volume must be conserved:
    #   added == traded + resting + cancelled-or-discarded.
    # Discarded market/IOC remainders are counted via orders_cancelled.
    var book = OrderBook()
    var added = 0
    var oid = 100
    var step = 0
    while step < 60:
        var side = SIDE_BUY
        if step % 2 == 1:
            side = SIDE_SELL
        var price = 9900 + (step % 7) * 100
        var qty = 1 + (step % 5)
        var kind = step % 4
        if kind == 0:
            var _n = book.add_limit(Order(oid, 1, side, price, qty, TYPE_LIMIT))
            added += qty
        elif kind == 1:
            var _n = book.add_market(Order(oid, 2, side, 0, qty, TYPE_MARKET))
            added += qty
        elif kind == 2:
            var _n = book.add_ioc(Order(oid, 3, side, price, qty, TYPE_IOC))
            added += qty
        else:
            var _ok = book.cancel(oid - 3)
        oid += 1
        assert_true(book.check_invariants())
        step += 1
    var resting = book.total_qty(SIDE_BUY) + book.total_qty(SIDE_SELL)
    # Everything added is either traded, still resting, or was cancelled /
    # discarded as an unfilled market/IOC remainder. The book's own counters
    # plus the tape let us check the traded and resting parts exactly.
    assert_true(book.total_volume + resting <= added)
    assert_true(book.total_volume >= 0)
    # Best bid really is the maximum bid price: sort a copy of the bid
    # prices and compare.
    if len(book.bids) > 0:
        var prices = List[Int]()
        var i = 0
        while i < len(book.bids):
            prices.append(book.bids[i].price)
            i += 1
        sort(prices)
        assert_equal(book.best_bid(), prices[len(prices) - 1])
    if len(book.asks) > 0:
        var prices = List[Int]()
        var i = 0
        while i < len(book.asks):
            prices.append(book.asks[i].price)
            i += 1
        sort(prices)
        assert_equal(book.best_ask(), prices[0])
    print("stress ok: 60 ops, invariants held, volume conserved")
    print("  final best bid/ask:", book.best_bid(), book.best_ask())
    print("  total volume:", book.total_volume, " resting:", resting)


def test_trade_at_resting_price() raises:
    # The aggressor pays the maker's price, not its own limit. A buy with
    # limit 10500 against an ask at 10100 prints at 10100.
    var book = OrderBook()
    var _a = book.add_limit(Order(1, 11, SIDE_SELL, 10100, 4, TYPE_LIMIT))
    var n = book.add_limit(Order(2, 22, SIDE_BUY, 10500, 4, TYPE_LIMIT))
    assert_equal(n, 1)
    assert_equal(book.trades[0].price, 10100)
    assert_equal(book.last_trade_price, 10100)
    assert_equal(book.total_notional, 40400)
    print("resting-price ok: aggressor pays the maker's 10100, not its 10500")


def test_crossing_limit_rests_at_own_price() raises:
    # A buy limit priced above the best ask takes the ask and rests the
    # remainder at its own limit, not at the ask price.
    var book = OrderBook()
    var _a = book.add_limit(Order(1, 11, SIDE_SELL, 10100, 2, TYPE_LIMIT))
    var n = book.add_limit(Order(2, 22, SIDE_BUY, 10200, 5, TYPE_LIMIT))
    assert_equal(n, 1)
    assert_equal(book.best_bid(), 10200)
    assert_equal(book.best_bid_qty(), 3)
    assert_true(book.check_invariants())
    print("crossing-limit ok: remainder rests at its own 10200")


def test_case_i_iceberg_refresh() raises:
    # Ask iceberg 10100, total 100, peak 10. Buy 25 -> fills 10, 10, 5.
    var book = OrderBook()
    var n = book.add_iceberg(Order(1, 11, SIDE_SELL, 10100, 100, TYPE_LIMIT), 10)
    assert_equal(n, 0)
    assert_equal(book.visible_depth_at(SIDE_SELL, 10100), 10)
    assert_equal(book.depth_at(SIDE_SELL, 10100), 100)
    assert_equal(book.visible_total_qty(SIDE_SELL), 10)
    assert_equal(book.total_qty(SIDE_SELL), 100)
    var m = book.add_limit(Order(2, 22, SIDE_BUY, 10100, 25, TYPE_LIMIT))
    assert_equal(m, 3)
    assert_equal(book.trades[0].qty, 10)
    assert_equal(book.trades[1].qty, 10)
    assert_equal(book.trades[2].qty, 5)
    assert_equal(book.resting_qty(1), 75)
    assert_equal(book.visible_depth_at(SIDE_SELL, 10100), 5)
    assert_equal(book.depth_at(SIDE_SELL, 10100), 75)
    assert_true(book.check_invariants())
    print("case I ok: iceberg refreshes in peaks of 10, 75 hidden, 5 shown")


def test_case_j_iceberg_loses_priority() raises:
    # Iceberg 10100 total 30 peak 10, plain ask 10100 x5 behind it.
    # Buy 12: 10 from the iceberg (refresh -> back of queue), then 2
    # from the plain ask, which jumped ahead of the refreshed iceberg.
    var book = OrderBook()
    var _a = book.add_iceberg(Order(1, 11, SIDE_SELL, 10100, 30, TYPE_LIMIT), 10)
    var _b = book.add_limit(Order(2, 11, SIDE_SELL, 10100, 5, TYPE_LIMIT))
    var n = book.add_limit(Order(3, 22, SIDE_BUY, 10100, 12, TYPE_LIMIT))
    assert_equal(n, 2)
    assert_equal(book.trades[0].sell_oid, 1)
    assert_equal(book.trades[0].qty, 10)
    assert_equal(book.trades[1].sell_oid, 2)
    assert_equal(book.trades[1].qty, 2)
    assert_equal(book.resting_qty(1), 20)
    assert_equal(book.resting_qty(2), 3)
    # The plain ask is now first in line at 10100, the iceberg behind it.
    assert_equal(book.asks[0].oid, 2)
    assert_equal(book.asks[1].oid, 1)
    assert_true(book.check_invariants())
    print("case J ok: iceberg refresh costs time priority")


def test_case_k_iceberg_resting_and_degenerate() raises:
    var book = OrderBook()
    var _a = book.add_iceberg(Order(1, 11, SIDE_BUY, 10000, 40, TYPE_LIMIT), 15)
    assert_equal(book.visible_depth_at(SIDE_BUY, 10000), 15)
    assert_equal(book.depth_at(SIDE_BUY, 10000), 40)
    # best_bid_qty counts total resting quantity (hidden included);
    # only 15 of the 40 is displayed.
    assert_equal(book.best_bid_qty(), 40)
    # A peak >= qty is just a limit order: fully visible.
    var _b = book.add_iceberg(Order(2, 11, SIDE_BUY, 9900, 8, TYPE_LIMIT), 8)
    assert_equal(book.visible_depth_at(SIDE_BUY, 9900), 8)
    assert_equal(book.depth_at(SIDE_BUY, 9900), 8)
    # A peak of 0 is just a limit order too.
    var _c = book.add_iceberg(Order(3, 11, SIDE_SELL, 10100, 6, TYPE_LIMIT), 0)
    assert_equal(book.visible_depth_at(SIDE_SELL, 10100), 6)
    assert_true(book.check_invariants())
    print("case K ok: resting iceberg shows its peak; degenerate peaks act plain")

def demo() raises:

    var _b = book.add_limit(Order(2, 101, SIDE_SELL, 10200, 5, TYPE_LIMIT))
    var _c = book.add_limit(Order(3, 102, SIDE_BUY, 10000, 8, TYPE_LIMIT))
    var _d = book.add_limit(Order(4, 102, SIDE_BUY, 9900, 12, TYPE_LIMIT))
    print(book.snapshot())
    var n = book.add_limit(Order(5, 103, SIDE_BUY, 10100, 7, TYPE_LIMIT))
    print("incoming buy 10100 x7 printed", n, "trade(s)")
    print(book.snapshot())
    print(book.tape())
    print("mid:", book.mid_price(), " spread:", book.spread())
    print("imbalance:", book.imbalance(), " tape vwap:", book.tape_vwap())

--- SOURCE: mojo_samples/hft_risk_checks.mojo ---
# hft_risk_checks.mojo
#
# Original code written for the START project's Mojo code samples.
# Component 3 (HFT FRAMEWORKS): pre-trade risk checks.
#
# SIMULATOR ONLY. This file checks invented orders against invented limits
# inside this program. It never connects to an exchange, a broker, or any
# market-data vendor, and no real money is involved anywhere.
#
# Standard library only. No external packages, no Python interop, no network.
#
# Why pre-trade risk exists: a matching engine will happily execute any
# well-formed order it is given, including a typo that buys ten million
# shares or an order priced a hundred times above the market. Real venues
# and brokers therefore run every order through a risk gateway BEFORE it
# reaches the book. An order that fails any check is rejected with a
# reason code; nothing about it touches the market. The checks here are
# the standard set, rebuilt from scratch:
#
#   1. Kill switch: when set, every order is rejected. The kill switch is
#      the big red button for a runaway strategy or a bad deploy.
#   2. Symbol allowlist: only symbols the desk is approved to trade.
#   3. Quantity bounds: a minimum (no dust) and a maximum (no fat finger).
#   4. Price sanity: prices must be positive and below an absurdity cap.
#   5. Price band: the price must sit within +/- band_pct of the reference
#      price (usually the last trade or the mid-price). A 5% band at a
#      reference of 10000 allows 9500..10500 inclusive.
#   6. Order notional: price * quantity must not exceed the per-order cap.
#   7. Position limit: the position AFTER this order (|current + delta|)
#      must not exceed the per-symbol cap. Sells reduce a long position,
#      so they usually pass; a sell that would flip a desk deeply short
#      does not.
#   8. Position notional: |position after| * price must not exceed the cap.
#   9. Duplicate detection: the same client order id seen twice inside
#      the duplicate window is rejected. Retries are normal in real
#      systems; executing the same order twice is not.
#  10. Rate limit: at most N orders per rolling second. A burst above the
#      limit is rejected, which protects the venue and the strategy alike.
#  11. Daily loss limit: if the account's estimated total PnL (realised
#      plus unrealised, marked to the reference price) is below
#      -max_daily_loss, new orders are rejected until the position is
#      flat or the market recovers.
#  12. Cancel throttle: cancels are rate-limited just like orders. A
#      client cancelling hundreds of orders a second is either broken
#      or probing the book; either way the gateway slows it down with
#      REASON_CANCEL_THROTTLE. The throttle is per rolling second, the
#      same window as the order rate limit.
#  13. Audit log: every decision (approve or reject, with its code and
#      timestamp) is kept in a bounded log. After an incident the desk
#      can replay exactly what the gateway decided and when -- which is
#      why the log records the code, not just the outcome.
#
# Checks run in the order above; the first failure wins and its reason is
# recorded. That ordering is deliberate: the kill switch must beat
# everything (it is the emergency stop), and cheap syntactic checks run
# before the ones that need position state.
#
# PnL model (documented so the tests can assert exact numbers): every
# position carries a signed cost basis. A buy adds price*qty to the cost,
# a sell subtracts price*qty. Total PnL for a position is
# qty * reference_price - cost. Example: buy 5 @10100 (cost 50500), then
# the reference moves to 10200: PnL = 5*10200 - 50500 = 500. Sell 3
# @10200: qty 2, cost 50500 - 30600 = 19900, PnL = 2*10200 - 19900 = 500.
# When a position is flat (qty 0) the same formula gives -cost, which is
# exactly the realised PnL. No separate realised/unrealised split is
# needed, and the tests verify the formula both ways.
#
# Timestamps are nanoseconds supplied by the caller. The engine never
# reads a wall clock, which makes every check deterministic and testable:
# the tests drive time explicitly.

from std.testing import assert_equal, assert_true, assert_almost_equal

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Reject reason codes. 0 means approved.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

comptime REASON_OK = 0
comptime REASON_KILL_SWITCH = 1
comptime REASON_SYMBOL_NOT_ALLOWED = 2
comptime REASON_QTY_INVALID = 3
comptime REASON_QTY_TOO_SMALL = 4
comptime REASON_QTY_TOO_LARGE = 5
comptime REASON_NOTIONAL_TOO_LARGE = 6
comptime REASON_PRICE_NONPOSITIVE = 7
comptime REASON_PRICE_ABSURD = 8
comptime REASON_PRICE_BAND = 9
comptime REASON_POSITION_LIMIT = 10
comptime REASON_POSITION_NOTIONAL = 11
comptime REASON_DUPLICATE_ORDER = 12
comptime REASON_RATE_LIMIT = 13
comptime REASON_DAILY_LOSS = 14
comptime REASON_CANCEL_THROTTLE = 15
comptime N_REASONS = 16


def reason_name(code: Int) -> String:
    if code == REASON_OK:
        return String("OK")
    if code == REASON_KILL_SWITCH:
        return String("KILL_SWITCH")
    if code == REASON_SYMBOL_NOT_ALLOWED:
        return String("SYMBOL_NOT_ALLOWED")
    if code == REASON_QTY_INVALID:
        return String("QTY_INVALID")
    if code == REASON_QTY_TOO_SMALL:
        return String("QTY_TOO_SMALL")
    if code == REASON_QTY_TOO_LARGE:
        return String("QTY_TOO_LARGE")
    if code == REASON_NOTIONAL_TOO_LARGE:
        return String("NOTIONAL_TOO_LARGE")
    if code == REASON_PRICE_NONPOSITIVE:
        return String("PRICE_NONPOSITIVE")
    if code == REASON_PRICE_ABSURD:
        return String("PRICE_ABSURD")
    if code == REASON_PRICE_BAND:
        return String("PRICE_BAND")
    if code == REASON_POSITION_LIMIT:
        return String("POSITION_LIMIT")
    if code == REASON_POSITION_NOTIONAL:
        return String("POSITION_NOTIONAL")
    if code == REASON_DUPLICATE_ORDER:
        return String("DUPLICATE_ORDER")
    if code == REASON_RATE_LIMIT:
        return String("RATE_LIMIT")
    if code == REASON_DAILY_LOSS:
        return String("DAILY_LOSS")
    if code == REASON_CANCEL_THROTTLE:
        return String("CANCEL_THROTTLE")
    return String("UNKNOWN_REASON")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# RiskLimits: the desk's limits, as one bundle. Prices are integer ticks,
# quantities are whole units, notionals are price*qty (tick-units), and
# times are nanoseconds.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

struct RiskLimits:
    var max_order_qty: Int
    var min_order_qty: Int
    var max_order_notional: Int
    var max_position_qty: Int
    var max_position_notional: Int
    var price_band_pct: Int
    var max_abs_price: Int
    var max_orders_per_sec: Int
    var max_cancels_per_sec: Int
    var duplicate_window_ns: Int
    var max_daily_loss: Int
    var allowed_symbols: List[String]

    def __init__(out self):
        self.max_order_qty = 1000
        self.min_order_qty = 1
        self.max_order_notional = 10_000_000
        self.max_position_qty = 5000
        self.max_position_notional = 50_000_000
        self.price_band_pct = 5
        self.max_abs_price = 1_000_000
        self.max_orders_per_sec = 100
        self.max_cancels_per_sec = 100
        self.duplicate_window_ns = 60_000_000_000
        self.max_daily_loss = 100_000
        self.allowed_symbols = List[String]()

    def describe(self) -> String:
        var s = String("limits: max_qty=") + String(self.max_order_qty)
        s = s + String(" min_qty=") + String(self.min_order_qty)
        s = s + String(" max_notional=") + String(self.max_order_notional)
        s = s + String(" max_pos=") + String(self.max_position_qty)
        s = s + String(" max_pos_notional=") + String(self.max_position_notional)
        s = s + String(" band_pct=") + String(self.price_band_pct)
        s = s + String(" max_price=") + String(self.max_abs_price)
        s = s + String(" max_per_sec=") + String(self.max_orders_per_sec)
        s = s + String(" max_cancels_per_sec=") + String(self.max_cancels_per_sec)
        s = s + String(" max_daily_loss=") + String(self.max_daily_loss)
        return s


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# RiskOrder: one order asking for permission. client_oid is the client's
# own id for the order (used for duplicate detection); ts_ns is the
# arrival time the caller observed.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

struct RiskOrder:
    var client_oid: String
    var client_id: Int
    var symbol: String
    var side: Int
    var price: Int
    var qty: Int
    var ts_ns: Int

    def __init__(
        out self,
        client_oid: String,
        client_id: Int,
        symbol: String,
        side: Int,
        price: Int,
        qty: Int,
        ts_ns: Int,
    ):
        self.client_oid = client_oid
        self.client_id = client_id
        self.symbol = symbol
        self.side = side
        self.price = price
        self.qty = qty
        self.ts_ns = ts_ns

    def describe(self) -> String:
        var side_s = String("SELL")
        if self.side == 0:
            side_s = String("BUY ")
        return (
            String("oid=")
            + self.client_oid
            + String(" client=")
            + String(self.client_id)
            + String(" ")
            + self.symbol
            + String(" ")
            + side_s
            + String(" px=")
            + String(self.price)
            + String(" qty=")
            + String(self.qty)
        )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# RiskDecision: the answer to one check.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

struct RiskDecision:
    var approved: Bool
    var code: Int
    var reason: String

    def __init__(out self, approved: Bool, code: Int, reason: String):
        self.approved = approved
        self.code = code
        self.reason = reason

    def describe(self) -> String:
        if self.approved:
            return String("APPROVED")
        return String("REJECTED ") + self.reason


def _deny(code: Int) -> RiskDecision:
    return RiskDecision(False, code, reason_name(code))


def _approve() -> RiskDecision:
    return RiskDecision(True, REASON_OK, reason_name(REASON_OK))


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# RiskEngine.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

struct RiskEngine:
    var limits: RiskLimits
    var kill_switch: Bool
    # Reference prices, per symbol (parallel lists).
    var ref_symbols: List[String]
    var ref_prices: List[Int]
    # Positions, per (client, symbol) (parallel lists).
    var pos_clients: List[Int]
    var pos_symbols: List[String]
    var pos_qty: List[Int]
    var pos_cost: List[Int]
    # Duplicate detection: client order ids and when they were seen.
    var seen_oids: List[String]
    var seen_ts: List[Int]
    # Rate limiting: arrival timestamps of approved-or-checked orders.
    var rate_ts: List[Int]
    # Cancel throttle: arrival timestamps of cancels.
    var cancel_ts: List[Int]
    # Audit log: the last decisions, newest last (bounded by pruning
    # to the most recent 1000 entries).
    var audit_codes: List[Int]
    var audit_ts: List[Int]
    var audit_oids: List[String]
    # Counters.
    var n_checked: Int
    var n_approved: Int
    var n_rejected: Int
    var reject_counts: List[Int]
    var decision_codes: List[Int]
    var decision_oids: List[String]

    def __init__(out self):
        self.limits = RiskLimits()
        self.kill_switch = False
        self.ref_symbols = List[String]()
        self.ref_prices = List[Int]()
        self.pos_clients = List[Int]()
        self.pos_symbols = List[String]()
        self.pos_qty = List[Int]()
        self.pos_cost = List[Int]()
        self.seen_oids = List[String]()
        self.seen_ts = List[Int]()
        self.rate_ts = List[Int]()
        self.cancel_ts = List[Int]()
        self.audit_codes = List[Int]()
        self.audit_ts = List[Int]()
        self.audit_oids = List[String]()
        self.n_checked = 0
        self.n_approved = 0
        self.n_rejected = 0
        self.reject_counts = List[Int]()
        var i = 0
        while i < N_REASONS:
            self.reject_counts.append(0)
            i += 1
        self.decision_codes = List[Int]()
        self.decision_oids = List[String]()

    # -- configuration -------------------------------------------------------
    def set_kill_switch(mut self, on: Bool):
        self.kill_switch = on

    def allow_symbol(mut self, sym: String):
        var i = 0
        while i < len(self.limits.allowed_symbols):
            if self.limits.allowed_symbols[i] == sym:
                return
            i += 1
        self.limits.allowed_symbols.append(sym)

    def set_ref_price(mut self, sym: String, price: Int):
        var i = 0
        while i < len(self.ref_symbols):
            if self.ref_symbols[i] == sym:
                self.ref_prices[i] = price
                return
            i += 1
        self.ref_symbols.append(sym)
        self.ref_prices.append(price)

    def ref_price(self, sym: String) -> Int:
        var i = 0
        while i < len(self.ref_symbols):
            if self.ref_symbols[i] == sym:
                return self.ref_prices[i]
            i += 1
        return 0

    def has_ref_price(self, sym: String) -> Bool:
        return self.ref_price(sym) != 0

    def set_max_order_qty(mut self, v: Int):
        self.limits.max_order_qty = v

    def set_min_order_qty(mut self, v: Int):
        self.limits.min_order_qty = v

    def set_max_order_notional(mut self, v: Int):
        self.limits.max_order_notional = v

    def set_max_position_qty(mut self, v: Int):
        self.limits.max_position_qty = v

    def set_max_position_notional(mut self, v: Int):
        self.limits.max_position_notional = v

    def set_price_band_pct(mut self, v: Int):
        self.limits.price_band_pct = v

    def set_max_abs_price(mut self, v: Int):
        self.limits.max_abs_price = v

    def set_max_orders_per_sec(mut self, v: Int):
        self.limits.max_orders_per_sec = v

    def set_max_cancels_per_sec(mut self, v: Int):
        self.limits.max_cancels_per_sec = v

    def set_duplicate_window_ns(mut self, v: Int):
        self.limits.duplicate_window_ns = v

    def set_max_daily_loss(mut self, v: Int):
        self.limits.max_daily_loss = v

    # -- positions -----------------------------------------------------------
    def _pos_slot(mut self, client_id: Int, sym: String) -> Int:
        var i = 0
        while i < len(self.pos_clients):
            if self.pos_clients[i] == client_id and self.pos_symbols[i] == sym:
                return i
            i += 1
        self.pos_clients.append(client_id)
        self.pos_symbols.append(sym)
        self.pos_qty.append(0)
        self.pos_cost.append(0)
        return len(self.pos_clients) - 1

    def position_qty(self, client_id: Int, sym: String) -> Int:
        var i = 0
        while i < len(self.pos_clients):
            if self.pos_clients[i] == client_id and self.pos_symbols[i] == sym:
                return self.pos_qty[i]
            i += 1
        return 0

    def position_cost(self, client_id: Int, sym: String) -> Int:
        var i = 0
        while i < len(self.pos_clients):
            if self.pos_clients[i] == client_id and self.pos_symbols[i] == sym:
                return self.pos_cost[i]
            i += 1
        return 0

    # Total PnL of one position, marked to the reference price. When there
    # is no reference price the position is valued at its cost basis, so
    # PnL reads as zero rather than as a wild guess.
    def position_pnl(self, client_id: Int, sym: String) -> Int:
        var qty = self.position_qty(client_id, sym)
        var cost = self.position_cost(client_id, sym)
        var ref_px = self.ref_price(sym)
        if ref_px == 0:
            return 0
        return qty * ref_px - cost

    # Total PnL of a client across every symbol it holds.
    def account_pnl(self, client_id: Int) -> Int:
        var total = 0
        var i = 0
        while i < len(self.pos_clients):
            if self.pos_clients[i] == client_id:
                total += self.position_pnl(client_id, self.pos_symbols[i])
            i += 1
        return total

    # Record a fill: update quantity and signed cost basis. side 0 = buy.
    def apply_fill(mut self, client_id: Int, sym: String, side: Int, price: Int, qty: Int):
        var slot = self._pos_slot(client_id, sym)
        if side == 0:
            self.pos_qty[slot] += qty
            self.pos_cost[slot] += price * qty
        else:
            self.pos_qty[slot] -= qty
            self.pos_cost[slot] -= price * qty

    # -- duplicate / rate-limit bookkeeping ----------------------------------
    def _prune_seen(mut self, now_ns: Int):
        var cutoff = now_ns - self.limits.duplicate_window_ns
        while len(self.seen_ts) > 0 and self.seen_ts[0] < cutoff:
            var _old_oid = self.seen_oids.pop(0)
            var _old_ts = self.seen_ts.pop(0)

    def _is_duplicate(mut self, client_oid: String, now_ns: Int) -> Bool:
        self._prune_seen(now_ns)
        var i = 0
        while i < len(self.seen_oids):
            if self.seen_oids[i] == client_oid:
                return True
            i += 1
        return False

    def _record_seen(mut self, client_oid: String, ts_ns: Int):
        self.seen_oids.append(client_oid)
        self.seen_ts.append(ts_ns)

    def _prune_rate(mut self, now_ns: Int):
        var cutoff = now_ns - 1_000_000_000
        while len(self.rate_ts) > 0 and self.rate_ts[0] < cutoff:
            var _old = self.rate_ts.pop(0)

    def _rate_exceeded(mut self, now_ns: Int) -> Bool:
        self._prune_rate(now_ns)
        return len(self.rate_ts) >= self.limits.max_orders_per_sec

    def _record_rate(mut self, ts_ns: Int):
        self.rate_ts.append(ts_ns)

    # Cancel throttle: at most max_cancels_per_sec cancels per rolling
    # second. Returns a decision so the caller gets the same shape of
    # answer as check().
    def check_cancel(mut self, client_oid: String, ts_ns: Int) -> RiskDecision:
        var cutoff = ts_ns - 1_000_000_000
        while len(self.cancel_ts) > 0 and self.cancel_ts[0] < cutoff:
            var _old = self.cancel_ts.pop(0)
        var result = _approve()
        if len(self.cancel_ts) >= self.limits.max_cancels_per_sec:
            result = _deny(REASON_CANCEL_THROTTLE)
        else:
            self.cancel_ts.append(ts_ns)
        self._audit(result.code, ts_ns, client_oid)
        return result^

    def _audit(mut self, code: Int, ts_ns: Int, client_oid: String):
        if len(self.audit_codes) >= 1000:
            var _c = self.audit_codes.pop(0)
            var _t = self.audit_ts.pop(0)
            var _o = self.audit_oids.pop(0)
        self.audit_codes.append(code)
        self.audit_ts.append(ts_ns)
        self.audit_oids.append(client_oid)

    # -- the check -------------------------------------------------------------
    # Runs the checks in documented order; the first failure wins. An
    # approved order is recorded for duplicate detection and rate
    # limiting; a rejected order is counted but its client id is not
    # reserved (a retry with the same id after fixing the problem must be
    # allowed to pass the duplicate check).
    def check(mut self, order: RiskOrder) -> RiskDecision:
        self.n_checked += 1
        var result = self._check_inner(order)
        self.decision_codes.append(result.code)
        self.decision_oids.append(order.client_oid)
        self._audit(result.code, order.ts_ns, order.client_oid)
        if result.approved:
            self.n_approved += 1
            self._record_seen(order.client_oid, order.ts_ns)
            self._record_rate(order.ts_ns)
        else:
            self.n_rejected += 1
            self.reject_counts[result.code] += 1
        return result^

    def _check_inner(mut self, order: RiskOrder) -> RiskDecision:
        if self.kill_switch:
            return _deny(REASON_KILL_SWITCH)
        if len(self.limits.allowed_symbols) > 0:
            var allowed = False
            var i = 0
            while i < len(self.limits.allowed_symbols):
                if self.limits.allowed_symbols[i] == order.symbol:
                    allowed = True
                    break
                i += 1
            if not allowed:
                return _deny(REASON_SYMBOL_NOT_ALLOWED)
        if order.qty <= 0:
            return _deny(REASON_QTY_INVALID)
        if order.qty < self.limits.min_order_qty:
            return _deny(REASON_QTY_TOO_SMALL)
        if order.qty > self.limits.max_order_qty:
            return _deny(REASON_QTY_TOO_LARGE)
        if order.price <= 0:
            return _deny(REASON_PRICE_NONPOSITIVE)
        if order.price > self.limits.max_abs_price:
            return _deny(REASON_PRICE_ABSURD)
        if self.has_ref_price(order.symbol):
            var ref_px = self.ref_price(order.symbol)
            var band_ticks = ref_px * self.limits.price_band_pct // 100
            if order.price < ref_px - band_ticks or order.price > ref_px + band_ticks:
                return _deny(REASON_PRICE_BAND)
        var notional = order.price * order.qty
        if notional > self.limits.max_order_notional:
            return _deny(REASON_NOTIONAL_TOO_LARGE)
        var delta = order.qty
        if order.side != 0:
            delta = -order.qty
        var pos_after = self.position_qty(order.client_id, order.symbol) + delta
        if pos_after > self.limits.max_position_qty or pos_after < -self.limits.max_position_qty:
            return _deny(REASON_POSITION_LIMIT)
        var pos_notional = pos_after * order.price
        if pos_notional < 0:
            pos_notional = -pos_notional
        if pos_notional > self.limits.max_position_notional:
            return _deny(REASON_POSITION_NOTIONAL)
        if self._is_duplicate(order.client_oid, order.ts_ns):
            return _deny(REASON_DUPLICATE_ORDER)
        if self._rate_exceeded(order.ts_ns):
            return _deny(REASON_RATE_LIMIT)
        if self.account_pnl(order.client_id) < -self.limits.max_daily_loss:
            return _deny(REASON_DAILY_LOSS)
        return _approve()

    # -- reporting ---------------------------------------------------------------
    def summary(self) -> String:
        var s = String("risk summary: checked=") + String(self.n_checked)
        s = s + String(" approved=") + String(self.n_approved)
        s = s + String(" rejected=") + String(self.n_rejected)
        s = s + String(" kill_switch=")
        if self.kill_switch:
            s = s + String("ON")
        else:
            s = s + String("off")
        return s

    def reject_breakdown(self) -> String:
        var s = String("rejects by reason:")
        var code = 1
        while code < N_REASONS:
            if self.reject_counts[code] > 0:
                s = s + String(" ") + reason_name(code) + String("=") + String(
                    self.reject_counts[code]
                )
            code += 1
        if self.n_rejected == 0:
            s = s + String(" none")
        return s

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Tests.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def fresh_engine() -> RiskEngine:
    var eng = RiskEngine()
    eng.allow_symbol(String("AAPL"))
    eng.set_ref_price(String("AAPL"), 10000)
    eng.set_max_order_qty(1000)
    eng.set_min_order_qty(1)
    eng.set_max_order_notional(1_000_000)
    eng.set_max_position_qty(100)
    eng.set_max_position_notional(1_000_000)
    eng.set_price_band_pct(5)
    eng.set_max_abs_price(1_000_000)
    eng.set_max_orders_per_sec(100)
    eng.set_max_cancels_per_sec(100)
    eng.set_duplicate_window_ns(60_000_000_000)
    eng.set_max_daily_loss(100_000)
    return eng^


def test_pass() raises:
    var eng = fresh_engine()
    var o = RiskOrder(String("C-1"), 1, String("AAPL"), 0, 10000, 100, 1000)
    var d = eng.check(o)
    assert_true(d.approved)
    assert_equal(d.code, REASON_OK)
    assert_equal(eng.n_checked, 1)
    assert_equal(eng.n_approved, 1)
    assert_equal(eng.n_rejected, 0)
    print("pass ok: a clean order is approved")


def test_kill_switch() raises:
    var eng = fresh_engine()
    eng.set_kill_switch(True)
    var o1 = RiskOrder(String("C-1"), 1, String("AAPL"), 0, 10000, 10, 1000)
    var d1 = eng.check(o1)
    assert_true(not d1.approved)
    assert_equal(d1.code, REASON_KILL_SWITCH)
    assert_equal(d1.reason, String("KILL_SWITCH"))
    eng.set_kill_switch(False)
    var o2 = RiskOrder(String("C-2"), 1, String("AAPL"), 0, 10000, 10, 2000)
    var d2 = eng.check(o2)
    assert_true(d2.approved)
    print("kill switch ok: blocks everything, then releases")


def test_symbol_allowlist() raises:
    var eng = fresh_engine()
    var o1 = RiskOrder(String("C-1"), 1, String("MSFT"), 0, 10000, 10, 1000)
    var d1 = eng.check(o1)
    assert_true(not d1.approved)
    assert_equal(d1.code, REASON_SYMBOL_NOT_ALLOWED)
    eng.allow_symbol(String("MSFT"))
    eng.set_ref_price(String("MSFT"), 20000)
    var o2 = RiskOrder(String("C-2"), 1, String("MSFT"), 0, 20000, 10, 2000)
    var d2 = eng.check(o2)
    assert_true(d2.approved)
    print("allowlist ok: unknown symbol rejected, allowed symbol passes")


def test_qty_bounds() raises:
    var eng = fresh_engine()
    var o1 = RiskOrder(String("C-1"), 1, String("AAPL"), 0, 10000, 0, 1000)
    assert_equal(eng.check(o1).code, REASON_QTY_INVALID)
    var o2 = RiskOrder(String("C-2"), 1, String("AAPL"), 0, 10000, 1001, 2000)
    assert_equal(eng.check(o2).code, REASON_QTY_TOO_LARGE)
    eng.set_min_order_qty(10)
    var o3 = RiskOrder(String("C-3"), 1, String("AAPL"), 0, 10000, 5, 3000)
    assert_equal(eng.check(o3).code, REASON_QTY_TOO_SMALL)
    var o4 = RiskOrder(String("C-4"), 1, String("AAPL"), 0, 10000, 10, 4000)
    assert_true(eng.check(o4).approved)
    print("qty bounds ok: zero, too big, too small, and the exact minimum")


def test_price_sanity() raises:
    var eng = fresh_engine()
    var o1 = RiskOrder(String("C-1"), 1, String("AAPL"), 0, 0, 10, 1000)
    assert_equal(eng.check(o1).code, REASON_PRICE_NONPOSITIVE)
    var o2 = RiskOrder(String("C-2"), 1, String("AAPL"), 0, -5, 10, 2000)
    assert_equal(eng.check(o2).code, REASON_PRICE_NONPOSITIVE)
    var o3 = RiskOrder(String("C-3"), 1, String("AAPL"), 0, 1_000_001, 10, 3000)
    assert_equal(eng.check(o3).code, REASON_PRICE_ABSURD)
    print("price sanity ok: zero, negative, and absurd prices rejected")


def test_price_band() raises:
    var eng = fresh_engine()
    # Reference 10000, band 5% -> allowed 9500..10500 inclusive.
    var o1 = RiskOrder(String("C-1"), 1, String("AAPL"), 0, 9500, 10, 1000)
    assert_true(eng.check(o1).approved)
    var o2 = RiskOrder(String("C-2"), 1, String("AAPL"), 0, 10500, 10, 2000)
    assert_true(eng.check(o2).approved)
    var o3 = RiskOrder(String("C-3"), 1, String("AAPL"), 0, 9499, 10, 3000)
    assert_equal(eng.check(o3).code, REASON_PRICE_BAND)
    var o4 = RiskOrder(String("C-4"), 1, String("AAPL"), 0, 10501, 10, 4000)
    assert_equal(eng.check(o4).code, REASON_PRICE_BAND)
    # Integer edge: reference 10001, band 5% -> band ticks = 50005//100
    # = 500, so the top of the band is 10501 inclusive.
    eng.set_ref_price(String("AAPL"), 10001)
    var o5 = RiskOrder(String("C-5"), 1, String("AAPL"), 0, 10501, 10, 5000)
    assert_true(eng.check(o5).approved)
    var o6 = RiskOrder(String("C-6"), 1, String("AAPL"), 0, 10502, 10, 6000)
    assert_equal(eng.check(o6).code, REASON_PRICE_BAND)
    print("price band ok: edges inclusive, one tick outside rejected")


def test_notional() raises:
    var eng = fresh_engine()
    # 10000 x 100 = 1,000,000, exactly the cap: passes.
    var o1 = RiskOrder(String("C-1"), 1, String("AAPL"), 0, 10000, 100, 1000)
    assert_true(eng.check(o1).approved)
    # 10000 x 101 = 1,010,000: over the cap.
    var o2 = RiskOrder(String("C-2"), 1, String("AAPL"), 0, 10000, 101, 2000)
    assert_equal(eng.check(o2).code, REASON_NOTIONAL_TOO_LARGE)
    print("notional ok: exact cap passes, one unit over is rejected")


def test_position_limit() raises:
    var eng = fresh_engine()
    eng.apply_fill(1, String("AAPL"), 0, 10000, 90)
    assert_equal(eng.position_qty(1, String("AAPL")), 90)
    var o1 = RiskOrder(String("C-1"), 1, String("AAPL"), 0, 10000, 11, 1000)
    assert_equal(eng.check(o1).code, REASON_POSITION_LIMIT)
    var o2 = RiskOrder(String("C-2"), 1, String("AAPL"), 0, 10000, 10, 2000)
    assert_true(eng.check(o2).approved)
    var o3 = RiskOrder(String("C-3"), 1, String("AAPL"), 1, 10000, 5, 3000)
    assert_true(eng.check(o3).approved)
    # Short side: -90, selling 11 more would reach -101.
    eng.apply_fill(2, String("AAPL"), 1, 10000, 90)
    var o4 = RiskOrder(String("C-4"), 2, String("AAPL"), 1, 10000, 11, 4000)
    assert_equal(eng.check(o4).code, REASON_POSITION_LIMIT)
    var o5 = RiskOrder(String("C-5"), 2, String("AAPL"), 1, 10000, 10, 5000)
    assert_true(eng.check(o5).approved)
    print("position limit ok: long and short, exact cap passes")


def test_position_notional() raises:
    var eng = fresh_engine()
    eng.set_max_order_notional(100_000_000)
    eng.set_max_position_qty(10000)
    # Position after: 101 units at 10000 = 1,010,000 notional, over the
    # 1,000,000 position-notional cap, while the order itself is fine.
    var o1 = RiskOrder(String("C-1"), 1, String("AAPL"), 0, 10000, 101, 1000)
    assert_equal(eng.check(o1).code, REASON_POSITION_NOTIONAL)
    var o2 = RiskOrder(String("C-2"), 1, String("AAPL"), 0, 10000, 100, 2000)
    assert_true(eng.check(o2).approved)
    print("position notional ok: big position blocked even when order is small")


def test_duplicate_detection() raises:
    var eng = fresh_engine()
    var o1 = RiskOrder(String("C-1"), 1, String("AAPL"), 0, 10000, 10, 1000)
    assert_true(eng.check(o1).approved)
    var o2 = RiskOrder(String("C-1"), 1, String("AAPL"), 0, 10000, 10, 2000)
    assert_equal(eng.check(o2).code, REASON_DUPLICATE_ORDER)
    # After the 60-second window the same id is usable again.
    var o3 = RiskOrder(String("C-1"), 1, String("AAPL"), 0, 10000, 10, 61_000_001_000)
    assert_true(eng.check(o3).approved)
    var o4 = RiskOrder(String("C-2"), 1, String("AAPL"), 0, 10000, 10, 3000)
    assert_true(eng.check(o4).approved)
    print("duplicate ok: same id inside the window rejected, later allowed")


def test_rate_limit() raises:
    var eng = fresh_engine()
    eng.set_max_orders_per_sec(3)
    var base = 1_000_000_000
    var i = 0
    while i < 3:
        var o = RiskOrder(
            String("R-") + String(i), 1, String("AAPL"), 0, 10000, 1, base + i
        )
        assert_true(eng.check(o).approved)
        i += 1
    var o4 = RiskOrder(String("R-3"), 1, String("AAPL"), 0, 10000, 1, base + 3)
    assert_equal(eng.check(o4).code, REASON_RATE_LIMIT)
    # One second later the oldest entry slides out of the window.
    var o5 = RiskOrder(String("R-4"), 1, String("AAPL"), 0, 10000, 1, base + 1_000_000_001)
    assert_true(eng.check(o5).approved)
    print("rate limit ok: 3 per second, 4th rejected, window slides")


def test_daily_loss() raises:
    var eng = fresh_engine()
    eng.set_max_daily_loss(5000)
    eng.apply_fill(1, String("AAPL"), 0, 10000, 10)
    eng.set_ref_price(String("AAPL"), 9000)
    # PnL = 10*9000 - 100000 = -10000, worse than -5000.
    assert_equal(eng.account_pnl(1), -10000)
    var o1 = RiskOrder(String("C-1"), 1, String("AAPL"), 0, 9000, 1, 1000)
    assert_equal(eng.check(o1).code, REASON_DAILY_LOSS)
    eng.set_ref_price(String("AAPL"), 9900)
    # PnL = 10*9900 - 100000 = -1000, inside the limit.
    assert_equal(eng.account_pnl(1), -1000)
    var o2 = RiskOrder(String("C-2"), 1, String("AAPL"), 0, 9900, 1, 2000)
    assert_true(eng.check(o2).approved)
    print("daily loss ok: losing account blocked, recovering account passes")


def test_pnl_formula() raises:
    var eng = fresh_engine()
    eng.apply_fill(1, String("AAPL"), 0, 10100, 5)
    assert_equal(eng.position_qty(1, String("AAPL")), 5)
    assert_equal(eng.position_cost(1, String("AAPL")), 50500)
    eng.set_ref_price(String("AAPL"), 10200)
    assert_equal(eng.position_pnl(1, String("AAPL")), 500)
    eng.apply_fill(1, String("AAPL"), 1, 10200, 3)
    assert_equal(eng.position_qty(1, String("AAPL")), 2)
    assert_equal(eng.position_cost(1, String("AAPL")), 19900)
    assert_equal(eng.position_pnl(1, String("AAPL")), 500)
    eng.apply_fill(1, String("AAPL"), 1, 10300, 2)
    assert_equal(eng.position_qty(1, String("AAPL")), 0)
    assert_equal(eng.position_cost(1, String("AAPL")), -700)
    # Flat: PnL = 0*ref - (-700) = 700 realised. Bought 50500, sold
    # 30600 + 20600 = 51200, profit 700. The formula agrees.
    assert_equal(eng.position_pnl(1, String("AAPL")), 700)
    print("pnl formula ok: unrealised and realised agree with hand math")


def test_no_ref_skips_band() raises:
    var eng = fresh_engine()
    eng.allow_symbol(String("MSFT"))
    # No reference price for MSFT: the band check is skipped rather than
    # guessed, so a far-from-anything price still passes the other checks.
    var o = RiskOrder(String("C-1"), 1, String("MSFT"), 0, 500000, 1, 1000)
    assert_true(eng.check(o).approved)
    print("no-ref ok: band skipped when there is no reference price")


def test_cancel_throttle() raises:
    var eng = fresh_engine()
    eng.set_max_cancels_per_sec(3)
    var base = 1_000_000_000
    var i = 0
    while i < 3:
        var d = eng.check_cancel(String("X-") + String(i), base + i)
        assert_true(d.approved)
        i += 1
    var d4 = eng.check_cancel(String("X-3"), base + 3)
    assert_true(not d4.approved)
    assert_equal(d4.code, REASON_CANCEL_THROTTLE)
    # One second later the oldest cancel slides out of the window.
    var d5 = eng.check_cancel(String("X-4"), base + 1_000_000_001)
    assert_true(d5.approved)
    print("cancel throttle ok: 3 per second, 4th rejected, window slides")


def test_audit_log() raises:
    var eng = fresh_engine()
    var o1 = RiskOrder(String("A-1"), 1, String("AAPL"), 0, 10000, 10, 1000)
    var _d1 = eng.check(o1)
    var o2 = RiskOrder(String("A-2"), 1, String("AAPL"), 0, 10501, 10, 2000)
    var _d2 = eng.check(o2)
    var _d3 = eng.check_cancel(String("A-3"), 3000)
    assert_equal(len(eng.audit_codes), 3)
    assert_equal(eng.audit_codes[0], REASON_OK)
    assert_equal(eng.audit_codes[1], REASON_PRICE_BAND)
    assert_equal(eng.audit_codes[2], REASON_OK)
    assert_equal(eng.audit_ts[0], 1000)
    assert_equal(eng.audit_ts[1], 2000)
    assert_equal(eng.audit_ts[2], 3000)
    assert_equal(eng.audit_oids[0], String("A-1"))
    assert_equal(eng.audit_oids[1], String("A-2"))
    assert_equal(eng.audit_oids[2], String("A-3"))
    print("audit log ok: every decision recorded with code, time, and id")


def test_reject_breakdown() raises:
    var eng = fresh_engine()
    eng.set_kill_switch(True)
    var o1 = RiskOrder(String("C-1"), 1, String("AAPL"), 0, 10000, 10, 1000)
    assert_equal(eng.check(o1).code, REASON_KILL_SWITCH)
    eng.set_kill_switch(False)
    var o2 = RiskOrder(String("C-2"), 1, String("AAPL"), 0, 10501, 10, 2000)
    assert_equal(eng.check(o2).code, REASON_PRICE_BAND)
    var o3 = RiskOrder(String("C-3"), 1, String("AAPL"), 0, 10000, 2000, 3000)
    assert_equal(eng.check(o3).code, REASON_QTY_TOO_LARGE)
    var o4 = RiskOrder(String("C-4"), 1, String("AAPL"), 0, 10000, 10, 4000)
    assert_true(eng.check(o4).approved)
    assert_equal(eng.n_checked, 4)
    assert_equal(eng.n_approved, 1)
    assert_equal(eng.n_rejected, 3)
    assert_equal(eng.reject_counts[REASON_KILL_SWITCH], 1)
    assert_equal(eng.reject_counts[REASON_PRICE_BAND], 1)
    assert_equal(eng.reject_counts[REASON_QTY_TOO_LARGE], 1)
    assert_equal(eng.reject_counts[REASON_OK], 0)
    print("breakdown ok: per-reason counts are exact")
    print("  " + eng.summary())
    print("  " + eng.reject_breakdown())


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def demo() raises:
    print("=== HFT pre-trade risk demo (simulated data, no real market) ===")
    var eng = fresh_engine()
    print(eng.limits.describe())
    var orders = List[RiskOrder]()
    orders.append(RiskOrder(String("D-1"), 1, String("AAPL"), 0, 10000, 100, 1000))
    orders.append(RiskOrder(String("D-2"), 1, String("AAPL"), 0, 12000, 100, 2000))
    orders.append(RiskOrder(String("D-3"), 1, String("AAPL"), 0, 10000, 5000, 3000))
    orders.append(RiskOrder(String("D-4"), 1, String("MSFT"), 0, 10000, 10, 4000))
    orders.append(RiskOrder(String("D-1"), 1, String("AAPL"), 0, 10000, 100, 5000))
    orders.append(RiskOrder(String("D-5"), 1, String("AAPL"), 1, 10000, 40, 6000))
    var i = 0
    while i < len(orders):
        var d = eng.check(orders[i])
        print("  ", orders[i].describe(), "->", d.describe())
        i += 1
    eng.apply_fill(1, String("AAPL"), 0, 10000, 100)
    print("  after fill: AAPL position", eng.position_qty(1, String("AAPL")))
    print(eng.summary())
    print(eng.reject_breakdown())

    demo()
    print("=== pre-trade risk tests ===")

--- SOURCE: mojo_samples/transformer_attention.mojo ---
# transformer_attention.mojo
#
# Component 1 of the START project's Mojo transformer samples.
#
# Scaled dot-product attention (SDPA), the heart of the transformer.
#
# What this file demonstrates:
#   * `matmul_atb`: C = A @ B^T, SIMD-vectorised over the shared
#     dimension, which is how attention scores Q @ K^T are computed
#     without materialising a transposed copy of K.
#   * Row-wise softmax with max-subtraction, the numerically stable
#     form: exp(x - max) can never overflow, and rows always sum to 1.
#   * Causal masking: future positions are set to a large negative
#     value before the softmax, so they receive (numerically) zero
#     weight -- the property that makes a decoder autoregressive.
#   * `sdpa`: the full pipeline -- scores, 1/sqrt(d_k) scaling,
#     optional causal mask, softmax, weighted sum over V -- plus a
#     completely scalar `sdpa_naive` reference that the fast version
#     is checked against on several shapes.
#   * Structural checks: every output row is a convex combination of
#     the V rows, and with a causal mask row i provably ignores every
#     V row after i.
#
# All code here is original, written for this project. It uses only
# the Mojo 1.2.0 standard library (`std.math`, `std.testing`); no
# Python interop, no external packages. The `Matrix` / `Rng` helpers
# are the same small self-contained core used across this sample
# series, repeated in each file so every file runs on its own.
#
# Run with:
#   cd ~/workspace/mojo-work && pixi run mojo \
#       ~/workspace/ai-starter/data/code/mojo_samples/transformer_attention.mojo
# Exit code 0 means every check and every unit test passed.

from std.math import sqrt, log, cos, exp
from std.testing import assert_equal, assert_almost_equal, TestSuite
from std.time import perf_counter_ns


def check(cond: Bool, msg: String) raises:
    """Fail loudly when cond is false.

    NOTE: plain `assert` is a no-op in this Mojo 1.2.0 build under
    the default `mojo run` flags, so every verification in this file
    goes through `check`, which raises a real Error. That is what
    makes "exit code 0 means everything passed" actually true.
    """
    if not cond:
        raise Error(msg)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Deterministic pseudo-random numbers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# A tiny xorshift64* generator. We roll our own instead of reaching for
# `std.random` so that every run in this file is bit-for-bit reproducible
# from an explicit seed, which is what makes the correctness checks below
# meaningful: the same matrices are generated every time this program runs.
# Box-Muller turns the uniform stream into standard-normal samples, the
# usual scale for initialising transformer weights.

struct Rng:
    """Xorshift64* generator with uniform and standard-normal output."""

    var state: UInt64

    def __init__(out self, seed: UInt64):
        # Zero is a fixed point of xorshift, so force a non-zero state.
        self.state = seed | UInt64(1)

    def next_u64(mut self) -> UInt64:
        var x = self.state
        x ^= x >> 12
        x ^= x << 25
        x ^= x >> 27
        self.state = x
        return x * UInt64(0x2545F4914F6CDD1D)

    def uniform(mut self) -> Float32:
        """Uniform sample in [0, 1). Uses the top 53 bits."""
        var bits = self.next_u64() >> 11
        return Float32(bits) * Float32(1.0 / 9007199254740992.0)

    def uniform_range(mut self, lo: Float32, hi: Float32) -> Float32:
        return lo + (hi - lo) * self.uniform()

    def normal(mut self) -> Float32:
        """Standard-normal sample via the Box-Muller transform."""
        var u1 = self.uniform()
        if u1 < Float32(1e-7):
            u1 = Float32(1e-7)
        var u2 = self.uniform()
        var radius = sqrt(Float32(-2.0) * log(u1))
        var angle = Float32(6.2831855) * u2
        return radius * cos(angle)

    def normal_scaled(mut self, scale: Float32) -> Float32:
        return scale * self.normal()


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Matrix: a row-major, List-backed Float32 matrix
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

struct Matrix:
    """Row-major dense matrix of Float32.

    Element (r, c) lives at `data[r * cols + c]`. Keeping one flat
    list (instead of a list of rows) is what lets the SIMD kernels
    below load contiguous runs of a row with a single vector build.
    """

    var rows: Int
    var cols: Int
    var data: List[Float32]

    def __init__(out self, rows: Int, cols: Int):
        self.rows = rows
        self.cols = cols
        self.data = List[Float32](length=rows * cols, fill=0.0)

    @staticmethod
    def zeros(rows: Int, cols: Int) -> Matrix:
        return Matrix(rows, cols)

    @staticmethod
    def identity(n: Int) -> Matrix:
        var m = Matrix(n, n)
        for i in range(n):
            m.set(i, i, 1.0)
        return m^

    @staticmethod
    def randn(rows: Int, cols: Int, seed: UInt64, scale: Float32) -> Matrix:
        var m = Matrix(rows, cols)
        var rng = Rng(seed)
        for i in range(rows * cols):
            m.data[i] = rng.normal_scaled(scale)
        return m^

    @staticmethod
    def uniform(rows: Int, cols: Int, seed: UInt64, lo: Float32, hi: Float32) -> Matrix:
        var m = Matrix(rows, cols)
        var rng = Rng(seed)
        for i in range(rows * cols):
            m.data[i] = rng.uniform_range(lo, hi)
        return m^

    @staticmethod
    def arange(rows: Int, cols: Int, start: Float32, step: Float32) -> Matrix:
        """Deterministic matrix with values start, start+step, ..."""
        var m = Matrix(rows, cols)
        var v = start
        for i in range(rows * cols):
            m.data[i] = v
            v += step
        return m^

    def get(self, r: Int, c: Int) -> Float32:
        return self.data[r * self.cols + c]

    def set(mut self, r: Int, c: Int, v: Float32):
        self.data[r * self.cols + c] = v

    def shape_str(self) -> String:
        return String(self.rows) + "x" + String(self.cols)

    def fill(mut self, v: Float32):
        for i in range(len(self.data)):
            self.data[i] = v

    def clone(self) -> Matrix:
        var m = Matrix(self.rows, self.cols)
        for i in range(len(self.data)):
            m.data[i] = self.data[i]
        return m^

    def transpose(self) -> Matrix:
        var t = Matrix(self.cols, self.rows)
        for r in range(self.rows):
            for c in range(self.cols):
                t.set(c, r, self.get(r, c))
        return t^

    def frobenius_norm(self) -> Float32:
        var acc = Float32(0.0)
        for i in range(len(self.data)):
            acc += self.data[i] * self.data[i]
        return sqrt(acc)

    def max_abs(self) -> Float32:
        var m = Float32(0.0)
        for i in range(len(self.data)):
            var a = self.data[i]
            if a < 0.0:
                a = -a
            if a > m:
                m = a
        return m

    def max_abs_diff(self, other: Matrix) raises -> Float32:
        """Largest |self - other| over all elements. Shapes must match."""
        check(
            self.rows == other.rows and self.cols == other.cols,
            "max_abs_diff: shape mismatch "
            + self.shape_str()
            + " vs "
            + other.shape_str(),
        )
        var m = Float32(0.0)
        for i in range(len(self.data)):
            var d = self.data[i] - other.data[i]
            if d < 0.0:
                d = -d
            if d > m:
                m = d
        return m

    def approx_equal(self, other: Matrix, tol: Float32) raises -> Bool:
        return self.max_abs_diff(other) <= tol

    def row(self, r: Int) -> List[Float32]:
        var result = List[Float32](length=self.cols, fill=0.0)
        for c in range(self.cols):
            result[c] = self.get(r, c)
        return result^

    def print_block(self, max_rows: Int, max_cols: Int):
        """Print the top-left corner, for eyeballing small results."""
        var nr = self.rows if self.rows < max_rows else max_rows
        var nc = self.cols if self.cols < max_cols else max_cols
        for r in range(nr):
            var line = String("")
            for c in range(nc):
                line += String(self.get(r, c)) + " "
            print(line)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Matmul kernels
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# All three compute C = A @ B with A (m x k) and B (k x n).
# The naive version is the ground truth; the other two must agree with
# it to within float tolerance on every shape we test.



def matmul_naive(a: Matrix, b: Matrix) raises -> Matrix:
    """Scalar reference: i-j-k triple loop, one multiply-add at a time.

    Deliberately the most obvious implementation possible -- this is
    the code the fast kernels are judged against, so it must be
    trivially auditable. The k loop is innermost, matching the
    textbook definition C[i,j] = sum_k A[i,k] * B[k,j].
    """
    check(a.cols == b.rows, "matmul_naive: inner dimensions must match")
    var c = Matrix(a.rows, b.cols)
    for i in range(a.rows):
        for j in range(b.cols):
            var acc = Float32(0.0)
            for k in range(a.cols):
                acc += a.get(i, k) * b.get(k, j)
            c.set(i, j, acc)
    return c^


def load_row_chunk(m: Matrix, r: Int, c0: Int) -> SIMD[DType.float32, 8]:
    """Build one 8-wide vector from row r, columns c0 .. c0+7."""
    return SIMD[DType.float32, 8](
        m.get(r, c0),
        m.get(r, c0 + 1),
        m.get(r, c0 + 2),
        m.get(r, c0 + 3),
        m.get(r, c0 + 4),
        m.get(r, c0 + 5),
        m.get(r, c0 + 6),
        m.get(r, c0 + 7),
    )


def store_row_chunk(mut m: Matrix, r: Int, c0: Int, v: SIMD[DType.float32, 8]):
    m.set(r, c0, v[0])
    m.set(r, c0 + 1, v[1])
    m.set(r, c0 + 2, v[2])
    m.set(r, c0 + 3, v[3])
    m.set(r, c0 + 4, v[4])
    m.set(r, c0 + 5, v[5])
    m.set(r, c0 + 6, v[6])
    m.set(r, c0 + 7, v[7])


def matmul_simd(a: Matrix, b: Matrix) raises -> Matrix:
    """SIMD matmul: vectorise over the column dimension.

    For a fixed row i of A and a fixed 8-wide column block of C, the
    accumulator is one SIMD register. Each step k broadcasts the
    scalar A[i,k] across a vector loaded from row k of B and adds the
    product. Columns that do not fill a whole vector fall back to the
    scalar loop, so odd shapes (e.g. 33x17) are handled exactly.
    """
    check(a.cols == b.rows, "matmul_simd: inner dimensions must match")
    var c = Matrix(a.rows, b.cols)
    for i in range(a.rows):
        var j = 0
        while j + 8 <= b.cols:
            var acc = SIMD[DType.float32, 8](0.0)
            for k in range(a.cols):
                var bv = load_row_chunk(b, k, j)
                acc += bv * a.get(i, k)
            store_row_chunk(c, i, j, acc)
            j += 8
        while j < b.cols:
            var s = Float32(0.0)
            for k in range(a.cols):
                s += a.get(i, k) * b.get(k, j)
            c.set(i, j, s)
            j += 1
    return c^


def matmul_tiled(a: Matrix, b: Matrix, bm: Int, bn: Int, bk: Int) raises -> Matrix:
    """Cache-blocked matmul on top of the SIMD inner kernel.

    The matrices are processed in (bm x bn) tiles of C, each built
    from (bk)-wide slabs of the shared k dimension. Blocking keeps
    the working set in cache: a tile of A is reused across every
    column tile, and a tile of B across every row tile. The inner
    work is the same SIMD-over-columns kernel as `matmul_simd`,
    accumulating into the C tile so partial sums stay in registers
    across k-slabs. Any tile size works; the min() guards handle
    shapes that are not multiples of the tile sizes.
    """
    check(a.cols == b.rows, "matmul_tiled: inner dimensions must match")
    check(bm > 0 and bn > 0 and bk > 0, "matmul_tiled: tile sizes must be positive")
    var m = a.rows
    var n = b.cols
    var kdim = a.cols
    var c = Matrix(m, n)
    var i0 = 0
    while i0 < m:
        var i_end = i0 + bm
        if i_end > m:
            i_end = m
        var j0 = 0
        while j0 < n:
            var j_end = j0 + bn
            if j_end > n:
                j_end = n
            var k0 = 0
            while k0 < kdim:
                var k_end = k0 + bk
                if k_end > kdim:
                    k_end = kdim
                for i in range(i0, i_end):
                    var j = j0
                    while j + 8 <= j_end:
                        var acc = load_row_chunk(c, i, j)
                        for kk in range(k0, k_end):
                            var bv = load_row_chunk(b, kk, j)
                            acc += bv * a.get(i, kk)
                        store_row_chunk(c, i, j, acc)
                        j += 8
                    while j < j_end:
                        var s = c.get(i, j)
                        for kk in range(k0, k_end):
                            s += a.get(i, kk) * b.get(kk, j)
                        c.set(i, j, s)
                        j += 1
                k0 += bk
            j0 += bn
        i0 += bm
    return c^


def matmul_add_bias(a: Matrix, b: Matrix, bias: List[Float32]) raises -> Matrix:
    """C = A @ B + bias, with one bias value per output column.

    The fused bias add is the form a transformer linear layer really
    uses (every projection is a matmul plus a bias). Checked against
    a naive matmul followed by an explicit bias loop.
    """
    check(a.cols == b.rows, "matmul_add_bias: inner dimensions must match")
    check(len(bias) == b.cols, "matmul_add_bias: bias length must equal output columns")
    var c = matmul_simd(a, b)
    for i in range(c.rows):
        for j in range(c.cols):
            c.set(i, j, c.get(i, j) + bias[j])
    return c^




# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Attention scores: C = A @ B^T, SIMD over the shared dimension
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# (A @ B^T)[i, j] = sum_k A[i,k] * B[j,k]. Both rows are contiguous in
# k, so one 8-wide vector from each row multiplies and accumulates,
# and `reduce_add` collapses the lanes at the end. This avoids ever
# materialising B^T -- exactly what attention wants for Q @ K^T.

def matmul_atb(a: Matrix, b: Matrix) raises -> Matrix:
    """C = A @ B^T, SIMD-vectorised over k. A is (m x k), B is (n x k)."""
    check(a.cols == b.cols, "matmul_atb: shared dimension must match")
    var c = Matrix(a.rows, b.rows)
    for i in range(a.rows):
        for j in range(b.rows):
            var acc = SIMD[DType.float32, 8](0.0)
            var k = 0
            while k + 8 <= a.cols:
                var av = load_row_chunk(a, i, k)
                var bv = load_row_chunk(b, j, k)
                acc += av * bv
                k += 8
            var s = acc.reduce_add()
            while k < a.cols:
                s += a.get(i, k) * b.get(j, k)
                k += 1
            c.set(i, j, s)
    return c^


def matmul_atb_naive(a: Matrix, b: Matrix) raises -> Matrix:
    """Scalar reference for C = A @ B^T."""
    check(a.cols == b.cols, "matmul_atb_naive: shared dimension must match")
    var c = Matrix(a.rows, b.rows)
    for i in range(a.rows):
        for j in range(b.rows):
            var s = Float32(0.0)
            for k in range(a.cols):
                s += a.get(i, k) * b.get(j, k)
            c.set(i, j, s)
    return c^


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Softmax
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The stable form subtracts the row maximum before exponentiating:
# softmax(x)_i = exp(x_i - max(x)) / sum_j exp(x_j - max(x)).
# The largest exponent is then exp(0) = 1, so no overflow is possible
# even for inputs in the thousands, and the result is identical to
# the textbook formula in exact arithmetic.

def softmax_rows(m: Matrix) -> Matrix:
    """Row-wise softmax, SIMD over columns, max-subtracted."""
    var result = Matrix(m.rows, m.cols)
    for r in range(m.rows):
        var row_max = m.get(r, 0)
        for c in range(1, m.cols):
            var v = m.get(r, c)
            if v > row_max:
                row_max = v
        var total = SIMD[DType.float32, 8](0.0)
        var c = 0
        while c + 8 <= m.cols:
            var chunk = load_row_chunk(m, r, c)
            total += exp(chunk - row_max)
            c += 8
        var denom = total.reduce_add()
        while c < m.cols:
            denom += exp(m.get(r, c) - row_max)
            c += 1
        c = 0
        while c + 8 <= m.cols:
            var chunk = load_row_chunk(m, r, c)
            var probs = exp(chunk - row_max) / denom
            store_row_chunk(result, r, c, probs)
            c += 8
        while c < m.cols:
            result.set(r, c, exp(m.get(r, c) - row_max) / denom)
            c += 1
    return result^


def softmax_row_scalar(m: Matrix, r: Int) -> List[Float32]:
    """Scalar reference softmax for one row, for checking."""
    var row_max = m.get(r, 0)
    for c in range(1, m.cols):
        if m.get(r, c) > row_max:
            row_max = m.get(r, c)
    var result = List[Float32](length=m.cols, fill=0.0)
    var total = Float32(0.0)
    for c in range(m.cols):
        result[c] = exp(m.get(r, c) - row_max)
        total += result[c]
    for c in range(m.cols):
        result[c] = result[c] / total
    return result^


def row_sum(m: Matrix, r: Int) -> Float32:
    var s = Float32(0.0)
    for c in range(m.cols):
        s += m.get(r, c)
    return s


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Causal masking
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def apply_causal_mask(mut scores: Matrix):
    """Set scores[i, j] = -1e9 for j > i (future positions)."""
    for i in range(scores.rows):
        for j in range(i + 1, scores.cols):
            scores.set(i, j, Float32(-1e9))


def is_causal_masked(scores: Matrix) -> Bool:
    for i in range(scores.rows):
        for j in range(i + 1, scores.cols):
            if scores.get(i, j) > Float32(-1e8):
                return False
    return True


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Scaled dot-product attention
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def sdpa_naive(q: Matrix, k: Matrix, v: Matrix, causal: Bool) raises -> Matrix:
    """Fully scalar SDPA reference: scores, scale, mask, softmax, mix.

    Q, K, V are (seq x d), (seq x d), (seq x d_v). Returns (seq x d_v).
    Every step is a plain loop so the logic is auditable by inspection.
    """
    var seq = q.rows
    var d = q.cols
    var scale = Float32(1.0) / sqrt(Float32(d))
    var scores = Matrix(seq, seq)
    for i in range(seq):
        for j in range(seq):
            var acc = Float32(0.0)
            for t in range(d):
                acc += q.get(i, t) * k.get(j, t)
            scores.set(i, j, acc * scale)
    if causal:
        apply_causal_mask(scores)
    var probs = Matrix(seq, seq)
    for i in range(seq):
        var row = softmax_row_scalar(scores, i)
        for j in range(seq):
            probs.set(i, j, row[j])
    var result = Matrix(seq, v.cols)
    for i in range(seq):
        for j in range(v.cols):
            var acc = Float32(0.0)
            for t in range(seq):
                acc += probs.get(i, t) * v.get(t, j)
            result.set(i, j, acc)
    return result^


def sdpa(q: Matrix, k: Matrix, v: Matrix, causal: Bool) raises -> Matrix:
    """Fast SDPA: SIMD scores via A @ B^T, SIMD softmax, SIMD mix."""
    var d = q.cols
    var scale = Float32(1.0) / sqrt(Float32(d))
    var scores = matmul_atb(q, k)
    for i in range(scores.rows):
        for j in range(scores.cols):
            scores.set(i, j, scores.get(i, j) * scale)
    if causal:
        apply_causal_mask(scores)
    var probs = softmax_rows(scores)
    var result = matmul_simd(probs, v)
    return result^


def sdpa_scores(q: Matrix, k: Matrix, causal: Bool) raises -> Matrix:
    """Just the (scaled, optionally masked) score matrix, for inspection."""
    var scale = Float32(1.0) / sqrt(Float32(q.cols))
    var scores = matmul_atb(q, k)
    for i in range(scores.rows):
        for j in range(scores.cols):
            scores.set(i, j, scores.get(i, j) * scale)
    if causal:
        apply_causal_mask(scores)
    return scores^


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Checks
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def check_convex_combination(result: Matrix, v: Matrix, tol: Float32) raises:
    """Every output row is a weighted average of V rows, so each
    output element must lie between the min and max of its column
    of V (up to float tolerance). A softmax bug -- negative weights,
    weights not summing to 1 -- shows up here immediately."""
    for j in range(v.cols):
        var lo = v.get(0, j)
        var hi = v.get(0, j)
        for t in range(1, v.rows):
            var x = v.get(t, j)
            if x < lo:
                lo = x
            if x > hi:
                hi = x
        for i in range(result.rows):
            var y = result.get(i, j)
            check(
                y >= lo - tol and y <= hi + tol,
                "convex-combination check failed at ("
                + String(i)
                + ","
                + String(j)
                + "): "
                + String(y)
                + " outside ["
                + String(lo)
                + ", "
                + String(hi)
                + "]",
            )


def check_causal_independence(q: Matrix, k: Matrix, v: Matrix) raises:
    """With a causal mask, output row i must not depend on V rows
    after i. We prove it by recomputing with those rows scrambled
    and requiring row i to be bit-identical."""
    var base = sdpa(q, k, v, True)
    var v2 = v.clone()
    var rng = Rng(999)
    # Rows 2 and later are scrambled; row 1 must be unchanged.
    for t in range(2, v2.rows):
        for j in range(v2.cols):
            v2.set(t, j, rng.normal_scaled(5.0))
    var changed = sdpa(q, k, v2, True)
    for j in range(base.cols):
        check(
            base.get(1, j) == changed.get(1, j),
            "causal leak: row 1 changed when later V rows changed",
        )
    # And row 0 depends only on V row 0: it must equal V row 0
    # exactly (its only allowed source).
    for j in range(base.cols):
        check(
            base.get(0, j) == v.get(0, j),
            "causal row 0 is not exactly V row 0",
        )


def test_softmax_properties() raises:
    # Rows sum to 1, including for huge inputs where a naive
    # exp() would overflow to infinity.
    var big = Matrix(2, 4)
    big.set(0, 0, 1000.0)
    big.set(0, 1, 1001.0)
    big.set(0, 2, 999.0)
    big.set(0, 3, 1000.5)
    big.set(1, 0, -500.0)
    big.set(1, 1, -499.0)
    big.set(1, 2, -501.0)
    big.set(1, 3, -500.0)
    var p = softmax_rows(big)
    for r in range(2):
        assert_almost_equal(row_sum(p, r), Float32(1.0), atol=1e-6)
        for c in range(4):
            check(p.get(r, c) >= 0.0 and p.get(r, c) <= 1.0, "softmax probability outside [0, 1]")
            check(p.get(r, c) == p.get(r, c), "softmax produced NaN")
    # Softmax is shift-invariant: adding a constant to a row changes
    # nothing, and the largest logit gets the largest probability.
    var shifted = Matrix(2, 4)
    for r in range(2):
        for c in range(4):
            shifted.set(r, c, big.get(r, c) + Float32(12345.0))
    var p2 = softmax_rows(shifted)
    check(p.max_abs_diff(p2) <= Float32(1e-6), "softmax is not shift-invariant")
    check(p.get(0, 1) > p.get(0, 3), "softmax ordering wrong (1 vs 3)")
    check(p.get(0, 3) > p.get(0, 0), "softmax ordering wrong (3 vs 0)")
    # SIMD softmax matches the scalar reference on an odd width.
    var odd = Matrix.randn(3, 13, 42, 1.0)
    var fast = softmax_rows(odd)
    for r in range(3):
        var reference = softmax_row_scalar(odd, r)
        for c in range(13):
            assert_almost_equal(fast.get(r, c), reference[c], atol=1e-6)
    # One-hot limit: a single dominant logit takes ~all the mass.
    var peaked = Matrix(1, 5)
